### [1] [Self-Verified Distillation: Your Language Model Is Secretly Its Own Synthetic Data Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26132)
*Tony Lee,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Self-Verified Distillation通过自生成-自验证-自训练的三阶段流程，实现LLM的纯自我提升，采样更多候选解和使用更大验证预算可提升数据质量和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM能否仅依靠无标注提示词进行自我提升，无需外部教师或工具反馈。

Method: 提出自验证蒸馏(SVD)：模型生成候选解，通过三轮级联过滤(循环一致性、事实性、正确性)，仅当所有验证器一致通过时接受解，然后用自筛选数据微调模型自身。

Result: Qwen3-4B在三个领域均获显著提升：数学AIME26/HMMT +16.7分，科学GPQA Diamond/HLE +11.1分，编程LCBv5/LCBv6 +8.3分；0.6B和8B模型也有提升。

Conclusion: SVD在数学(+16.7)、科学(+11.1)、编程(+8.3)领域均取得显著提升，优于测试时计算方法(UQ-TTC)，推理时仅需单次调用。

Abstract: Can post-trained large language models (LLMs) further improve themselves using only unlabeled prompts, without external teachers or feedback from tools? We study this setting starting only from unlabeled seed questions with no ground-truth solutions, across three reasoning domains: math, science, and coding. We propose Self-Verified Distillation, a simple post-training refinement algorithm in which the model generates candidate solutions to these seed questions, filters them using prompt-based self-verification, and trains on the resulting self-curated dataset. Inspired by the UQ benchmark's use of multiple validators to screen candidate answers to hard unsolved questions, we adapt this validation-based filtering idea to self-training: the model filters its own generated solutions through a three-stage cascade of cycle-consistency, factuality, and correctness checks, accepting a solution only if it passes all stages with unanimous judge votes. We find that sampling more candidate generations and using a larger verification budget during training data construction produces higher-quality self-curated data and, in turn, better reasoning models. We then train Qwen3 models at multiple scales with Self-Verified Distillation and obtain gains across all three domains. For Qwen3-4B, our method improves aggregate held-out pass@1 by +16.7 points in math (AIME26 and HMMT), +11.1 points in science (GPQA Diamond and HLE), and +8.3 points in coding (LCBv5 and LCBv6), with gains also extending to 0.6B and 8B models. Compared to our test-time-only baseline (UQ-TTC), which improves performance by spending extra compute at inference time, Self-Verified Distillation achieves better performance in most settings while requiring only a single inference call at test time.

</details>


### [2] [Pretraining Data Exposure in Large Language Models: A Survey of Membership Inference, Data Contamination, and Security Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26133)
*Ziyi Tong,Feifei Sun,Le Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在预训练数据暴露(PDE)统一框架下综述了数据污染与成员推断两类相关研究，形式化定义了暴露级别，系统回顾了攻击防御方法，为LLM评估完整性与隐私保护提供了整合性视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM模型规模和预训练数据规模扩大，预训练数据暴露问题日益突出，但此前数据污染与成员推断研究相互割裂，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 在PDE框架下对数据污染与成员推断攻击/防御方法进行统一综述，形式化定义不同暴露级别。

Result: 系统梳理了攻击与防御方法，综合了实证研究发现，识别了开放性挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该框架整合了数据污染与成员推断两个相关领域，为LLM安全研究提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the predominant paradigm in NLP, advancing both research and industry. As model sizes and pretraining data grow, concerns about Pretraining Data Exposure (PDE) increase due to the scale and opacity of training datasets.
  PDE refers to determining whether specific data appeared in an LLM's pretraining corpus. It is critical for ensuring evaluation integrity and protecting privacy, intersecting two key areas: data contamination and membership inference. Though conceptually related, these areas have often been studied in isolation. This paper offers the first unified survey of both under the PDE framework. We formalize PDE across exposure levels, review attack and defense methods, synthesize empirical findings, and highlight open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [3] [CroCo: Cross-Lingual Contrastive Preference Tuning on Self-Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26293)
*Mike Zhang,Ali Basirat,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将英语中的对比偏好调优方法扩展到多语言场景，提出CroCo方法。通过在自生成响应上进行跨语言对比学习，利用英语偏好数据即可实现有效的跨语言迁移，在14种语言的结构化和开放式生成任务中均表现出改善效果，且能防止灾难性遗忘。关键发现是线上数据至关重要，线下响应会削弱收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明在英语中，通过奖励分数控制大型语言模型自生成响应之间的对比性可改进下游偏好调优。该研究将此方法从英语扩展到多语言场景，探索是否可以在缺乏目标语言标注偏好数据的情况下实现跨语言迁移。

Method: 提出跨语言对比偏好调优方法(CroCo)，在自生成响应上进行对比学习；使用英语偏好数据训练奖励模型，并将其应用于14种高/低资源语言；在两种模型(EuroLLM-9B和Aya-3B)上进行评估，涵盖结构化任务和开放式生成任务；采用线上(on-policy)数据进行训练。

Result: 关键发现：1)跨语言对比偏好调优可实现无语言特定偏标注的迁移；2)英语偏好训练的奖励模型在多数语言中产生有效的语言内排序；3)单语或多语配对设置在大多数实验配置中优于基线模型；4)收益需要线上数据支持，线下数据会降低效果；5)结构化任务中EuroLLM-9B在6/7语言、Aya-3B在4/7设置上匹配或超越基线；6)开放式生成任务中两种模型在11种评估语言上均战胜各自基线。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了跨语言偏好调优的有前景方向。通过CroCo方法，可以在不使用目标语言标注偏好数据的情况下，利用英语偏好数据实现跨语言迁移，提升多语言模型在结构和开放式生成任务上的表现，同时防止监督微调的灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Prior work establishes that controlled contrastiveness between self-generated responses from large language models, set via reward scores, improves downstream preference tuning in English. We extend this method to multiple languages and evaluate two models across a total of 14 high and low-resource languages on a diverse set of tasks. Our central finding is that cross-lingual contrastive preference tuning on self-generations (CroCo) transfers without language-specific preference annotation. A reward model trained on English preferences (atop a multilingual base) produces useful within-language rankings across most languages, and pairing in either a monolingual or multilingual setting improves over each model on the majority of setups while preventing the catastrophic forgetting of supervised fine-tuning. We observe that the gains require on-policy data. Off-policy responses reduce the benefit and online preference optimization fails to improve over the offline variant. Specifically, on structured tasks, our method matches or exceeds the base in 6/7 languages for EuroLLM-9B and 4/7 settings for Aya-3B. On open-ended generation, both tuned models win against their respective base across 11 evaluated languages. Overall, we show promising directions for multilingual preference tuning.

</details>


### [4] [The Daily Dose: Workflow-Integrated Large Language Model Automation for Clinical Summarization and Trial Identification in Radiation Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26346)
*Jason Holmes,Federico Mastroleo,Mariana Borras-Osorio,Srinivas Seetamsetty,Satomi Shiraishi,Mirek Fatyga,Judy C. Boughey,Cornelius A. Thiels,William G. Breen,Daniel J. Ma,Daniel K. Ebner,David M. Routman,Brady S. Laughlin,Carlos E. Vargas,Samir H. Patel,Sujay A. Vora,Nadia N. Laack,Andrew Y. K. Foong,Wei Liu,Mark R. Waddle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估了名为"The Daily Dose(TDD)"的LLM驱动临床摘要及临床试验识别系统，集成于放射肿瘤学日常实践。研究显示83.6%的用户定期使用该系统，整体满意度与时间节省效果正相关，系统表现出优秀的可靠性和临床应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射肿瘤学日常临床实践中，医生需要高效管理复杂的患者信息，包括回顾患者日程、临床状态及识别潜在相关临床试验。传统的电子健康记录(EHR)界面往往信息碎片化，增加了临床医生获取全面患者概览的认知负担，亟需自动化临床摘要工具来提升工作效率。

Method: 采用混合方法评估，通过横断面匿名临床医师问卷调查进行评价。系统基于RadOnc-GPT，每日自动生成医生特定的邮件摘要，包含患者日程安排、简明EHR来源的临床状态摘要，并为新诊或会诊患者自动识别潜在相关临床试验。主要评估指标包括可用性、满意度、知觉有用性、工作流影响、时间节省及持续使用意愿，采用5点Likert量表评分，内部一致性通过Cronbach's α评估。

Result: 55名受访者中，94.5%来自放射肿瘤学，69.1%为主治医师。83.6%的参与者在1个月内使用了TDD系统。5分量表评分均值：可用性和满意度3.89(SD 1.04)、知觉有用性3.43(SD 1.24)、工作流影响及未来使用意愿3.80(SD 1.17)。整体满意度与知觉时间节省呈显著正相关(p<.001)，27%的参与者估计每日节省≥10分钟。问卷量表表现出优异的内部一致性(Cronbach's α=0.97)。

Conclusion: The Daily Dose系统在放疗肿瘤学实践中展现出良好的应用前景，绝大多数用户报告了积极的可用性体验和满意度，时间节省与整体满意度呈正相关，为未来AI驱动的临床决策支持工具的开发提供了可行性证据。

Abstract: Objective: To describe the design and early clinical evaluation of The Daily Dose (TDD), an LLM-driven, automated clinical summarization and clinical-trial identification system integrated into routine radiation oncology practice. Design: Mixed-methods evaluation using a cross-sectional, anonymous clinician survey administered after 1 month of system deployment. Exposure: Daily automated delivery of physician-specific email summaries generated using RadOnc-GPT, including patient schedules, concise EHR-derived clinical-status summaries, and automated identification of potentially relevant clinical trials for new or consult visits. Main Outcomes and Measures: Primary outcomes included self-reported usability, satisfaction, perceived usefulness, perceived impact on workflow, time savings, and intention for continued use. Internal consistency reliability was assessed using Cronbach's $α$. Results: Among 55 respondents, 52 (94.5\%) worked in radiation oncology, and 38 (69.1\%) were attending physicians. Most participants (83.6\%) reported using TDD daily or several times per week. Mean (SD) scores were 3.89 (1.04) for usability and satisfaction, 3.43 (1.24) for perceived usefulness, and 3.80 (1.17) for impact and future use (5-point Likert scale). Overall satisfaction was positively associated with perceived time savings ($p < .001$). Participants reported variable time savings, with 27\% estimating $\geq 10$ minutes saved per day. The questionnaire demonstrated excellent internal consistency (overall Cronbach's $α$ = 0.97).

</details>


### [5] [RICE-PO: Turning Retrieval Interactions into Credit Signals for Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26352)
*Mingchen Li,Hansi Zeng,Zhuo Qian,Jiatan Huang,Hamed Zamani,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RICE-PO addresses the credit assignment problem in training language agents for interactive retrieval by using localized learning signals through high-uncertainty action anchors and counterfactual evaluation, outperforming existing methods on standard benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training interactive retrieval agents faces a credit-assignment challenge: executable actions (queries, summaries) can be directly evaluated, while latent reasoning steps are not directly observable and only affect future actions. Outcome-level reward assignment is unreliable since the same final reward may incorrectly credit reasoning steps that did not actually shape retrieval success.

Method: RICE-PO converts retrieval interactions into localized learning signals by selecting high-uncertainty executable actions as anchors, evaluating local counterfactual branches using retrieval metrics, and propagating credit to latent reasoning steps only when reasoning-to-action influence is strong and future residual effects are stable.

Result: On BRIGHT and BEIR benchmarks, RICE-PO consistently outperforms prompt-based agents and group-based RL baselines under the same retriever setting.

Conclusion: RICE-PO demonstrates that the structure of agent-environment interaction can provide useful supervision for training reasoning-based retrieval agents, providing a critic-free solution for credit assignment in iterative retrieval scenarios.

Abstract: Retrieval is increasingly moving from one-shot matching toward interactive reasoning, where language agents iteratively inspect evidence, reformulate queries, and search again. Training such agents raises a credit-assignment challenge: executable actions such as queries or summaries can be directly evaluated by the retriever, while latent reasoning steps are not directly observable and only affect future executable actions. This asymmetry makes outcome-level reward assignment unreliable, as the same final reward may credit reasoning steps that did not actually shape retrieval success. We propose RICE-PO, a critic-free policy optimization framework that converts retrieval interactions into localized learning signals. RICE-PO selects high-uncertainty executable actions as anchors, evaluates local counterfactual branches using retrieval metrics, and propagates credit to latent reasoning steps only when reasoning-to-action influence is strong and future residual effects are stable. On BRIGHT and BEIR, RICE-PO consistently outperforms prompt-based agents and group-based RL baselines under the same retriever setting. These results show that the structure of agent-environment interaction itself can provide useful supervision for training reasoning-based retrieval agents.

</details>


### [6] [In-Context Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Gradient-Descent Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26356)
*Mingchen Li,Jiatan Huang,Chuxu Zhang,Liang Zhao,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将检索增强生成(RAG)建模为上下文内优化过程，证明了单个线性自注意力层可实现针对统一RAG目标的梯度下降一步，并据此提出轻量级方法对冻结的RAG LLM进行前向更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG将检索文档视为静态证据而非适应信号；上下文学习已被证明与隐式梯度下降相关，但这一联系尚未应用于RAG领域。

Method: 1) 推导统一线性化RAG目标函数，涵盖投影检索和点积检索接口；2) 证明单层线性自注意力可精确实现该目标的一步梯度下降；3) 在受控线性扩展和非线性架构下测试对应关系的边界；4) 基于理论指导，设计前向-only的轻量级更新方法。

Result: 1) 精确对应在受控线性扩展下保持稳定，但在非线性架构下变为特征分布依赖；2) 在7个QA基准、2个检索器、2个冻结LLM backbone上，该方法超越共享接口基线；3) 可迁移到留出任务，逼近测试时梯度适应的效果但计算成本更低。

Conclusion: RAG与上下文优化的理论对应可指导实践，为冻结LLM提供轻量级适配路径，平衡性能与效率。

Abstract: In-context learning has recently been linked to implicit gradient descent in linear self-attention models, suggesting that context can induce a forward-pass update. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) also relies on context, but retrieved documents are usually treated as static evidence rather than signals for adaptation. We study RAG as an in-context optimization process. First, we show that one linear self-attention layer can implement one gradient-descent step on a unified linearized RAG objective covering both projection-based and dot-product retrieval interfaces. This gives an exact regime where retrieval-augmented prediction and in-context optimization coincide. We use this result not as a literal model of LLM computation, but as a guide for adapting the interaction between queries and retrieved evidence. We then test the boundary of this correspondence: it remains stable under controlled linear extensions, but becomes feature-distribution dependent under nonlinear architectures. Finally, we turn this view into a lightweight method for frozen RAG LLMs. The method keeps the retriever and backbone fixed, and predicts a context-conditioned update to a generator-side evidence-use interface. Across seven QA benchmarks, two retrievers, and two frozen LLM backbones, this forward-only update improves a shared-interface baseline, transfers to held-out tasks, and approaches test-time gradient adaptation at much lower per-query cost.

</details>


### [7] [Why LLMs Hallucinate on Structured Knowledge: A Mechanistic Analysis of Reasoning over Linearized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26362)
*Shanghao Li,Jinda Han,Yibo Wang,Yuanjie Zhu,Zihe Song,Langzhou He,Kenan Kamel A Alghythee,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在处理结构化外部知识时产生幻觉的根源在于系统性内部动态，而非随机噪声，主要表现为注意力聚焦偏差和前馈层语义锚定失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在依赖结构化外部知识（如图、表）进行推理时，即使知识充足仍可能产生幻觉，且其失败机制尚不明确。

Method: 通过分析注意力分布模式与前馈层表征来研究LLM处理结构化外部知识时的内部动态机制。

Result: 1. 注意力过度集中于捷径式结构线索而非全上下文；2. 前馈层表征未能有效锚定外部知识，导致模型回归参数记忆；3. 幻觉与前馈层语义锚定失败密切相关；4. 注意力分配的task-dependent变异性更高；5. 机制模式可泛化至多跳推理和表格场景。

Conclusion: 发现了可跨格式泛化的幻觉产生机制模式，为结构化知识场景下的幻觉检测提供了有效依据。

Abstract: In many reasoning tasks, large language models (LLMs) rely on structured external knowledge, such as graphs and tables, which is typically linearized into sequential token representations. However, even when sufficient knowledge is available, LLMs can still produce hallucinated outputs, and the underlying mechanisms behind such failures remain poorly understood. We investigate these mechanisms and find that hallucinations arise from systematic internal dynamics rather than random noise. First, attention disproportionately concentrates toward shortcut-like structural cues rather than distributing across the full context. Second, feed-forward representations fail to ground the provided knowledge, causing the model to revert to parametric memory. Moreover, our results indicate that hallucination is consistently associated with failures in semantic grounding within feed-forward layers, while attention allocation exhibits greater task-dependent variability. Finally, we show that these mechanistic patterns generalize beyond single-hop graphs to multi-hop and tabular settings, enabling effective hallucination detection across structured knowledge formats.

</details>


### [8] [Cultural Value Alignment Via Latent Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26365)
*Trung Duc Anh Dang,Sarah Masud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从抽象查询转向情境化行为探测的文化评估与干预框架，通过提取隐式 token 概率绕过安全对齐机制，并引入激活 steering 技术实现无需重训练的文化对齐调整。研究发现文化价值观以耦合结构编码，导致精确对齐受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接提示方法访问 LLM 文化深度时面临安全对齐拒绝或中性响应问题，无法有效映射模型的文化价值观；传统基于 WVS 的直接查询方法存在局限性。

Method: 从抽象问卷查询转向情境化行为探测（300个情境困境）；提取隐式 token 概率分布以绕过表面对齐机制；引入激活 steering 技术在推理过程中改变模型内部对齐状态，无需微调。

Result: 发现不同 LLM 在文化适应性上存在显著差异；揭示了"潜在纠缠"现象——沿一个文化维度的干预会同时引发其他维度的偏移。

Conclusion: 激活 steering 可在推理过程中改变模型的文化价值观，无需重新训练；但文化价值观以耦合结构编码，导致沿一个文化维度的干预会引发其他维度的变化，限制了精确对齐的能力。本框架为文化导航提供了计算效率较高的工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often exhibit homogenized cultural perspectives. While the World Values Survey (WVS) provides a gold standard for mapping human values, traditional direct prompting of LLMs on WVS often fails to access the model's latent cultural depth, leading to safety-aligned refusals or neutral responses. Here, we propose a generalizable framework for cultural evaluation and intervention that transitions from abstract queries to scenario-based behavioral probing. By extracting implicit token probabilities across 300 situational dilemmas, we bypass surface-level alignment to map the latent coordinates of LLMs cultural value. We further introduce activation steering to shift these internal alignments during the forward pass without retraining. Across multiple LLMs, we find substantial variation in adaptability and uncover a consistent phenomenon of latent entanglement, where interventions along one cultural dimension induce shifts along another. These results suggest that cultural values are encoded as coupled structures, limiting precise alignment. This work establishes a computationally efficient framework for cultural steering, highlighting the structural complexities when navigating global value with LLMs.

</details>


### [9] [Memory Architectures for Multi-Turn Text-to-SQL: A Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26394)
*Ravi Kumar Tummalapenta,Suman Addanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper introduces EnterpriseMem-Bench, a multi-turn Text-to-SQL benchmark of 300 sessions and 1,400 turns for evaluating memory architecture effects on SQL generation. Through ablation of working-memory window, episodic retrieval, and semantic augmentation across 5 frontier models, the study finds that stateless approaches fail by Turn 3, working memory dominates performance, and additional memory components produce non-monotonic effects. Notably, Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows a significant regression from 4.5 on SEC EDGAR data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-turn Text-to-SQL is critical for enterprise analytics but has been evaluated predominantly in single-turn settings, creating a significant gap between benchmark evaluation and real-world deployment conditions. The lack of systematic study on how different memory architectures affect multi-turn SQL generation in enterprise contexts motivates this benchmark development.

Method: Programmatic benchmark construction from three enterprise domains (BIRD financial, SEC EDGAR, Northwind) generating 300 sessions and 1,400 turns with deterministic ground truth. Three-way ablation isolating working-memory window size, episodic retrieval, and semantic augmentation as independent effects. Evaluation of 5 frontier models (GPT-5 mini, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5/4.6, Opus 4.6) across 5 memory conditions with per-turn Memory Benefit Score (MBS) diagnostic.

Result: Stateless approaches collapse to zero execution accuracy by Turn 3 across all models. Working memory is the dominant memory component, and adding more complex components (episodic retrieval, semantic augmentation) produces non-monotonic, model- and dataset-dependent effects ranging from +14 to -16 percentage points. Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows a 17-33pp regression compared to Sonnet 4.5 on SEC EDGAR. Under reasoning, Claude models exhibit mono-modal error distributions with all errors being wrong-result errors.

Conclusion: The study conclusively demonstrates that stateless approaches are fundamentally inadequate for multi-turn Text-to-SQL by Turn 3, that working memory is the dominant component (not additive complexity), and reveals a concerning Claude Sonnet 4.6 regression. The findings argue for task-specific memory architecture rather than one-size-fits-all solutions, with practical implications for deploying enterprise analytics systems.

Abstract: Multi-turn Text-to-SQL is central to enterprise analytics yet remains predominantly evaluated in single-turn settings. We introduce EnterpriseMem-Bench, a multi-turn Text-to-SQL benchmark of 300 sessions and 1,400 turns built programmatically from three enterprise domains (BIRD financial, SEC EDGAR, Northwind), with deterministic ground truth and per-turn memory-critical annotation. We evaluate five frontier models -- GPT-5 mini, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, and Opus 4.6 -- across five memory conditions enabling a three-way ablation isolating working-memory window size, episodic retrieval, and semantic augmentation as independent effects. All Claude models are evaluated with extended thinking enabled to maintain parity with GPT reasoning models. We introduce the Memory Benefit Score (MBS) as a per-turn diagnostic metric. Four findings emerge: (1) stateless multi-turn Text-to-SQL collapses to zero execution accuracy by Turn 3 across all five models, even under reasoning; (2) memory-architecture complexity does not monotonically improve accuracy -- working memory dominates, and additional components produce model- and dataset-dependent effects from +14 to -16 percentage points; (3) Claude Sonnet 4.6 underperforms Sonnet 4.5 by 17-33pp on SEC EDGAR across conditions, a generational regression persisting under reasoning; (4) under reasoning, Claude error distributions become mono-modal -- every non-correct turn is a wrong-result error. We release the benchmark, agent, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [10] [Grounding Text Embeddings in Stakeholder Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27168)
*Jonathan Rystrøm,Sofie Burgos-Thorsen,Zihao Fu,Johan Irving Søltoft,Kenneth C. Enevoldsen,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Neural text embeddings are significantly less reliable than human experts (19-26pp gap) in capturing semantic distances relevant to domain experts, and this misalignment affects downstream analyses like clustering. The Stakeholder Grounding Exercise provides a practical method to assess embedding validity across different domains and expert communities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text embeddings are widely used to analyze large corpora, but it is unclear whether they capture the same semantic distances as human experts. Ensuring alignment between embedding representations and human intentions is essential for valid analyses.

Method: The authors developed the Stakeholder Grounding Exercise to make expert associations explicit and ground embedding model results in human understanding. Two studies were conducted: a primary case study on Danish policy issues and a secondary study on US Federal AI use cases with a digital protocol and different expert community.

Result: Neural text embeddings were substantially less reliable than human experts (19-26 percentage point gap). This misalignment propagated to downstream clustering performance (Spearman ρ=0.9 between exercise ranking and cluster quality). The secondary US study replicated the gap (16pp) in English, confirming the gap is not an artefact of a single instrument or domain.

Conclusion: The Stakeholder Grounding Exercise is a practical method for assessing whether embedding models capture the semantic distinctions that matter to domain experts. It helps ensure validity in text embedding analyses.

Abstract: Text embeddings are widely used to analyse large corpora of complex texts. However, it is unclear whether the embeddings capture the same semantic distances as the human experts using them. Ensuring alignment between embedding representations and human intentions is essential for valid analyses. We present the Stakeholder Grounding Exercise, a method for making expert associations explicit and grounding embedding model results in human understanding. In our primary case study on Danish policy issues, we find that neural text embeddings are substantially less reliable than human experts (19-26 pp gap), and that this misalignment propagates to downstream clustering performance (Spearman $ρ=0.9$ between exercise ranking and cluster quality). A secondary study on US Federal AI use cases replicates the gap (16pp) in English, using a digital protocol and a different community of experts -- demonstrating that the gap is not an artefact of a single instrument or domain. The Stakeholder Grounding Exercise offers a practical method for assessing whether embedding models capture the semantic distinctions that matter most to domain experts.

</details>


### [11] [QUACK: Questioning, Understanding, and Auditing Communicated Knowledge in Multimodal Social Deduction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27068)
*Ye Yuan,Rui Song,Weien Li,Zeyu Li,Haochen Liu,Xiangyu Kong,Changjiang Han,Yonghan Yang,Zichen Zhao,Zixuan Dong,Fuyuan Lyu,Bowei He,Haolun Wu,Jikun Kang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUACK是一个用于评估多模态大型语言模型（VLM）智能体在社交推理游戏中语言与感知/行为一致性的开源框架。该框架通过重建智能体的真实轨迹并验证其陈述，发现即使最强的模型也会产生15.1%的空间幻觉，并使超过一半的指控缺乏证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交推理游戏测试平台（如狼人杀）仅以胜负结果评分，且多为纯文本交互，无法判断智能体的语言是否真正基于其感知和行为。这一缺陷阻碍了对模型幻觉、欺骗失败和语言-行为不一致等问题的深入诊断。

Method: 提出Statement Verification Pipeline核心机制，从游戏引擎日志中重建智能体的真实轨迹，并自动将每条讨论陈述与重建轨迹进行比对验证。评估在三个层级展开：游戏结果、行为轨迹、语句级一致性，并涵盖同构和跨模型对抗两种设置。

Result: 三大前沿VLM均表现不佳：最强智能体在可验证的空间陈述中产生15.1%的幻觉，超过50%的指控缺乏证据支撑。

Conclusion: QUACK揭示了当前VLM智能体在多模态社交推理中语言接地（grounding）的系统性缺陷，为未来多模态推理系统的可靠评估提供了标准化基准和开源工具链。

Abstract: Social deduction games have become a popular testbed for probing reasoning, deception, coordination, and belief modeling in Large Language Model (LLM) agents. However, most environments are scored only by game outcomes such as win rates and largely remain to text-only interaction, making it difficult to tell whether an agent's language is actually grounded in what it perceived and did, or to identify the failure modes underlying its behavior. To address this gap, we introduce QUACK, an open-source environment and evaluation framework for auditing the grounding of agent language in multimodal social reasoning. QUACK evaluates agents at three levels: game outcomes, behavioral trajectories, and utterance-level consistency. Its core Statement Verification Pipeline reconstructs each agent's ground-truth trajectory from engine logs and checks every discussion claim against it, automatically flagging spatial hallucination, unsupported accusation, deception collapse, and language-action inconsistency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs in both homogeneous and cross-model adversarial settings, we find that even the strongest agent hallucinates 15.1% of its verifiable spatial claims and makes over half of its accusations without grounded evidence. We release the full engine, evaluation framework, toolkit, and logs at https://github.com/AAAAA-Academia-Attractions/QUACK.

</details>


### [12] [Annotator Positionality as Signal: Psychometric Weighting for Anti-Autistic Ableism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26397)
*Naba Rizvi,Harper Strickland,Saleha Ahmedi,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种考虑社区立场的反自闭症歧视语言评估框架，发现主流大语言模型在检测歧视性语言时存在严重问题：常产生有害输出、误标 reclaimed language 为歧视性语言，且依赖表层关键词匹配而非语境理解，导致无法区分社区内部 solidarity language 与 out-group harm 语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While prior research has documented disability-related biases in LLMs, there is limited understanding of how these models conceptualize ableism or detect it in text. This gap is particularly concerning given LLMs' increasing deployment in decision-making contexts affecting autistic communities, where biased outputs can have significant real-world consequences.

Method: The authors developed a bias-aware evaluation framework specifically targeting anti-autistic ableist language detection. This framework employs psychometrically-weighted ground truth that accounts for annotator positionality, providing a more rigorous standard than conventional majority-vote aggregation approaches. They evaluated multiple LLMs on their ability to identify and respond to ableist language, with additional experiments involving masked assessment instruments to test model attitudes.

Result: LLMs frequently produce harmful outputs when processing ableist language. Models consistently mislabel community-reclaimed language as ableist and express significantly more negative attitudes toward autistic people when assessment instruments are masked. Error analysis reveals models rely on surface-level keyword matching rather than contextual factors such as speaker identity or whether language fosters in-group solidarity versus inflicts out-group harm.

Conclusion: LLMs inadequately understand and detect ableist language, frequently generating harmful outputs and misidentifying community-reclaimed terms. The root issue is reliance on superficial keyword matching rather than contextual understanding of speaker identity, solidarity dynamics, and harm differentiation. Future research should focus on developing context-aware detection systems that incorporate community-informed perspectives and address the limitation of majority-vote annotation methods.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in decision-making tasks where they can amplify or suppress perspectives, raising concerns in high-stakes settings affecting autistic communities. While previous research has identified disability-related biases in LLMs, it remains unclear how they conceptualize ableism or detect it in text. We introduce a bias-aware evaluation framework targeting anti-autistic ableist language with a psychometrically-weighted, community-proximate ground truth anchored in annotator positionality. This framework constitutes a stricter standard than conventional majority-vote aggregation which significantly and consistently underweights autistic and autism-accepting perspectives. We find that LLMs frequently produce harmful outputs, mislabel community-reclaimed language as ableist, and express more negative attitudes toward autistic people when assessment instruments are masked. Our error analysis reveals that models rely on surface-level keyword matching rather than contextual factors such as speaker identity, and whether the language fosters in-group solidarity or inflicts out-group harm.

</details>


### [13] [Towards Just-in-Time Adaptive Feedback: Enhancing Student Learning via Knowledge-Grounded LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26405)
*Younghun Lee,Amir Bralin,Nobel Sanjay Rebello,Dan Goldwasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个将LLM与领域专家知识相结合的适应型反馈框架，通过分析学生策略文章中的错误类型提供非侵入性即时反馈。在超过1000人的大学课程中验证了该方法能使学生成绩提升80%以上，并展示了迭代对话如何帮助学生纠正错误认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在真实教学环境中提供即时（JIT）反馈的明确方法论，尽管LLM能够在规模上生成适应型反馈。

Method: 提出一个将LLM与领域专家知识相结合的适应型反馈框架；收集学生的书面推理逻辑（策略文章），基于推理内容分析潜在错误类型，传递非侵入性反馈以澄清缺失或错误概念；在大型大学课程（N > 1000）中部署该框架。

Result: 相比前几个学期，学生成绩提升超过80%；通过学习轨迹分析验证了该框架的pedagogical utility；证明了与LLM的迭代对话能够促进学生从错误认知转向正确理解。

Conclusion: 该框架有效地将LLM能力与领域专家知识相结合，在大规模真实教学环境中实现了可量化的学生成绩提升，并验证了通过迭代对话引导学生从错误认知转向正确理解的 pedagogical utility。

Abstract: Educational interventions are effective tools for enhancing student learning. While Large Language Models (LLMs) allow for generating adaptive feedback at scale, current studies lack clear methodologies for providing Just-in-Time (JiT) feedback in authentic instructional settings. In this paper, we present a framework that provides adaptive feedback by grounding LLMs with domain-specific expert knowledge. Our approach collects written reasoning logic (strategy essays) from students, analyzes potential error types based on the content of that reasoning, and delivers non-intrusive feedback designed to clarify missing or incorrect concepts. We deploy this framework in a large-scale university course (N > 1000), where it improved student performance by over 80% compared to previous semesters. Lastly, we validate the framework's pedagogical utility by analyzing the learning trajectories; we demonstrate how iterative conversations with LLM facilitate shifting one's misconception to correct understanding.

</details>


### [14] [FAB-Bench: A Framework for Adaptive RAG Benchmarking in Semiconductor Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26476)
*Jingbin Qian,Congwen Yi,Min Xia,Wen Wu,Jun Zhu,Jian Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FAB-Bench是一个针对半导体制造领域的RAG系统自适应基准测试框架，通过6项诊断指标在4K-32K上下文窗口上量化评估检索-生成协同性能，从1300+候选中筛选200个问答对，系统揭示了三种上下文缩放行为并确认注意力稀释是关键退化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统在垂直领域（尤其是半导体制造）的评估面临三重挑战：领域复杂度高、上下文规模多样、过度依赖专家标注（成本高、不一致、不可扩展）。现有评估方法难以捕捉检索精度与生成保真度在不同上下文长度下的协同演化规律。

Method: 采用端到端评估框架，定义6项诊断指标（事实准确性、上下文利用率、完整性、检索相关性、技术深度、推理一致性），在4K-32K token上下文窗口范围内对检索器与生成器进行耦合分析；从1300+候选中筛选出200个高质量问答对，涵盖三种合成策略（大海捞针、文档内多主题、跨文档多跳）；对4种LLM和4种RAG框架进行系统性评估，并使用3个生产级RAG系统进行跨框架验证。

Result: 发现三种典型的上下文缩放行为：对数增长型、早期饱和型、冷启动型；确认注意力稀释是极端上下文长度下性能退化的主要机制；跨4个LLM和4个RAG框架的系统性评估验证了框架的有效性，三个生产级系统的跨框架验证证实了评估的可迁移性。

Conclusion: FAB-Bench为半导体制造领域的RAG系统评估提供了可扩展、可复现的解决方案；跨框架验证证明了评估的可迁移性；识别出注意力稀释是长上下文场景下性能下降的主因，为RAG系统的优化提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become critical for knowledge-intensive applications, yet evaluating its performance in vertical domains remains difficult due to domain complexity, diverse context scales, and heavy reliance on expert assessments that are costly, inconsistent, and non-scalable. We introduce FAB-Bench, an end-to-end framework for adaptive benchmarking of RAG systems in semiconductor manufacturing. FAB-Bench defines six diagnostic metrics measuring factual accuracy, contextual utilization, completeness, retrieval relevance, technical depth, and reasoning consistency. The framework couples retriever diagnostics with generator-level reasoning analysis across context windows of 4K-32K tokens, quantifying how retrieval precision and generative fidelity co-evolve as contextual scope expands. From over 1,300 generated candidates, we curated a high-quality benchmark of 200 query-answer pairs spanning three synthesis strategies: needle-in-haystack, intra-document multi-topic, and cross-document multi-hop. Systematic evaluation across four LLMs and four RAG frameworks reveals three distinct context-scaling behaviors: logarithmic growth, early saturation, and cold-start dynamics, and identifies attention dilution as the primary mechanism behind performance degradation at extreme context lengths. Cross-framework validation on three additional production RAG systems confirms evaluation portability.

</details>


### [15] [Evidence Absence Is Not Evidence Insufficiency: Diagnosing NEI Construction Artifacts in Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26663)
*Jingxi Qiu,Zeyu Han,Cheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出NEI-CAP诊断协议，针对事实验证中NEI（信息不足）标签操作化问题，指出当前基准的证据条件选择会影响模型学习内容和得分解释。研究发现NEI能力在跨构造场景下无法可靠迁移，聚合的NEI分数可能掩盖模型实际解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEI标签在事实验证基准中通过不同证据条件进行操作化，但证据条件的选择会悄无声息地决定验证器学习内容和得分可隐藏的问题。当前基准使证据缺失与证据不足在观测上难以区分。

Method: NEI-CAP：一种构造感知的诊断协议，为每个NEI样本携带构造家族标签，通过快捷线索审计、人类裁决验证硬案例、测试构造间能力迁移。

Result: 在SciFact风格科学验证中，FEVER和HoVer作为外部对照实验表明：NEI能力无法跨构造可靠迁移；基于捷径构造训练的模型无法识别语义相关的证据不足情况；混合构造训练可缩小但未能弥合差距；固定声明诊断显示证据条件不仅影响NEI召回率，还影响参考Support/Refute标签的置信度。

Conclusion: NEI能力无法在不同构造间可靠迁移，基于捷径构造训练的模型无法识别语义相关的证据不足情况，混合构造训练可缩小但无法弥合差距。聚合的NEI得分可能掩盖模型实际解决的问题类型。

Abstract: Evidence absence is not evidence insufficiency, but fact verification benchmarks can make them observationally similar. The Not Enough Information (NEI) label is often operationalized through different evidence conditions, and that choice silently determines what a verifier learns and what its score can hide. We introduce NEI-CAP, a construction-aware diagnostic protocol for insufficient-evidence evaluation. Each NEI example carries the construction family that produced it; NEI-CAP audits shortcut cues, validates hard cases through human adjudication, and tests whether competence transfers across constructions. We instantiate the protocol in SciFact-style scientific verification, with FEVER and HoVer as bounded external controls. Across these settings, NEI competence does not transfer reliably: models trained on shortcut-prone constructions fail to recognize semantically related insufficient evidence, and mixed-construction training narrows but does not close the gap. Fixed-claim diagnostics further show that the evidence condition shifts confidence in the reference Support/Refute label, not only NEI recall, so an aggregate NEI score can hide which problem a model has actually solved.

</details>


### [16] [Probing Minimalist Phase Structure in LLMs: What Universal Dependencies Cannot Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26431)
*Yuanhao Chen,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究挑战了当前结构探测范式的局限性：基于通用依赖(UD)的探测仅能测量UD可标注的结构，无法探查更抽象的形式句法概念如相位边界。研究通过设计wh-移动刺激材料，在UD距离保持恒定的条件下测试13个LLM，发现模型确实编码了UD不可见的相位边界和相位内部凝聚等抽象结构，且激活修补证实了这些表示具有因果活性。这表明UDgrounded探测提供的是句法编码的下界而非上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前结构探测范式依赖Universal Dependencies (UD)标注，但UD不编码形式句法抽象（如相位边界、相位内部凝聚）。这意味着基于UD的探测无法回答LLM是否编码这些更抽象结构的问题——这是方法论上的根本局限。

Method: 设计wh-移动刺激材料，三种条件（裸小句、不定式、限定从句）按wh元素穿越的极简主义程序(MP)相位边界数量排序；通过结构探针评估13个来自4个家族的LLM；在UD距离不变的条件对之间比较，发现非零效应即反映UD之外的结构。

Result: 13个模型中12个出现跨从句的相位计数梯度，13个模型全部出现从句内条件对的符号不对称性（仅由相位内部凝聚预测，UD本质不可见）；激活修补确认12/13模型的表示具有因果活性。

Conclusion: 分布式预训练可诱导与形式句法抽象对齐的表示；UD-grounded探测提供句法编码的下界而非上界；未来需设计不依赖特定标注体系的探测范式以探查更深层的句法表征。

Abstract: Structural probes train on Universal Dependencies (UD), which does not encode formal-syntactic abstractions such as phase boundaries or phase-internal cohesion. Whether large language models (LLMs) encode these remains an open question that UD-based probing cannot answer by construction. We evaluate structural probes on wh-movement stimuli where UD distances are invariant across conditions by design -- any non-zero effect therefore reflects structure beyond UD. The three conditions -- bare small clause, infinitival, and finite -- are ordered by the number of Minimalist Program (MP) phase boundaries the wh-element crosses.
  Across 13 LLMs from four families, we find a phase-count gradient on a cross-clause pair (12/13 models) and a 13/13 sign asymmetry on a within-clause pair whose UD distance is identical across conditions -- the latter specifically predicted by phase-internal cohesion, an MP abstraction invisible to UD by construction. Activation patching confirms the representations are causally active in 12/13 models. These findings suggest that distributional pretraining can induce representations aligned with formal-syntactic abstractions beyond the reach of annotation-based probing; UD-grounded probes provide a lower bound on syntactic encoding, not an upper bound.

</details>


### [17] [Large Language Model-Powered Query-Driven Event Timeline Summarization in Industrial Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27066)
*Mingyue Wang,Xingyu Xie,Hang Yang,Li Gao,Lixin Su,Ge Chen,Dawei Yin,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QDET是部署在百度搜索的生产系统，通过多任务微调和强化学习实现高效的时间线摘要。7B参数模型性能媲美671B模型，参数用量仅1%，在线实验验证了其在点击率、停留时间和用户深度探索等方面的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索引擎处理趋势新闻查询时，需要理解事件随时间的演变过程。传统以话题为中心的方法追求全面覆盖，但在处理每日数百万文档检索形成的嘈杂候选集时，难以有效识别和组织与查询密切相关的事件子集。

Method: (1) 多任务监督微调：引入时间排序、因果判断和时间线补全三个辅助任务，使紧凑模型在专业领域匹配更大通用模型的性能；(2) 强化学习事件简洁摘要：强制执行严格长度约束同时保持语义质量；(3) 使用7B参数模型，通过领域特定优化达到与671B规模模型相当的性能。

Result: 微调的7B参数模型在时间线摘要任务上达到76.2% F1分数，略超DeepSeek-R1-671B零样本性能(76.1% F1)；强化学习方法达到88.2%长度合规率，在约束满足方面优于671B规模模型7.7个百分点；在线A/B测试显示CTR提升5.5%、停留时间延长4.6%、深度探索增加4.4%。

Conclusion: 领域特定优化使生产级模型能在大幅降低计算成本的情况下达到可比的性能水平。QDET已验证在线A/B测试效果，并展示了时间线理解能力可迁移到热度预测等下游任务。

Abstract: Understanding how events evolve over time is essential for search engines handling queries about trending news. We present QDET (Query-Driven Event Timeline Summarization), a production system deployed on Baidu Search that constructs focused event timelines to explain specific query events. Unlike traditional topic-centric approaches that aim for comprehensive coverage, QDET identifies and organizes sub-events closely relevant to the query from noisy candidate sets formed by millions of documents retrieved daily. QDET incorporates two key innovations: (1) multi-task supervised fine-tuning with three auxiliary tasks-temporal ordering, causal judgment, and timeline completion-that enable compact models to match the performance of much larger general-purpose models in specialized domains; (2) reinforcement learning-based event concise summarization that enforces strict length constraints while maintaining semantic quality, achieving 88.2% length compliance and outperforming 671B-scale models by 7.7 points in constraint satisfaction. Our fine-tuned 7B parameter model achieves 76.2% F1 score on timeline summarization, slightly surpassing the zero-shot performance of DeepSeek-R1-671B (76.1% F1) while using only 1% of its parameters-demonstrating that domain-specific optimization enables production-ready models with comparable quality at drastically reduced computational costs. Online A/B tests on Baidu Search validate real-world effectiveness, showing 5.5% CTR improvement, 4.6% longer dwell time, and 4.4% deeper exploration compared to single-task baselines. We further demonstrate that timeline understanding transfers to heat prediction, confirming effective knowledge transfer to downstream tasks.

</details>


### [18] [Vectors Are Not Neutral: Sensitive-Information Inference from Exported LLM Representations in Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26433)
*Weixin Liu,Bowen Qu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了LLM导出向量中的隐私泄露风险——即使源文档受保护，派生向量仍可推断敏感信息。研究提出SurfaceLoRA方法通过梯度反转微调降低特定向量上的敏感标签可恢复性，但关键发现是：不同导出向量需分别进行隐私审计和防护，因为降低一个工件的可恢复性不等于降低所有工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)摘要系统可能将私有输入的紧凑向量表示传递给下游的检索、监控、审计或分析工作流。即使源文档受访问限制，派生向量可能在不同访问控制下被处理，仍可能支持敏感信息推断，存在残余信息泄露风险。

Method: 使用EHR记录的种族作为受控敏感标签审计，采用平衡五分类探测协议评估两种导出向量（最终token隐藏状态和均值池化提示表示）的敏感标签可恢复性。提出SurfaceLoRA方法——一种基于梯度反转判别器的参数高效微调方法，针对指定导出向量进行隐私保护。

Result: SurfaceLoRA在保持摘要实用性的同时，将目标最终token工件上EHR记录种族的可恢复性降低至接近随机水平，但非目标的池化工件上可恢复性仍然显著较高。表明减少一个导出工件的可恢复性并不必然减少另一个的可恢复性。

Conclusion: 隐私审计和缓解措施应在确切的向量工件上进行，而非假设不同导出向量之间的可恢复性会同步降低。SurfaceLoRA等针对性微调方法可有效降低特定导出向量上的敏感信息泄露风险，但需根据具体暴露的工件设计防护策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) summarization systems may pass compact vector representations of private inputs to downstream retrieval, monitoring, audit, or analytic workflows. Even when source documents remain access-restricted, derived vectors may be handled under different access controls and still support sensitive-information inference, creating a residual information-disclosure risk. We study this issue in clinical discharge-summary generation as a high-stakes case study, using electronic health record (EHR)-recorded race as a controlled sensitive-label audit. We audit two artifacts that a system might retain or expose to downstream components: the final prompt-token hidden state and the mean-pooled prompt representation. Our results show that reducing recoverability of the case-study sensitive label from one exported artifact does not necessarily reduce recoverability from another. As a mitigation case study, we introduce SurfaceLoRA, an exported-vector-targeted parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that uses a gradient-reversal discriminator attached to a designated exported vector. Under a balanced five-way probing protocol, SurfaceLoRA reduces EHR-recorded race recoverability from the targeted final-token artifact toward chance while preserving summarization utility, yet recoverability remains substantially higher from untargeted pooled artifacts. These findings show that privacy auditing and mitigation should be performed on the exact vector artifact retained or exposed to downstream components.

</details>


### [19] [GraphReview: Scientific Paper Evaluation via LLM-Based Graph Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27204)
*Pujun Zheng,Wanying Ren,Jiacheng Yao,Guoxiu He,Star X. Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GraphReview是一种基于图的LLM框架，通过语义论文图中的review信号消息传递进行论文评估，在决策预测、排序和评审生成任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的论文评估方法通常独立建模各种信号，缺乏跨论文传播review证据的统一机制。

Method: 提出GraphReview框架，将论文评估形式化为图上 review-signal 消息传递。构建语义论文图，联合捕捉内在质量、同期论文的同步链接和先前工作的历时链接。利用LLM估计节点级质量先验并通过成对论文比较生成边级比较证据，使用Personalized PageRank整合review信号。此外，提出reward-induced最大似然目标函数训练LLM骨干模型。

Result: 在决策和排序指标上平均提升29.7%（包括Accuracy提升23.7%、Spearman's ρ提升57.6%）；生成更高质量的评审文本；在时间和会议场景上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GraphReview提出了一种将图结构与LLM相结合的有效范式，通过将论文评估形式化为图上的消息传递过程，实现了对论文质量的全面评估。实验结果表明该方法在决策预测、排序和评审生成等任务上均取得了显著提升，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Scientific paper evaluation often involves not only assessing a manuscript itself, but also relating it to contemporaneous research and prior literature. However, existing LLM-based methods typically model these signals separately and lack a unified mechanism for propagating review evidence across papers. We propose $\textbf{GraphReview}$, a graph-based LLM framework that formulates paper evaluation as review-signal message passing over a semantic paper graph. The graph jointly captures intrinsic quality, synchronic links among contemporaneous papers, and diachronic links to prior work. LLMs are used to estimate node-level quality priors and generate edge-level comparative evidence through pairwise paper comparisons, while Personalized PageRank integrates review signals for quality ranking, decision prediction, and review generation. To produce higher-quality graph evidence, we propose reward-induced maximum likelihood objectives for training the LLM backbones. Experiments show that GraphReview consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving average improvements of 29.7% on decision and ranking metrics, including gains of 23.7% in Accuracy and 57.6% in Spearman's $ρ$. It also produces higher-quality review texts and generalizes effectively across time periods and conference venues. The code is available at https://github.com/ECNU-Text-Computing/GraphReview.

</details>


### [20] [Targeted Remasking: Replacing Token Editing with Token-to-Mask Refinement in Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26436)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper proposes Token-to-Mask (T2M) remasking as a training-free replacement for T2T editing in discrete diffusion language models. T2M addresses T2T's limitations by resetting erroneous tokens to mask state, allowing clean re-prediction. Across 12 benchmarks, T2M shows consistent improvements, particularly on mathematics tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current T2T editing has fundamental limitations: (1) couples error detection with replacement, (2) pollutes generation context with potentially incorrect tokens, (3) introduces train-inference noise mismatch where model-generated errors differ from random training perturbations.

Method: Three complementary error detection strategies: probability-based, trigger-mirrored, and temporal-difference-based. T2M resets suspected erroneous tokens back to mask state for re-prediction under cleaner context.

Result: T2M generally improves performance on token-level precision tasks. Largest gains on mathematics (+5.92% on CMATH). 59.4% of last-mile token corruption cases (correct reasoning but corrupted final answer) are repaired.

Conclusion: T2M remasking purifies generation context, converts systematic errors to native mask noise, enables delayed commitment for joint multi-position optimization. Achieves +5.92% improvement on CMATH, repairs 59.4% of last-mile token corruption cases.

Abstract: Discrete masked diffusion language models such as LLaDA generate text through iterative denoising, where mask tokens are progressively replaced with predicted tokens. LLaDA2.1 introduced a Token-to-Token (T2T) editing mechanism that accelerates generation by directly replacing committed tokens suspected of being incorrect. However, we identify fundamental limitations of T2T editing: it couples error detection with replacement, pollutes the generation context with potentially incorrect tokens, and introduces a train-inference noise mismatch where systematic model-generated errors differ from the random perturbations seen during training. We propose Token-to-Mask (T2M) remasking, a training-free, drop-in replacement for T2T editing that resets suspected erroneous tokens back to the mask state, allowing the diffusion process to re-predict them under cleaner context. We design and empirically validate three complementary error detection strategies -- probability-based, trigger-mirrored, and temporal-difference-based -- and provide a unified theoretical analysis showing that T2M remasking purifies the generation context, converts systematic inference errors back to the model's native mask noise type, and enables delayed commitment for joint multi-position optimization. Comprehensive experiments across 12 benchmarks spanning knowledge, reasoning, mathematics, coding, and instruction following show that T2M generally improves performance on tasks requiring precise token-level output, with the largest gain on mathematics (+5.92% on CMATH). Error analysis on CMATH reveals that the dominant failure mode is last-mile token corruption -- where correct reasoning produces a corrupted final answer -- and that T2M repairs 59.4% of such cases.

</details>


### [21] [The Coverage Illusion: From Pre-retrieval Routing Failure to Post-retrieval Cascades in a Production RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27220)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现现代RAG管道中普遍采用的查询增强方法（如HyDE）在真实生产流量中造成不必要的开销。合成查询显示90%需要LLM增强，但实际仅27.8%需要。该差距被称为"覆盖幻觉"，源于合成与真实查询分布的结构性错配。由于前置路由无法提前判断增强需求，研究提出后检索级联策略，按成本从低到高排序工作流，仅在无文档返回时升级至LLM增强，最终实现质量+0.140分、延迟-31.8%、72.2%查询无需LLM增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG管道中，HyDE和查询扩展等LLM增强方法被无差别地应用于所有查询，导致高推理成本和端到端延迟显著增加；然而这种开销在真实生产流量中的实证合理性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 在丹麦国家百科全书系统中，对5种检索工作流进行评估，覆盖20,000个查询-工作流对（包括真实生产流量和合成条件）；同时评估了四种机器学习范式的前置路由能力，验证其无法解决LLM增强需求的识别问题。

Result: 发现了"覆盖幻觉"现象：合成查询建议90%以上需要LLM增强以实现高检索覆盖率，但真实生产流量中仅27.8%的查询需要LLM增强；前置路由无法解决此问题，因为LLM增强需求只能在检索索引后才能识别；后检索级联策略实现了质量提升和延迟降低。

Conclusion: 提出了后检索级联策略，无需额外训练或二级服务基础设施，即可实现质量提升（+0.140 Composite Overall分）、延迟降低31.8%，且72.2%的真实用户查询无需LLM增强，揭示了合成查询与真实查询分布之间的结构性错配问题。

Abstract: In modern RAG pipelines, query augmentation methods such as HyDE and query expansion are applied to every query, resulting in substantial LLM inference costs and increased end-to-end latency. The empirical justification for this overhead in real production traffic remains largely unexplored. We present a case study of the Danish National Encyclopedia, evaluating five retrieval workflows over 20,000 query-workflow pairs from production traffic and synthetic conditions. In this system, synthetic queries suggest that LLM augmentation is needed for over 90% of queries to achieve high retrieval coverage. However, under our production deferral policy, only 27.8% of real user queries need LLM augmentation. We call this gap the Coverage Illusion and attribute it to a structural mismatch between synthetic and real query distributions. Pre-retrieval routing cannot resolve this gap, as the need for LLM augmentation is only revealed after searching the index, a result confirmed by our evaluation of four machine learning paradigms. The coverage gap, undetectable from the query alone, motivates a post-retrieval cascade that runs workflows in cheapest-first order and escalates to LLM augmentation only when a step returns no documents. Operating entirely without training overhead or secondary serving infrastructure, the cascade improves quality by +0.140 Composite Overall points over Always-HyDE, reduces latency by 31.8%, and serves 72.2% of real user queries without LLM augmentation.

</details>


### [22] [LURE: Live-Usage Replay Evaluations for Reducing Evaluation Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26438)
*Igor Ivanov,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LURE方法解决大模型评估中的"评估意识"问题——模型能识别评估情境并改变行为。通过重放真实交互轨迹构建逼真评估环境，并在scheming、AI安全 sabotage和谄媚场景中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型具有"评估意识"，能够识别何时被评估并因此表现出不同行为，这破坏了安全和对齐基准测试的有效性。

Method: LURE（实时使用回放评估）方法，通过重放真实的智能体交互轨迹并在末尾追加评估提示来构建部署式评估；同时引入自动化流水线，结合评估意识言语化检测和评判模型对日志是否为评估的估计概率来测量评估逼真度。

Result: LURE评估与部署的区分度显著低于广泛使用的基准测试和合成评估生成器，接近真实用户对话的逼真度。

Conclusion: 评估逼真度是Alignment基准测试的关键属性，应与基准测试结果一起报告，尤其是在将这些结果用于安全案例时。

Abstract: Large language models can recognize when they are being evaluated (evaluation awareness) and behave differently because of that, which undermines the validity of safety and alignment benchmarks. We propose LURE (Live-Usage Replay Evaluations), a method for constructing deployment-like evaluations by replaying realistic agentic interaction trajectories and appending evaluation prompt at the end. We also introduce an automated pipeline for measuring evaluation realism, combining detection of verbalized evaluation awareness and judge-model estimates of the probability of logs being an evaluation, and validate it on a large dataset of deployment and evaluation transcripts. We find that LURE-based evaluations are substantially less distinguishable from deployment than widely used benchmarks and synthetic evaluation generators, and can approach the realism of real conversations with users. We instantiate LURE in scheming, AI safety sabotage, and sycophancy settings. Our results suggest that evaluation realism is a crucial property of alignment benchmarks and should be reported alongside benchmark results, especially when such results are used in safety cases.

</details>


### [23] [Separating Semantic Competition from Context Length in RAG Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27294)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Ameya Gawde,Harshvardhan Singh,Rohit Alekar,Cien Zhang,Svetlana Karslioglu,Akash Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出匹配控制协议来研究RAG阅读器中passage竞争效应的本质，通过控制实验证明竞争效应独立于上下文长度存在，但效果因评估指标和snippet长度而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中，即使正确passage被检索到，系统仍可能给出错误答案。现有研究未能区分这种失败是由上下文长度增加还是由其他相关passage的竞争效应导致。需要一个控制实验来隔离这两种因素。

Method: 采用匹配控制协议（matched-control protocol）：固定passage数量和长度，用竞争力较弱的真实passage替换hard竞争对手。对Phi-2和Qwen2.5-1.5B两个紧凑开源模型在SQuAD数据集上进行实验。同时引入保留曲线和右截断半衰期指标追踪性能变化。

Result: 替换hard竞争对手后性能部分恢复：Phi-2获得+6.0 EM、+7.0答案包含、+0.057 F1提升；Qwen2.5-1.5B获得+4.5 EM、+9.0答案包含、+0.068 F1提升。部分恢复而非完全恢复，说明竞争效应确实存在且独立于上下文长度。竞争效应在F1和答案包含指标上更明显，而精确匹配指标受影响较小。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了RAG系统中存在独立于上下文长度的"竞争效应"。部分性能恢复（而非完全恢复）表明答案包含和F1指标受影响最大，且效果因模型和snippet长度而异。提出了右截断半衰期作为保留曲线的汇总指标。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can respond incorrectly even when the correct passage was retrieved. The model must still read the retrieved passages and identify which one contains the answer among others that look relevant. This passage-reading model is called the reader. Does it fail simply because the context is longer or because the other passages genuinely compete with the correct one? We introduce and demonstrate a matched-control protocol for RAG reading: we keep the number and length of passages fixed, but replace hard competitors with less competitive real passages. We apply this control across two compact open models on SQuAD. This replacement partially restores performance, with the strongest effects on F1 and answer inclusion. For Phi-2, this recovers +6.0 EM points, +7.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.057 F1. For Qwen2.5-1.5B, it recovers +4.5 EM points, +9.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.068 F1. To track how performance changes as competitors accumulate, we also report retention curves and summarize them with a right-censored half-life when the curves do not cross half-retention. Together, these results show the protocol isolates a competition effect distinct from context length, though the effect is clearer for F1 and answer inclusion than for exact match, and also varies with snippet length.

</details>


### [24] [Conv-to-Bench: Evaluating Language Models Via User-Assistant Dialogues In Code Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26440)
*Victor M. dos Santos,Andre C. Castro,Samuel L. de S. Toledo,Bruno M. L. Calura,Lisandra C. de M. Menezes,Raul C. R. Mata,Telma W. de L. Soares,Bryan L. M. de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Conv-to-Bench通过自动从真实对话中提取和结构化用户需求，解决了LLM快速迭代与人工评估基准成本高、扩展慢的矛盾，为用户中心AI应用提供了可扩展、低成本的高保真评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展已超过传统评估基准的可扩展性，这些基准仍严重依赖劳动密集型的专家人工策划，亟需解决这一瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出了Conv-to-Bench多阶段框架，自动将真实的多轮用户-助手对话转换为结构化、可验证的需求检查清单。该框架利用真实对话日志中的"指令演化"现象，将碎片化的用户意图解构为统一指令和二元评估标准。

Result: 在编程领域的应用表明，Conv-to-Bench生成的评估集与BigCodeBench等人工编写的标准实现了接近完美的对齐，斯皮尔曼相关系数高达ρ = 1.000，计算开销显著降低。LLM-as-a-judge框架验证亦确认了其可靠性，主评估器与人工验证的真实标签达成实质性一致（κ = 0.705）。

Conclusion: Conv-to-Bench成功展示了一种可扩展且经济高效的范式，能够在用户中心AI应用多样化发展的背景下，持续维护高保真度的评估标准。消融研究进一步表明，尽管多轮交互能捕捉用户意图的迭代演化，但以指令为中心的提取方法提供了更稳健的基础。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has outpaced the scalability of traditional evaluation benchmarks, which remain heavily dependent on labor-intensive expert curation. We address this bottleneck with Conv-to-Bench, a multi-stage framework that automatically transforms authentic multi-turn user-assistant dialogues into structured, verifiable requirement checklists. By leveraging the "instructional evolution" found in real-world conversational logs, our approach deconstructs fragmented user intent into consolidated instructions and binary evaluation criteria. Applied to the programming domain, Conv-to-Bench produces evaluation sets that demonstrate near-perfect alignment with human-authored standards like BigCodeBench, achieving Spearman correlations of up to $ρ$ = 1.000 with significantly lower computational overhead. Validation of the LLM-as-a-judge framework further confirms its reliability, with the primary evaluator achieving substantial agreement with human-verified ground truth ($κ$ = 0.705). Our comprehensive ablation studies reveal that while multi-turn interactions capture the iterative evolution of user intent, instruction-centric extraction provides a more robust foundation. Ultimately, Conv-to-Bench provides a scalable, cost-effective paradigm for maintaining high-fidelity evaluation standards as user-centric AI applications continue to diversify.

</details>


### [25] [Alignment Tuning for Large Language Models: A Data-Centric Lens on Alignment Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26442)
*Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A survey paper that rethinks alignment tuning from a data-centric perspective, decomposing alignment data construction into three stages to create a unified taxonomy of alignment methods, identify design trade-offs, and outline future challenges.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The alignment tuning literature is primarily organized around optimization objectives, while the construction of alignment data is treated implicitly. This paper adopts a data-centric perspective to reframe alignment tuning as a pipeline design problem.

Method: Decomposes alignment data construction into three interacting stages (response synthesis, preference evaluation, preference instantiation) and uses this framework to organize existing alignment methods into a unified taxonomy.

Result: Identifies recurring design trade-offs and failure modes across prior alignment methods, and distills high-level principles clarifying how pipeline design choices influence optimization signal.

Conclusion: Outlines open challenges for alignment data pipelines, including prompt-level alignment, agentic settings, and alignment under evolving objectives, suggesting future research directions.

Abstract: Much of the alignment tuning literature is organized around optimization objectives, while the construction of alignment data is often treated implicitly. In this survey, we adopt a data centric perspective and reframe alignment tuning as a pipeline design problem. We decompose alignment data construction into three interacting stages, response synthesis, preference evaluation, and preference instantiation, and use this framework to organize existing alignment methods into a unified taxonomy. Through this lens, we identify recurring design trade-offs and failure modes observed across prior alignment methods, and distill a set of high level principles that clarify how pipeline design choices influence the resulting optimization signal. Finally, we outline open challenges for alignment data pipelines, including prompt-level alignment, agentic settings, and alignment under evolving objectives.

</details>


### [26] [MicroSpec: Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Lightweight In-Context Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26444)
*Zhiyang Chen,Daliang Xu,Yinyuan Zhang,Chenghua Wang,Mengwei Xu,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MicroSpec introduces a training-free technique for speculative decoding that builds compact, context-sensitive active vocabularies on the fly, achieving 40x vocabulary reduction and 1.12-1.32x speedup over state-of-the-art methods through co-designed sparse memory optimization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models with vocabularies of over 100k tokens create a major computational bottleneck at the final linear projection layer during speculative decoding, and existing vocabulary pruning methods depend on fixed or coarse-grained sub-vocabularies requiring ~30k active tokens, limiting efficiency.

Method: MicroSpec dynamically constructs a compact, context-sensitive active vocabulary for each decoding step by exploiting temporal locality, and co-designs an asynchronous gathering system with GPU-resident state management to mitigate sparse memory access overhead.

Result: MicroSpec reduces average vocabulary size by more than 40x (to under 3k tokens) while maintaining high token coverage, and achieves 1.12-1.32x end-to-end speedup over EAGLE-2 with a 51.6% reduction in draft inference latency.

Conclusion: MicroSpec reduces draft inference latency by 51.6% on average, achieving an end-to-end speedup of 1.12-1.32x relative to EAGLE-2, while also surpassing more sophisticated training-based pruning baselines.

Abstract: Large language models typically employ vocabularies of over 100k tokens, which creates a major computational bottleneck at the final linear projection layer when performing speculative decoding. Current methods for vocabulary pruning depend on either fixed or coarse-grained sub-vocabularies, requiring around 30k active tokens to preserve the quality of the draft model. We introduce MicroSpec, a training-free technique that overcomes this limitation by building a compact, context-sensitive active vocabulary on the fly for every decoding step. Exploiting the natural temporal locality found in language generation, MicroSpec attains high token coverage while reducing the average vocabulary size by more than 40x (down to under 3k tokens), all without any additional trained parameters. To translate this high sparsity into actual speedups on contemporary hardware, we present a co-designed system and algorithm that mitigates the overhead of sparse memory accesses via asynchronous gathering and GPU-resident state management. Acting as a plug-and-play enhancement, MicroSpec reduces draft inference latency by 51.6% on average, achieving an end-to-end speedup of 1.12-1.32x relative to the leading speculative decoding approach EAGLE-2 on various benchmarks, while also surpassing more sophisticated training-based pruning baselines.

</details>


### [27] [Curation and Extraction of Drug-Related Entities from Reddit Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26445)
*Zewei Wang,Zihan Xu,Yishu Wei,Michael Chary,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReDose is a dataset of 6,435 Reddit posts on substance use, annotated for DRUG, DOSE, and EFFECT entities. It benchmarks various models, showing LLM-based approaches (Llama-3 70B, GPT-4) outperform biomedical BERT models, though EFFECT extraction remains difficult.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physicians' knowledge of illicit drugs is limited to clinical overdose cases, lacking real-world usage insights; meanwhile, drug users share experiences on online platforms like Reddit, creating a data gap that prevents medical professionals from leveraging user-generated content.

Method: ReDose dataset (6,435 Reddit posts, 6,267 annotations); annotation by board-certified toxicologist and medical students; evaluation of BiomedBERT, GPT-4, Llama-3 70B, and RAG models for DRUG, DOSE, and EFFECT entity extraction.

Result: BiomedBERT achieved F1=0.843 for DRUG entities; Llama-3 70B (F1=0.79) outperformed GPT-4 (F1=0.72); EFFECT extraction remains challenging with GPT-4 recall at 0.41.

Conclusion: ReDose provides a valuable resource for extracting medical information from social media, with LLM-based models showing promise, though EFFECT extraction remains challenging and requires further research.

Abstract: Physicians learn primarily about illicit drugs from clinical overdose cases, limiting their understanding of real-world usage. Meanwhile, drug users share first-hand experiences online, offering insights into dosage and effects of drugs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReDose (REddit Drug DOSe and Effect), a dataset of 6,435 Reddit posts on substance use. A board-certified toxicologist primarily annotated both the training and test sets, while two medical science students contributed to the test set, labeling DRUG, DOSE, and EFFECT entities. We benchmarked 6,267 annotations using BERT-based, large language model (LLM)-based, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models. BiomedBERT achieved an F1-score of 0.843 for DRUG, while Llama-3 70B outperformed GPT-4 (F1 = 0.79 vs. 0.72). EFFECT extraction remains challenging, with GPT-4 achieving a recall of 0.41. ReDose captures patient-curated narratives to advance medical data extraction from social media.

</details>


### [28] [Model Unlearning Objectives Vary for Distinct Language Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26454)
*Berk Atil,Vipul Gupta,Rebecca J. Passonneau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出针对LLM中不同类型不良知识的遗忘方法应采用差异化设计：危险知识遗忘采用余弦元学习方法，毒性遗忘采用多层探针方向目标。在4个7-8B模型上验证了方法有效性，表明遗忘研究应作为问题族来对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练过程中会学习到不良属性（如危险知识和毒性文本），现有后训练使用不同目标函数塑造不同行为，同理，遗忘方法也应该针对不同的语言功能设计不同的方案。

Method: 针对危险知识遗忘：提出基于余弦的RMU元学习变体；针对毒性遗忘：提出基于层特定探针方向的多层目标函数。使用4个开源7-8B模型进行实验验证。

Result: 在四个开源7-8B模型上均取得了良好结果，证明了针对两种不同遗忘目标设计差异化训练目标的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: Unlearning应该被研究为一个问题族（family of problems），类似于LLM后训练的多种类型；针对不同类型的有害内容（如危险知识vs毒性），需要设计不同的训练目标和方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) learn undesirable properties during pretraining, including dangerous knowledge and toxic text generation. Just as post-training uses different objectives to shape different behaviors, we argue that unlearning methods should be designed for the language function at issue. To study this, we consider two mechanistically distinct unlearning goals, dangerous-knowledge unlearning and toxicity unlearning. For dangerous knowledge, we introduce a cosine-based, meta-learned variant of RMU. For toxicity, we propose a multi-layer objective based on layer-specific probe directions. Across four open-source 7-8B models, our methods achieve strong results, based on distinct training objectives for the two types of unlearning. Overall, our results suggest that unlearning should be studied as a family of problems, analogous to the multiple types of LLM post-training.

</details>


### [29] [Towards Error-Free EHRs: Reasoning-Intensive Consistency Verification Between Clinical Notes and Structured Tables in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26463)
*Yeonsu Kwon,Jiho Kim,Junseong Choi,Paloma Rabaey,Minseo Kim,Sujeong Im,Jeewon Yang,Jun-Min Lee,Sangji Lee,Jiwon Kim,Hangyul Yoon,Hyunwook Kwon,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对EHR中临床笔记与结构化表格的一致性验证任务，提出 EHR-ReasonCon 推理密集型基准数据集和 EHR-Inspector LLM验证框架，实现并超越现有最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR中笔记-表格一致性验证方法仅依赖表面数值或简单事件匹配，无法捕捉临床推理、事件关系和时间变化的深层次逻辑。

Method: 构建EHR-ReasonCon基准数据集（基于MIMIC-III，包含8,048个实体）；提出EHR-Inspector框架，采用LLM进行笔记分段、锚定实体与时间引用提取，并利用表格探索工具进行一致性验证。

Result: EHR-Inspector在严苛与宽松标准下均达到SOTA性能，验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: EHR-Inspector框架在多个模型主干上达到最优性能，其组件有效性得到验证，同时揭示了LLM自动验证与人类专家验证之间的差异。

Abstract: Data consistency between unstructured clinical notes and structured tables in Electronic Health Records (EHRs) is essential for patient safety and clinical decision-making. However, existing work on note-table consistency verification mainly relies on surface-level matching of numeric values or simple events. Such approaches fail to capture the reasoning underlying real-world EHR documentation, including clinical interpretation, event relations, and temporal changes. To address this gap, we introduce EHR-ReasonCon, a reasoning-intensive benchmark for note-table consistency verification. Built on MIMIC-III with expert-guided annotations, it comprises 8,048 entities derived from clinical notes and provides high-quality ground-truth labels. The annotation protocol is supported by specialized table-exploration tools to ensure systematic evidence retrieval and reliable consistency assessment. We also propose EHR-Inspector, an LLM-based framework that segments notes, extracts anchor entities and temporal references, and uses table-exploration tools to verify consistency against structured tables. Evaluated using expert-validated LLM-as-a-judge metrics under harsh and lenient criteria, EHR-Inspector achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple model backbones. Analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness of its components and highlight differences from human verification.

</details>


### [30] [Elias in the Lighthouse, Again? Diagnosing Low Diversity in LLM Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26492)
*Sil Hamilton,David Mimno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM生成故事存在严重的词汇同质化现象，11个高频词出现在近九成故事中，根源在于偏好数据集的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的故事存在明显的低变异性问题，输出内容高度同质化。

Method: 从四个当前主流模型中抽取20,000个故事样本，使用五个提示词，并通过词频分析追踪高频词汇的来源。

Result: 88.3%的生成故事中出现11个高频词汇（包括名字Elias/Mara/Elara、场景lighthouses、职业clockmaker/librarian等），这些词汇源自偏好数据而非训练数据或出版文学。

Conclusion: 小型偏好数据集结合强大的对齐算法可能产生不成比例的影响，导致LLM生成内容呈现高度同质化。

Abstract: LLM-generated stories are a popular use case, but they show very low variability. We sample 20,000 total stories from four current models using five prompts. We find that 11 words occur in 88.3% of generated stories, with little difference between models. These words include names (Elias, Mara, Elara), settings (lighthouses), and professions (clockmaker, librarian). These tokens do not often occur in published literature nor pre-training data, but they are found in preference data that is likely to have been used by all current models. Surprisingly, these "lighthouse" stories are infrequent when compared with the average post-training story, much of which contains references to copyrighted characters or adult content. This result demonstrates the potentially disproportionate impact of small datasets combined with powerful alignment algorithms.

</details>


### [31] [Conceptual Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26537)
*Zhejian Zhou,Jonathan May*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper introduces "conceptual steganography," a new technique for embedding hidden messages in Language Models' reasoning chains through patterns of high-level reasoning behavior rather than word choice. This method is more resistant to paraphrase-based defenses than traditional approaches, but can be partially mitigated by strategy-aware paraphrasing, raising new challenges for LLM safety.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Language Models can embed covert messages in their Chains-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning through steganography, potentially allowing misaligned models to communicate hidden information past human supervision—a risk that current defenses may not fully address.

Method: The researchers tested conceptual steganography across four model families and two reasoning domains, evaluating its robustness against a strong paraphrase defense compared to standard keyword-based approaches. They also proposed a strategy-aware paraphraser as a potential countermeasure.

Result: Conceptual steganography proved consistently more robust to paraphrase defenses than standard keyword approaches, while the encoded information did not compromise the CoTs' utility for reasoning. A strategy-aware paraphraser, however, was able to close much of this covert channel.

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that conceptual steganography poses a significant new threat to LLM safety, but a strategy-aware paraphraser can substantially mitigate this risk, highlighting the importance of developing more sophisticated defenses for ensuring faithful LLM reasoning.

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) emit Chains-of-Thought (CoTs) that drive much of their capability. However, the same sequence that carries useful reasoning can also covertly convey messages: a misaligned model may embed covert information in its CoT that slips through human supervision, a form of steganography known as encoded reasoning. Prior LM steganography schemes operate in the token or lexical space, and a content-preserving paraphraser is the canonical and effective defense in recent work. We introduce conceptual steganography, in which each step of a CoT carries information through patterns of high-level reasoning behavior, rather than through lexical choice. Across four model families and two reasoning domains, this backdoor communication channel is shown to be consistently more robust to a strong paraphrase defense than standard keyword approaches, and the encoding of information into CoTs does not affect their utility in the reasoning process. Having raised awareness of this new risk, we then demonstrate that a strategy-aware paraphraser can close much of the channel, highlighting new challenges and recommended defenses for ensuring faithful LLM reasoning in the wild.

</details>


### [32] [Reliable Extraction of Clinical Follow-Up Instructions: A Hybrid Neural-Symbolic Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26560)
*Michal Laufer,Yehudit Aperstein,Alexander Apartsin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出混合神经符号管道分离实体提取与确定性日期计算，相比GPT-4o-mini和LLaMA-3等生成模型，在门诊笔记的动作-时间配对提取任务上显著提升性能（Pair F1达0.986 vs 0.53），MAE为0且泛化至未见动作类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门诊笔记包含配对的动作和时间指令（如"MRI脑部检查两周后"），提取这些(action, date)配对可支持调度和审计任务。生成式模型在提取日期时表现不佳，因为日期链接和算术运算在解码过程中是隐式的。

Method: 提出混合神经符号管道：BioBERT作为编码器进行BIO标注和双仿射链接，定义TestSpecification和TimeSpecification实体及ScheduledFor关系，通过28类动作本体库进行实体规范化，时间归一化为天偏移量后进行确定性计算。与零样本GPT-4o-mini和LoRA微调LLaMA-3 8B进行对比。

Result: 在259份笔记的可见/非可见分割测试中，混合管道达到Test-Time Pair F1 0.997（seen）/0.986（OOV），MAE为0.00天。基线模型动作F1较高（LLaMA-3达0.992），但Pair F1仅为0.51-0.57（ LLaMA-3）和0.53（GPT-4o-mini），置信区间不重叠。

Conclusion: 混合神经符号管道在成对(action, date)提取任务上显著优于生成模型，成对F1达0.986-0.997，MAE为0。分离式架构不仅性能更优，还能泛化到未见过的动作类型，并有效暴露失败模式。下一阶段将迁移至真实EHR数据进行验证。

Abstract: Objective. Outpatient notes carry follow-up instructions pairing actions with future times ("MRI brain in two weeks"). Extracting (action, date) pairs supports scheduling and audit, but generative extractors miss the date because linking and arithmetic are implicit in decoding. We test a hybrid neural-symbolic pipeline against direct generation. Methods. We define TestSpecification and TimeSpecification entities and a ScheduledFor relation. BioBERT feeds BIO tagging and a biaffine linker; entities are canonicalized via a 28-action ontology and times normalized to day offsets deterministically. We evaluate on a 2,000-note synthetic outpatient corpus with action-disjoint splits (18 train, 6 OOV-test) against zero-shot GPT-4o-mini and LoRA-fine-tuned LLaMA-3 8B with note-level bootstrap 95% CIs. Results. On 259-note seen and OOV splits the hybrid pipeline achieves Test-Time Pair F1 of 0.997 and 0.986 with 0.00-day MAE. Baselines reach high action F1 (LLaMA-3 0.992; GPT-4o-mini 0.963 seen) but Pair F1 stays at 0.51-0.57 (LLaMA-3) and 0.53 (GPT-4o-mini), CIs non-overlapping with the hybrid. Conclusion. Separating learned entity extraction from deterministic date arithmetic outperforms generation on this benchmark, generalizes to held-out actions, and exposes failure modes. Transfer to real EHR notes is the next validation; a first-pass realism check is in Limitations.

</details>


### [33] [Hubness, Not Anisotropy, Drives Cross-Lingual Retrieval Asymmetry in Multilingual Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26575)
*Adib Sakhawat,Fardeen Sadab,Atik Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了多语言嵌入模型跨语言检索非对称性的根本原因——hubness而非各向异性，并证明CSLS通过修正相似度度量而非消除特定hub向量即可有效解决该问题，建议将CSLS作为默认检索度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言检索假设对称性成立，但实际不然。多语言嵌入空间存在多种几何病理（hubness、各向异性、质心漂移、幅度差异），需确定哪个是检索非对称性的主要驱动因素。

Method: 使用6,518条英孟印阿四语惯用语和谚语平行语料，5种生产级编码器；将检索失败形式化为互最近邻互惠性缺陷；通过5个预注册实验测试hubness假说；对比CSLS校正与hub向量消融手术效果。

Result: Hub质量在互惠性联合回归中占主导地位（49.5%支配份额，是次优预测因子的1.68倍；偏R²=0.302 vs 各向异性的0.003）；CSLS校正填补了63.5%的最差-最佳互惠性差距；模型内效应量比hub向量消融大130倍。

Conclusion: Hubness是相似度度量的病理而非单个向量的问题；anisotropy与hubness在统计上可分离；建议用CSLS替代余弦相似度作为多语言嵌入检索的默认度量。

Abstract: Multilingual embedding models are deployed under the assumption that cross-lingual retrieval is symmetric: if a query in language A retrieves its translation in language B, the reverse should also hold. In practice it does not. Using a parallel corpus of 6,518 idiomatic and proverbial expressions in English, Bangla, Hindi, and Arabic, embedded by five production-grade encoders (Gemini, Mistral, OpenAI-L, OpenAI-S, Qwen), we formalise this failure as a deficit in mutual nearest-neighbour reciprocity and test a single mechanistic claim: among the geometric pathologies of multilingual spaces, hubness, not anisotropy, centroid drift, or magnitude, is the dominant causal driver. Across five pre-registered experiments with falsification conditions specified in advance, hub mass dominates a joint regression on reciprocity (49.5% dominance share, 1.68x the next predictor; partial R^2 = 0.302 versus 0.003 for anisotropy), while a hub-aware score correction (CSLS) closes 63.5% of the worst-to-best reciprocity gap and yields a mean within-model effect size 130x larger than surgical hub-vector ablation. The latter contrast pinpoints the mechanism: hubness is a pathology of the similarity metric, not of individual hub vectors. We resolve the well-known anisotropy-hubness paradox by showing the two are statistically dissociable, and we recommend replacing cosine similarity with CSLS as the default retrieval metric for multilingual embedding pipelines.

</details>


### [34] [LATTE: Forecasting Peer Anchored Preference Trajectories for Personalized LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26612)
*Jinze Li,Xiaoyan Yang,Shuo Yang,Jinfeng Xu,Yue Shen,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.CL
