TL;DR: LATTE通过同侪锚定机制将用户偏好表示为相对差异状态，利用轻量序列预测器预测用户偏好的时序演变，并通过单软令牌注入冻结LLM，在Amazon Reviews 2023和MemoryCD数据集上显著优于多种基线方法，验证了轨迹预测而非静态档案或软提示接口才是性能提升的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法将用户过去行为视为聚合档案，将稳定身份、最近漂移和项目内容混在同一表示中，无法有效区分这些不同维度的信息。此外，检索或摘要方法虽然高效，但缺乏对用户偏好动态演变过程的显式建模。

Method: LATTE提出了一种将个性化建模为同侪锚定相对偏好状态预测的框架。具体包括：(1) 对于每个历史会话，从响应相同项目的可比用户中提取时间掩码基线并相减，得到衡量目标用户与同侪差异的状态；(2) 使用轻量级序列预测器预测轨迹中的下一状态；(3) 通过状态到令牌桥接器，将预测状态注入冻结的指令调优LLM中，使用单个锚定软令牌实现。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews 2023和MemoryCD数据集上，LATTE一致性地优于检索、摘要记忆、静态潜在档案、差异感知潜在档案和软提示压缩等基线方法。在Amazon Reviews 2023上，ROUGE-L从静态潜在档案的0.219和最强潜在压缩基线的0.245提升至0.259。诊断分析表明，性能提升主要归因于预测用户特定轨迹信息，而非简单添加软提示接口。

Conclusion: LATTE通过预测用户特定轨迹信息而非仅添加软提示接口来实现性能提升。在Amazon Reviews 2023和MemoryCD数据集上的一致性提升验证了该方法的有效性。LATTE通过将个性化建模为同侪锚定的相对偏好状态预测，实现了更精确的用户建模。

Abstract: Personalized generation with frozen large language models requires a conditioning signal that is both compact and current. Existing personalization methods typically retrieve or summarize user histories in text, or compress them into static latent profiles and soft prompts. These approaches are efficient, but they treat a user's past behavior as an aggregate profile and therefore mix stable identity, recent drift, and item content in the same representation. We propose LAtent Trajectory Tracking and Extrapolation (LATTE), a framework that represents personalization as forecasting a peer anchored relative preference state. For each historical session, LATTE subtracts a time masked baseline formed from comparable users who responded to the same item, producing a state that measures how the target user differs from peers under a shared item context. A lightweight sequence predictor then forecasts the next state in this trajectory, and a State to Token Bridge injects the forecast into a frozen instruction tuned LLM through a single anchored soft token. We provide a latent factor analysis showing when peer anchoring cancels shared item variation and why temporal forecasting trades off stale averages against noisy recent states. Experiments on Amazon Reviews 2023 and MemoryCD show that LATTE consistently outperforms retrieval, summary memory, static latent profiles, difference aware latent profiles, and soft prompt compression baselines. On Amazon Reviews 2023, LATTE improves average ROUGE-L from 0.219 for a static latent profile and 0.245 for the strongest added latent compression baseline to 0.259. Additional pairwise comparisons and diagnostic analyses suggest that the improvement is mainly due to forecasting user-specific trajectory information, rather than merely adding a soft prompt interface.

</details>


### [35] [Granuscore: A Reference-Free Measure of Granularity for Text Analysis and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26620)
*Lukas Ellinger,Alexander Fichtl,Miriam Anschütz,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Granuscore，一种利用层次嵌入空间结构特性的无参考粒度度量方法，能够恢复层次排序、捕捉跨语境粒度差异、解释句子特异性变化，并揭示QA模型行为的粒度特征差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言在不同的粒度层级上传递信息，从细粒度指称到宽泛描述。虽然粒度是人类交流的基础，但现有度量方法大多只捕捉表面细节或句子特异性。

Method: 利用层次嵌入空间的结构特性，引入了一种无参考的粒度度量方法Granuscore。

Result: Granuscore能够可靠地恢复Granola-EQ数据集上的层次排序，捕捉语篇情境中的粒度差异，解释句子特异性中超出句子长度的非线性变化。在QA基准测试中揭示了问题、金标准答案和模型输出在粒度上的差异。

Conclusion: Granuscore被定位为一种可扩展且广泛适用的工具，用于分析文本中的粒度。它能够恢复层次排序，捕捉不同语篇情境下的粒度差异，并解释句子特异性中的非线性变化。在QA基准测试中的应用揭示了模型行为的差异，并为QA数据集的难度特征提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Natural language conveys information at varying levels of granularity, from fine-grained references to broad descriptions. While granularity is fundamental to human communication, existing measures mostly capture surface detail or sentence specificity. We introduce Granuscore, a reference-free measure of granularity that leverages structural properties of a hierarchical embedding space. Granuscore reliably recovers hierarchical orderings on the Granola-EQ dataset and captures expected differences in granularity across discourse contexts. Across domains, we further show that Granuscore explains non-linear variation in sentence specificity beyond sentence length. Finally, we apply Granuscore to four question-answering benchmarks and analyze how granularity differs for questions, gold answers, and model outputs across response outcomes. The analysis reveals consistent differences in model behavior and provides a principled lens for characterizing the difficulty of QA datasets. Together, the results position Granuscore as a scalable, broadly applicable tool for analyzing granularity in text.

</details>


### [36] [Bounded Path Context: A Controlled Study of Visible Path History in LLM-Based Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26645)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Bounded Path Context (BPC) proposes that LLM-based KGQA systems should only include the last K hops in relation-selection prompts rather than the complete path history, achieving equal or better performance with fewer tokens.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM-based KGQA systems serialize complete partial paths into every routing prompt by default, despite the controller already maintaining exact symbolic state. This creates redundant token overhead without clear justification.

Method: Bounded Path Context (BPC) decouples two roles of path context: controller retains full paths symbolically for answer extraction/audit, while relation-selection prompt exposes only question, current entity, outgoing relation candidates, and at most last K hops. Controlled experiments sweep K while fixing graph neighborhoods, beam budget, depth, decoding, and answer-extraction format.

Result: BPC matches or exceeds full-history prompting with fewer tokens. Key findings: (1) K=1 optimal for 9B model on WebQSP (0.487 F1) and 4B model on both benchmarks; (2) K=0 best for 9B on CWQ (0.287 F1); (3) Significant token reduction (9.7-12.1%); (4) 71-84% examples unaffected by history length; (5) Affected cases show prior hops can both disambiguate and distract.

Conclusion: BPC outperforms full-history prompting while using fewer tokens. Qwen3.5-9B-AWQ: K=1 achieves 0.487 F1 on WebQSP (+0.015 vs full history), K=0 reaches 0.287 on CWQ (+0.013 vs full history). Reduces input tokens by 9.7-12.1%. At 4B scale, K=1 remains optimal. 71-84% examples unaffected; remaining cases reveal when prior hops disambiguate vs distract.

Abstract: LLM-based knowledge-graph question answering (KGQA) delegates graph traversal to language models, turning each question into a sequence of local relation-selection decisions repeated across beams and hops. A common but untested default is to serialize the complete partial path into every routing prompt, even though the controller already maintains this path as exact symbolic state. Bounded Path Context (BPC) decouples these two roles: the controller retains full paths in symbolic memory for answer extraction and audit, while the relation-selection prompt exposes only the question, the current entity, outgoing relation candidates, and at most the last K hops. A controlled sweep over K -- fixing graph neighborhoods, beam budget, depth, decoding, and answer-extraction format -- shows that bounded histories match or exceed full-history prompting on complete WebQSP and CWQ test sets with Qwen3.5-9B-AWQ: K=1 achieves 0.487 answer-set F1 on WebQSP versus 0.472 for full history, and K=0 reaches 0.287 on CWQ versus 0.274, with 9.7% and 12.1% fewer input tokens respectively. At the 4B scale, K=1 remains the strongest setting on both benchmarks. Per-example analysis reveals that 71-84% of examples are unaffected by history length, while the affected cases expose when prior hops disambiguate versus distract. These results suggest that path serialization length is better treated as a tunable interface variable than as a default assumption in LLM-based graph controllers.

</details>


### [37] [AI evaluation may bias perceptions: The importance of context in interpreting academic writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26662)
*Shang Wu,Randol Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，在评估科学写作中AI使用时，不考虑国家和领域上下文差异的 pooled benchmarks 会产生系统性偏差。Country-field-specific benchmarks能更准确、公平地测量AI使用情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI使用评估方法忽略了国家和研究领域间的上下文差异，导致AI使用估计产生偏倚。

Method: 基于Dimensions数据库的大规模期刊出版数据，构建人类撰写摘要与LLM改写摘要之间的差异基准。通过对比pooled benchmark与country-field-specific benchmarks的表现来验证方法有效性。

Result: Pooled基准会将预先存在的文体差异与AI生成文本混淆，在不同country-field组别中产生严重失真。Country-field-specific基准能够消除此类偏差，为比较提供更可信的基准线。应用于2025年出版物时，pooled benchmark系统性地高估某些国家和领域的AI使用，同时低估其他情况。

Conclusion: 上下文感知测量对准确、公平评估科学论文中的AI使用至关重要。不同国家和领域间存在预先存在的文体差异(pooled benchmark会将这些差异与AI生成文本混淆)，而country-field-specific benchmarks能有效消除这种偏差。

Abstract: This paper examines how estimates of AI use in scientific writing can be biased when evaluation methods ignore contextual differences across countries and fields. Using large-scale data on journal publications from Dimensions, we construct AI-likeness benchmarks based on differences between human-written and LLM-rephrased abstracts. We show that a pooled benchmark may confound pre-existing stylistic variation with AI-generated text, producing substantial distortions across country-field groups even in pre-LLM publications. In contrast, country-field-specific benchmarks attenuate such distortions and provide a more credible baseline for comparison. Applying these methods to publications in 2025 reveals that the pooled benchmark systematically overestimates AI use in certain countries and fields while underestimating it in others. These findings highlight the importance of context-aware measurement for accurate and equitable evaluation of AI use in science.

</details>


### [38] [The Labyrinth and the Thread: Rethinking Regularizations in Sequential Knowledge Editing for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26670)
*Zheng Wang,Kaixuan Zhang,Wanfang Chen,Jingwen Zhang,Xiaonan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型中顺序编辑知识的基础机制。通过优化分析证明一次性编辑与顺序编辑的形式等价性，表明稳定性来自正确处理累积约束而非特殊正则化，从而简化知识更新流程，提升可解释性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序编辑方法依赖复杂的正则化或约束机制，但这些机制的必要性尚不明确，需要系统性地探究有效且稳定的顺序编辑机制。

Method: 通过严格优化分析建立一次性编辑与顺序编辑之间的形式等价性，将等价性推广至更广泛的编辑目标类别；系统分析AlphaEdit的成功机制；测试常见正则化策略的必要性；扩展框架处理冲突编辑。

Result: 证明了许多常用正则化策略对于可靠的顺序更新是不必要的；稳定性可从正确处理累积编辑约束中自然产生，无需专门的正则化或零空间操作。

Conclusion: 研究表明，稳定性源于对累积编辑约束的正确处理，而非专门的正则化或零空间操作。提出了一个更简单、更可解释、更可靠的知识更新框架，并扩展至处理冲突编辑场景。

Abstract: Sequential editing of structured knowledge in large language models allows targeted factual updates without retraining, yet existing methods often rely on complex regularization or constraint mechanisms whose necessity remains unclear. In this work, we systematically investigate the mechanisms underlying effective and stable sequential editing. Specifically, we first analyze the empirical success of AlphaEdit and establish, via a rigorous optimization analysis, the formal equivalence between one-time and sequential editing. Building on this insight, we generalize the equivalence to a broader class of editing objectives, demonstrating that stability emerges naturally from properly accounting for accumulated editing constraints, rather than from specialized regularization or null-space operations. We empirically confirm that many commonly used regularization strategies are unnecessary for reliable sequential updates. Furthermore, we extend our framework to handle conflicting edits, ensuring robust and consistent behavior under contradictory updates. Ultimately, our work provides Ariadne's thread through the labyrinth of sequential editing, charting a path toward simpler, more interpretable, and dependable knowledge updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/Wangzzzzzzzz/OTE-SE-Alignment.

</details>


### [39] [NestedKV: Nested Memory Routing for Long-Context KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26678)
*Hong Chen,Xiang Liu,Yubo Gao,Yuxuan Fan,Bo Wang,Yuanlin Chu,Yuanguo Lin,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NestedKV是一种训练无关的KV缓存压缩方法，通过多时间尺度余弦异常评分和多级Key锚点策略，在低缓存保留率下显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV压缩方法仅依赖单一重要性信号，在全局差异性、局部片段性或即时相关性的上下文场景下表现脆弱。

Method: NestedKV：维护全局/块级/滑动窗口Key锚点，通过多时间尺度余弦异常评分，结合头自适应混合与惊异门控路由，配合自适应每头预算实现KV缓存压缩。

Result: Qwen3-4B上，r=0.75时RULER提升19.10分、LongBench提升19.29分；r=0.95时LongBench保持37.32分（KeyDiff仅17.55分）。

Conclusion: NestedKV通过多时间尺度余弦异常评分和多锚点策略，在低缓存保留率场景下显著优于单一信号方法，验证了混合重要性信号的优越性。

Abstract: Long-context language models are limited by the memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache. Existing training-free KV compression methods usually rank tokens by one importance signal -- attention, recency, layer-wise allocation, or key distinctiveness -- which becomes brittle when useful context is globally distinctive, locally episodic, or immediately relevant. We introduce NestedKV, a key-only KV cache compression method inspired by the Continuum Memory System in Nested Learning. NestedKV maintains global, block-level, and sliding-window key anchors, scores tokens by multi-time-scale cosine anomaly, and combines the resulting rankings with a training-free outer learner using head-adaptive mixing and surprise-gated token routing. The score is paired with adaptive per-head budgets and requires no training or LLM modification. Across RULER (4k--32k), LooGLE, LongBench, LongBench-E, InfiniteBench, and MMLU-Pro on Qwen3 and Llama-3.2 models, NestedKV is strongest when the retained cache is small. On Qwen3-4B, it improves over KeyDiff by up to 19.10 points on RULER and 19.29 on LongBench at $r=0.75$; at $r=0.95$, it retains 37.32 on LongBench versus 17.55 for KeyDiff.

</details>


### [40] [An In-Vitro Study on Cross-Lingual Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26683)
*Adrian Cosma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper introduces a controlled in-vitro framework to study cross-lingual transfer by creating procedurally generated languages. Through 700 experiments, they demonstrate that tokenization preserving cross-lingual substructure is more critical than lexical similarity or tokenizer balance. Smaller vocabularies facilitate transfer by maintaining decomposable shared fragments, and the transfer process occurs in stages from grammar/type competence to lexical generalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cross-lingual transfer in language models is difficult to study in natural corpora due to entanglement of multiple confounding factors: lexical overlap, morphology, data imbalance, and tokenization cannot be independently varied.

Method: In-vitro controlled framework using two procedurally generated languages sharing ontology, typed grammar, and compositional structure but differing in surface realization. This allows independent manipulation of lexical distance, minority-language proportion, tokenizer training regime, and vocabulary size across 700 controlled runs.

Result: Key findings: (1) Transfer is governed more by whether tokenization preserves reusable cross-lingual substructure than by tokenizer balance or raw lexical similarity; (2) Smaller vocabularies often improve masked transfer by keeping words decomposable into shared fragments, while larger vocabularies can turn forms into language-specific atoms; (3) Transfer emerges as a staged process where grammatical and type-level competence precede masked lexical generalization.

Conclusion: Tokenization acts as a "bridge" mechanism where bridge strength strongly correlates with masked reachability, explaining how cross-lingual transfer operates in neural language models.

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer in language models is difficult to study in natural corpora because lexical overlap, morphology, data imbalance, and tokenization are entangled. We introduce an in-vitro framework with two procedurally generated languages that share the same ontology, typed grammar, and compositional structure, but differ in surface realization. This lets us independently vary lexical distance, minority-language proportion, tokenizer training regime, and vocabulary size, while evaluating transfer on a masked minority-language condition whose lexical forms are never observed during training. Across 700 controlled runs, we find that transfer is governed less by tokenizer balance or raw lexical similarity than by whether tokenization preserves reusable cross-lingual substructure. Smaller vocabularies often improve masked transfer by keeping words decomposable into shared fragments, whereas larger vocabularies can turn forms into language-specific atoms. We further show that transfer emerges as a staged process: grammatical and type-level competence precede masked lexical generalization. Finally, we attempt to explain this mechanism through tokenizer bridges and show that bridge strength correlates strongly with masked reachability.

</details>


### [41] [The Need for an External Observer Formalizing the Sufficiency Gap: A Mathematical Extension of Mixture Identifiability and Contextual Grounding in Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26711)
*Francesco Corielli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在隐状态未观测情况下序列预测器的置信度问题，揭示了充足性缺口源于边缘化而非优化误差，并通过贝叶斯分析证明外部信号的纠正效果受限于文本后验权重分配的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题背景：即使理想的无穷容量序列预测器能精确恢复仅文本边缘律，当观测前缀与错误隐机制兼容时，仍会产生过度自信问题。这引发了对"充足性缺口"本质的追问。

Method: 构建一个二元混合机制过程：一个确定性文本机制和一个由未观测隐状态驱动的随机机制。形式化检索、工具使用和外部接地，通过保真度 γ∈[1/2,1] 的辅助二元信号进行贝叶斯更新。

Result: 引入辅助信号后推导出情境主导阈值：纠正信号在保真度超过文本后验权重时恰好逆转由误导性机制引起的赔率。但该阈值只能减少，不能完全消除充足性缺口；完全闭合需要完美揭示相关隐状态或等效验证机制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了充足性缺口的本质——源于对未观测隐状态的边缘化，而非优化误差。温度 scaling 无法弥补缺失的上下文；接地机制必须既具有信息性又可被模型学习使用；在高风险领域，自主序列模型需要结构上解耦的观察者或验证器。

Abstract: We construct a binary mixed-regime process with one deterministic textual regime and one random regime governed by an unobserved latent state. Even an ideal infinite-capacity sequence predictor that exactly recovers the text-only marginal law can become overconfident when the observed prefix is compatible with the wrong latent regime. The resulting entropy difference is not an ordinary optimization error; it is a sufficiency gap caused by marginalization over an unobserved state. We then formalize retrieval, tool use, and external grounding through an auxiliary binary signal with fidelity $γ\in [1/2,1]$. The resulting Bayesian update yields a contextual dominance threshold: a corrective signal reverses the posterior odds induced by the textual history exactly when its fidelity exceeds the text-only posterior weight assigned to the misleading regime. This threshold reduces, but does not generally eliminate, the sufficiency gap; complete closure requires perfect revelation of the relevant latent state or an equivalent verification mechanism. The analysis clarifies why temperature scaling cannot restore missing context, why grounding mechanisms must be both informative and learnably usable by the model, and why autonomous sequence models require structurally decoupled observers or verifiers in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [42] [Rethinking the Multilingual Reasoning Gap with Layer Swap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26735)
*Maxence Lasbordes,Amélie Chatelain,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在更大规模和可比监督条件下重新评估了多语言大模型的本地推理差距，发现该差距（1.9-3.5%）比先前报告小得多。通过权重分析发现推理核心是语言无关的（位于中间层），而外层是语言特定的。基于此发现，提出Layer Swap技术，通过迁移英语专家的强推理中间层到本地专家，有效缩小了本地推理差距，同时保留了目标语言输出的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为强迫CoT保持在输入语言（本地推理）相比允许先用英语推理再翻译回输入语言（英语转向推理）会显著降低性能，但这些研究多依赖推理时干预或有限的本地语言训练数据，需要在更大规模和可比监督条件下重新审视这一差距。

Method: 构建跨6种语言的长推理数据集；在Qwen3-8B-Base基础上分别进行本地推理和英语转向推理模式的微调；在数学、科学、常识和代码领域进行评估；对本地专家模型进行权重空间分析；提出Layer Swap方法——将英语专家较强的中间层迁移到各本地专家模型中。

Result: 在可比监督设置下，本地推理差距缩小至1.9-3.5%，远低于先前研究；权重空间分析发现本地专家模型在中间层有对齐的微调更新，在外层存在分歧，显示出语言无关的推理核心被语言特定层包围的结构；Layer Swap可关闭大部分本地推理差距。

Conclusion: Layer Swap技术通过迁移英语专家模型的中间层到本地语言专家模型，成功缩小了大部分本地推理差距，同时保持了目标语言的CoT输出。研究发布了所有模型和数据集。

Abstract: Recent reasoning Large Language Models produce a chain-of-thought (CoT) predominantly in English, even when prompted in non-English languages. Prior work suggests that forcing the CoT to remain in the input language (\emph{native reasoning}) substantially degrades performance relative to allowing the model to reason in English before answering in the input language (\emph{English-pivoted reasoning}). However, most studies of this native reasoning gap rely on inference-time interventions or limited native-language training data. We revisit this comparison at a larger scale and under comparable supervision. We construct long multilingual reasoning datasets across six languages (English, French, German, Spanish, Chinese and Swahili); fine-tune specialists in both native and English-pivoted regimes on top of \texttt{Qwen/Qwen3-8B-Base}, and evaluate across mathematics, science, general knowledge, and code. In this setting, the average native reasoning gap shrinks to 1.9--3.5\% across the five non-English languages, considerably smaller than previously reported. Weight-space analysis of the native specialists reveals aligned fine-tuning updates in the middle layers and divergence in the outer layers. This points to a largely language-agnostic reasoning core surrounded by language-specific layers. Exploiting this structure, we introduce a Layer Swap: transferring the English specialist's stronger reasoning mid-layers into each native specialist, closing most of the native reasoning gap across the five non-English languages while preserving CoT in the target language. We release all models and datasets.

</details>


### [43] [KARMA: Karma-Aligned Reward Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26738)
*Jared Scott,Jesse Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KARMA框架通过Reddit社交数据训练奖励模型，用强化学习微调LLM以提升语用能力。关键发现：预测karma更好的奖励模型反而导致更差的下游表现；仅使用对话上下文的奖励模型效果更优。所有条件下KARMA都会降低事实性，且这一权衡似乎根植于奖励信号本身而非训练数据噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Human communication relies heavily on implicit social signals (tone, context, conversational norms) rather than semantic content alone. Training LLMs to handle context-sensitive conversational behavior requires learning from large-scale social interaction data, which presents challenges in capturing pragmatics while maintaining other desirable properties.

Method: KARMA trains a reward model on Reddit conversations to predict response valuation conditioned on conversational context, then fine-tunes LLMs via reinforcement learning. The framework was evaluated with and without direct exposure to social media data to isolate the effects of the reward signal itself.

Result: A context-only reward model (worse at predicting Reddit karma) actually yielded substantially better downstream performance than a karma-predictive model. KARMA improves pragmatics-mediated behaviors with mitigated side effects, but factuality consistently diminishes across all conditions—including when the downstream model has no direct Reddit data exposure.

Conclusion: KARMA improves pragmatics-mediated conversational behaviors while largely mitigating side effects; however, the factuality-pragmatics trade-off appears inherent to the reward signal itself, as factuality diminishes even without direct Reddit exposure. The counterintuitive finding that better karma prediction leads to worse downstream performance suggests that context-only reward models may be more suitable for practical deployment.

Abstract: Human communication depends on implicit social signals where effectiveness is shaped by tone, context, and conversational norms rather than semantic content alone. We introduce KARMA (Karma-Aligned Reward Model Adaptation), a framework for LLM learning of context-sensitive conversational behavior from large-scale social interaction data. KARMA trains a reward model on Reddit conversations to predict response valuation conditioned on context, and uses this signal to fine-tune language models via reinforcement learning to improve performance on pragmatics-mediated tasks. Critically, we find that the highest performing reward model does not lead to better downstream model alignment: a reward model relying exclusively on conversational context was a worse predictor of Reddit karma but yielded substantially better downstream performance. We evaluate the effects of KARMA applied to a downstream model with and without direct exposure to the social media data. The resulting models show improved pragmatics-mediated behaviors with largely mitigated undesirable side effects. Factuality is consistently diminished by KARMA across all conditions, including when the downstream model has no direct exposure to Reddit data, suggesting that this tension is embedded in the reward signal itself rather than introduced by noisy training data.

</details>


### [44] [From Snippets to Semantics: Rethinking Evidence Granularity for Multilingual Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26755)
*Babu Kumar,Gaurav Kumar,Ayush Garg,Aditya Kishore,Jasabanta Patro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SEEK框架，通过语义主题转换识别和自适应分块策略从完整事实核查文章中构建连贯证据块，结合多语言编码器和LoRA微调的大语言模型进行真实性预测，在多语言事实核查任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统依赖搜索片段、句子级证据或局部分割段落，容易遗漏关键上下文并产生碎片化证据，无法满足可靠事实性预测所需的完整证据需求。

Method: 该方法首先通过识别语义主题转换来构建连贯的证据块，同时保留本地验证上下文；然后使用多语言编码器对证据块进行编码；最后通过LoRA适配器对多语言大语言模型进行微调以预测真实性。

Result: SEEK在X-FACT和RU22Fact数据集上的宏观F1值相比语义分块提升10%，相比句子分块提升19%，相比搜索片段基线提升20%。证据完整性和显著性分析表明该方法保留了更丰富的验证上下文。

Conclusion: SEEK框架通过语义分块策略显著提升了多语言事实核查的性能，在X-FACT和RU22Fact数据集上均展现出优于基线方法的表现，证明了构建连贯证据块对提升事实核查可靠性的有效性。

Abstract: Multilingual fact verification requires evidence that is both relevant and sufficiently complete for reliable factuality prediction. However, existing systems often rely on search snippets, sentence-level evidence, or locally segmented passages, which can miss decisive context and produce fragmented evidence. To overcome these limitations, we propose SEEK, a Semantic Evidence Extraction with an adaptive chunKing framework that constructs coherent evidence chunks from full fact-checking articles by identifying semantic topic transitions and preserving local verification context. The constructed chunks are encoded using a multilingual encoder and then multilingual LLMs are finetuned using LoRA adapter for veracity prediction. Experiments on X-FACT and RU22Fact show that SEEK improves macro-f1 by up to 10% over semantic chunking, 19% over sentence chunking, and 20% over search-snippet baselines. Evidence completeness and significance analyses further show that SEEK preserves richer verification context and enables more reliable multilingual fact-checking.

</details>


### [45] [Quality Without Usefulness: LLM-Generated XAI Narratives as Trust Heuristics Rather Than Decision Aids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26770)
*Fabian Lukassen,Jan Herrmann,Christoph Weisser,Alexander Silbersdorff,Benjamin Saefken,Thomas Kneib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Large Language Models can transform XAI outputs into high-quality natural language explanations, but this quality does not translate to practical usefulness. In energy forecasting tasks, such explanations fail to improve accuracy while inflating user confidence through mere text presence, and reduce the ability to detect unreliable predictions—highlighting a "Quality-Usefulness Gap" that demands evaluation beyond text-quality metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work demonstrated that LLMs can produce Natural Language Explanations (NLEs) scoring highly on quality metrics (plausibility, coherence, comprehensibility). However, it remains unclear whether these high-quality NLEs translate to practical usefulness in real-world tasks.

Method: Five controlled experiments (2,730 judgments across 60 test instances) in a time-series energy forecasting domain, each operationalizing a distinct facet of usefulness. A placebo control was used to isolate the effect of text presence versus content.

Result: NLEs do not improve task accuracy across any of the five tasks while inflating self-reported confidence. This confidence boost is driven by text presence rather than content. In out-of-distribution detection, NLEs reduce the LLM judge's ability to flag unreliable predictions, providing false reassurance that masks model failure.

Conclusion: The study introduces and characterizes the "Quality-Usefulness Gap" and argues that XAI-to-NLE pipeline evaluation must extend beyond text-quality metrics to downstream task performance.

Abstract: Prior work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can transform Explainable AI (XAI) outputs into Natural Language Explanations (NLEs) that score highly on quality metrics such as plausibility, coherence, and comprehensibility. But does explanation quality translate to practical usefulness? We investigate this question in a time-series energy forecasting domain through five controlled experiments (2,730 judgments across 60 test instances), each operationalising a distinct facet of usefulness studied in the XAI literature. Holding NLE quality constant at the high levels established by a prior factorial study, we find that NLEs do not improve task accuracy on any of the five tasks, while inflating self-reported confidence. A placebic control shows that this confidence boost is driven by text presence rather than content. In an out-of-distribution detection task, NLEs reduce the LLM judge's ability to flag unreliable predictions, providing false reassurance that masks model failure. We characterise these findings as the Quality-Usefulness Gap and argue that evaluation of the XAI-to-NLE pipeline must extend beyond text-quality metrics to downstream task performance.

</details>


### [46] [EmoDistill: Offline Emotion Skill Distillation for Language Model Agents in Adversarial Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26785)
*Yunbo Long,Haolang Zhao,Lukas Beckenbauer,Liming Xu,Alexandra Brintrup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出EmoDistill框架，通过离线方式将情绪谈判技能提炼到语言模型智能体中。该框架结合隐式Q学习进行情绪选择，以及LoRA微调与判断策略优化进行情绪表达，在多个高风险谈判场景中超越基线方法，并展现出良好的跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练LLM的对齐优化在对抗性谈判中可能成为弱点——情绪化语言会引导智能体偏离自身利益。论文认为情绪是一个战略性行动渠道，而非表面风格，因此需要专门将情绪谈判技能提炼到语言模型智能体中。

Method: EmoDistill将情绪策略分解为情绪选择和情绪表达两部分：隐式Q学习（IQL）选择器学习"何时"表达情绪，而基于低秩适应（LoRA）的策略通过监督微调（SFT）和判断策略优化（JPO）学习"如何"表达情绪。

Result: 在四个情绪敏感的高风险谈判领域中，基于EmoDistill框架训练的小型语言模型（SLM）策略获得最高效用，优于vanilla SLM/LLM基线和仅使用IQL的情绪选择方法。消融实验表明情绪条件化是关键，迁移研究表明该方法能够泛化到未见领域、未见对手以及训练-训练锦标赛场景。

Conclusion: EmoDistill能够从离线智能体间交互中学习技能，避免了训练过程中的高昂在线谈判成本，且展现出跨领域、跨对手及训练-训练锦标赛的泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-trained LLMs are often optimized to align responses with human preferences, making them safe, polite, and conversationally appropriate. In adversarial negotiation, however, this alignment can become a vulnerability: emotionally framed language may steer agents toward the counterparty's interests. Using GoEmotions-based affective prompting, we show that emotion substantially shifts negotiation outcomes, suggesting that emotion is a strategic action channel rather than a surface style. Thus, we introduce \textbf{EmoDistill}, an offline framework for distilling emotional negotiation skills into language model agents. EmoDistill decomposes emotional strategy into emotion selection and emotion expression: an Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) selector learns \emph{which} emotion to express, while a Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)-based policy learns \emph{how} to express it through Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Judge Policy Optimization (JPO). Across four emotion-sensitive, high-stakes negotiation domains, SLM policies trained under the EmoDistill framework achieve the highest utility, outperforming vanilla SLM/LLM baselines and IQL-only emotion selection. Ablations show that emotion conditioning is essential, and transfer studies demonstrate generalization across domains, unseen counterparties, and trained-vs-trained tournaments. Overall, EmoDistill learns skills from offline agent-to-agent interactions, avoiding costly online negotiation during training.

</details>


### [47] [SeDT: Sentence-Transformer Decision-Transformer Conditioning for Multi-Turn Conversation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26788)
*Ramakrishna Vamsi Setti,Jagadeesh Rachapudi,Sachin Chaudhary,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SeDT方法解决LLM多轮对话中的性能崩溃问题。该方法通过语义、词汇和位置三信号计算累积相关性分数并标注对话分片，使模型能在最终回合识别关键约束。实验证明此训练-free推理方法在9个组合中均有效，最高提升37.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在单轮任务中表现优异，但当相同任务跨多轮增量揭示时，性能下降高达39%，且几乎完全由可靠性失败导致（最佳情况下aptitude仅下降16%，但不可靠性增加超过112%）。根因在于对话历史结构扁平化，所有先前回合被赋予同等隐含权重，模型无法区分关键约束与偶然对话。

Method: SeDT从离线强化学习中引入return-to-go条件化机制，使用三个互补信号（语义、词汇、位置）计算累积相关性分数，对对话分片进行标注，在最终回合将完整标注后的历史呈现给模型。

Result: 在三个LLM和三个生成任务的Lost-in-Conversation基准测试中，SeDT在所有9个模型-任务组合中均优于分片基线，性能提升最高达37.7%，且在7个组合中同时减少不可靠性。

Conclusion: SeDT通过无需训练、无需权重更改且不丢弃上下文的推理时方法，在9个模型-任务组合中均优于基线，最高提升37.7%，同时在7个组合中降低不可靠性，证明了"告知模型哪些过去回合重要"足以显著恢复对话中丢失的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve impressive performance when a task is fully specified in a single turn, yet the same models lose up to 39% of that performance when the identical task is revealed incrementally across multiple turns, a phenomenon documented at scale as Lost in Conversation. Crucially, this collapse is almost entirely a reliability failure; the best case, the aptitude only falls 16%, while the unreliability more than doubles (+112%). We argue that the root cause is structural, a flat conversation history assigns equal implicit weight to every prior turn, giving the model no signal to distinguish a critical constraint from incidental dialog. We present SeDT Sentence-transformer Decision-Transformer, a training-free inference-time method that resolves this by importing return-to-go conditioning from offline reinforcement learning. SeDT annotates each conversation shard with a cumulative relevance score derived from three complementary semantic, lexical, and positional signals and presents the full annotated history to the model at the final turn, without weight changes, without training data, and without discarding context. Evaluated on the Lost-in-Conversation benchmark in three LLMs and three generation tasks, SeDT outperforms the sharded baseline in all nine model-task combinations, with gains up to +37.7% in mean performance P and simultaneous reductions in unreliability in seven of the nine combinations. In short, telling the model which past turns matter is sufficient to substantially recover the performance lost in conversation.

</details>


### [48] [Psychological Constructs in Shared Semantic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26801)
*Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用词嵌入空间将心理学构念表示为方向向量，实现跨测量工具的可比性。通过监督语义差异法(SSD)估计构念语义梯度，以VAD(效价、唤醒度、支配性)作为参考轴进行投影测试，在情感分类(GoEmotions)和人格特质(Big Five)数据上验证了该框架的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学构念通常在不同的测量工具、数据集和研究传统中进行测量，导致直接比较困难，缺乏统一的可通约性框架。

Method: 提出监督语义差异法(Supervised Semantic Differential)：(1)从文本-结果关联中估计构念特异性语义梯度；(2)将语义梯度投影到理论驱动的参考轴上。以效价、唤醒度和支配性(VAD)作为情感坐标系统进行验证。

Result: (1)从英语词汇情感norms中恢复了可解释的VAD方向；(2)将27个GoEmotions类别的语义梯度投影到VAD空间，重现了预期的情感组织结构(尤其沿效价和唤醒度)；(3)成功应用于Big Five人格领域和IPIP-NEO-300 facets水平。

Conclusion: 词嵌入空间可支持跨测量工具的构念级比较，但需对语义定位的稳定性和可解释性进行评估。该框架为整合不同心理学测量传统提供了方法学基础。

Abstract: Psychological constructs are often measured in separate instruments, datasets, and research traditions, which makes direct comparison difficult. This paper proposes a framework for making such constructs semantically commensurate by representing and comparing them as directions in a shared word-embedding space. Using Supervised Semantic Differential, we estimate construct-specific semantic gradients from text-outcome associations and project them onto theoretically motivated reference axes. As an initial test case, we use Valence, Arousal, and Dominance (VAD) as an affective coordinate system. First, we recover interpretable VAD directions from English word-level affective norms. Second, we project semantic gradients for 27 GoEmotions categories into this space and recover the expected organization of emotions, especially along valence and arousal. Third, we apply the same procedure to Big Five personality domains and facets derived from IPIP-NEO-300 item-factor associations. Domain-level placements are broadly coherent, while facet-level results are more exploratory because they rely on sparse questionnaire text. The results suggest that embedding spaces can support construct-level comparison across otherwise incommensurable psychological measurements, provided that semantic placements are assessed for stability and interpretability.

</details>


### [49] [Generating Logically Consistent Synthetic Supply Chain Data with LLM-Driven Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26823)
*Yunbo Long,Ge Zheng,Liming Xu,Alexandra Brintrup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabKG框架，通过构建列关系知识图谱(CR-KG)并结合多LLM集成与潜在扩散模型，生成既保留统计分布又遵守运营逻辑约束的合成供应链表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据是解决供应链分析中数据稀缺和数据隐私问题的有前景方案，但现有表格生成模型主要优化分布保真度和下游预测效用，生成的记录虽然统计上逼真但往往违反基本操作约束。供应链数据的"物理"特性（包括时序排序、数学依赖、层级分类和条件规则）尚未被有效保留。

Method: TabKG构建列关系知识图谱(CR-KG)来表示数据操作依赖关系，使用多LLM集成与多数投票机制从列元数据中提出候选关系，并针对真实数据进行验证以移除幻觉或不支持的边。随后，将原始表格压缩为独立列，使用潜在扩散模型生成，再根据验证后的关系确定性重建依赖列，从而在发现的运营规则上强制执行逻辑一致性。

Result: 通过知识图谱验证操作规则并引导生成过程，TabKG能够确保合成数据在统计真实性的同时遵守运营逻辑约束。

Conclusion: TabKG provides a novel framework for generating logically consistent synthetic supply chain data by combining knowledge graph validation with latent diffusion models, ensuring both statistical fidelity and operational plausibility.

Abstract: Synthetic data offers a promising solution to two persistent barriers in supply chain analytics: data scarcity and data privacy. However, for synthetic data to support operational simulation and decision-making, it must do more than reproduce the statistical distributions of real records, and also preserve the \emph{operational logic} that governs supply chain processes, including the temporal orderings, mathematical dependencies, hierarchical taxonomies, and conditional rules that make a record operationally plausible. We consider this logic as the ``physics'' of supply chain data. Existing tabular generative models are primarily optimized for distributional fidelity and downstream predictive utility, and therefore often generate records that appear statistically realistic but violate fundamental operational constraints. This paper introduces \textbf{\textit{TabKG}}, a knowledge-graph-guided framework for logically consistent synthetic supply chain tabular data generation. TabKG constructs a \textbf{\textit{Column Relationship Knowledge Graph (CR-KG)}} to represent data operational dependencies. It uses a multi-LLM ensemble with majority voting to propose candidate relationships from column metadata, validates these relationships against real data to remove hallucinated or unsupported edges, and then uses the validated CR-KG to guide generation. Specifically, TabKG compresses the original table into independent columns, generates these columns using a latent diffusion model, and deterministically reconstructs dependent columns according to the validated relationships, enforcing logical consistency by construction with respect to the discovered operational rules.

</details>


### [50] [ContextGuard: Structured Self-Auditing for Context Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26827)
*Hongbo Jin,Chi Wang,Haoran Tang,Zhongjing Du,Xu Jiang,Jingqi Tian,Qiaoman Zhang,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了LLMs在上下文推理中的选择性失败模式——模型能处理主要推理线索，但在细节性、持续性或格式相关的上下文要求上存在系统性遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型尽管具备强大的推理能力，但在忠实应用复杂上下文知识方面仍存在明显不足

Method: 通过基准测试评估LLMs在上下文丰富任务中的推理表现

Result: 失败并非整体性推理崩溃，而是表现为局部缺陷：模型能跟随核心推理路径，却遗漏边缘、持续性或格式敏感的要求

Conclusion: LLM reasoning failures are selective rather than complete—models successfully follow central reasoning paths but systematically miss peripheral, persistent, or format-sensitive contextual requirements.

Abstract: Recent benchmarks reveal that despite strong reasoning capabilities, large language models (LLMs) still struggle to faithfully apply complex contextual knowledge. These failures are often not wholesale reasoning collapses: in context-rich tasks, models may follow the central reasoning path while missing peripheral, persistent, or format-sensitive requirements.

</details>

### [51] [Examining the Challenges of Intellectual Property in AI-Generated Productions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26590)
*Ali Mazhar,Mohammad Zare,Marjan Veysi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了AI自主生成作品情境下的知识产权保护问题，比较分析了伊朗法律框架（1969年版权法和专利商标注册法）与欧盟、英国、美国等体系的差异。研究发现伊朗现行法律存在显著监管缺口，建议通过修订现有法律或引入AI生成作品专项知识产权权利来解决归属问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统能够自主生成艺术、文学、音乐作品甚至发明成果，在缺乏人类创作者的情况下，知识产权制度面临道德权利和经济权利归属的前所未有的问题，以及如何为此类产出提供法律保护的挑战。

Method: 比较法研究方法：对照伊朗1969年《作者、作曲家和艺术家权利保护法》及《专利和商标注册法》与欧盟、英国、美国等法律体系，系统梳理现有法律视角下AI生成作品的知识产权保护问题及执法挑战。

Result: 揭示了现行伊朗法律框架中存在重大监管空白。

Conclusion: 建议修订现有法律框架，引入新型知识产权权利或指定相关人类主体作为所有权人，以平衡创新激励与人类创造力保护之间的张力。

Abstract: With the advancement of artificial intelligence systems capable of autonomously generating artistic, literary, musical works, and even inventions without direct human intervention, the intellectual property (IP) regime faces unprecedented questions and challenges. The most critical issue concerns the ownership of moral and economic rights in the absence of a human creator, and how such outputs can be granted legal protection. This paper first reviews the theoretical foundations and existing literature in this domain, then comparatively examines Iranian legal frameworks such as the 1969 Law for the Protection of Authors, Composers, and Artists Rights and the Patent and Trademark Registration Law-alongside other legal systems, including the European Union, the United Kingdom, and the United States. Furthermore, existing legal perspectives on the intellectual property of AI-generated works and the related enforcement challenges are analyzed. The findings reveal significant regulatory gaps within the current Iranian legal framework. To balance the promotion of innovation with the preservation of human creativity, revising existing laws and introducing novel approaches such as defining a specific intellectual property right for AI-generated works or designating ownership among associated human agents appears to be essential.

</details>


### [52] [Generative artificial intelligence and the marginalization of minoritized knowledges in higher education: the case of disability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26769)
*Fatiha Tali-Otmani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式AI对高等教育认识论结构的影响，指出以英语和西方为中心的数据集强化了认识论殖民性，导致非主流知识体系被边缘化。研究以残疾人群体为例，说明技术设计如何通过刻板印象化或排斥性参与造成双重边缘化，并提出研究者与机器的杂交是否能维持认识论多元性的开放性问题，同时承认算法校正的姑息性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能正在重构高等教育中科学知识的生产和验证方式，但这种技术系统并非中立，而是积极排斥非主流认识论，尤其对残疾人群体造成双重边缘化。作者认为有必要揭示这一现象背后的认识论殖民性机制。

Method: 研究整合了教育科学、关键技术研究和残疾人研究三个学科视角，通过跨学科框架分析生成式AI对认识论结构的影响，并以残疾人群体为典型案例进行论证。

Result: 研究表明训练数据集的盎格鲁中心和西方中心特性强化了认识论殖民性；技术架构常将残疾人限定在简单刻板印象中或排斥于设计过程之外，导致双重边缘化。算法校正作为补救策略存在结构性局限。

Conclusion: 文章提出了"研究者和机器的杂交"是否能保留认识论多元性这一问题，并审慎地承认当算法校正被用作纯粹姑息策略时存在结构性局限。这一结论既肯定了混合路径的可能性，又保持了批判性距离，为后续研究提供了有价值的张力空间。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence redefines higher education by restructuring the processes through which scientific knowledge is produced and validated. These systems are not neutral; they actively contribute to the marginalization of non-hegemonic epistemologies. This research draws upon educational sciences, critical technology studies, and disability studies to demonstrate that training datasets, which remain predominantly Anglophone and Western-centric, reinforce epistemic coloniality. The situation of persons with disabilities provides a particularly clear illustration of this phenomenon. Technological architectures frequently confine these individuals to reductive stereotypes or exclude them from the design process, leading to a double marginalization. This article examines whether a hybridization between the researcher and the machine might preserve epistemic plurality, while acknowledging the structural limitations inherent in algorithmic correction when used as a purely palliative strategy.

</details>


### [53] [Implementation of Big Data Analytics for Diabetes Management: Needs Assessment in the Rwanda Healthcare System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26786)
*Silas Majyambere,Tony Lindgren,Workneh Y. Ayele,Celestin Twizere*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This study evaluates Rwanda's healthcare system readiness to adopt big data analytics for diabetes management through a stakeholder workshop, identifying challenges and proposing an explainable machine learning-based BDA framework to improve prediction, monitoring, and clinical decision-making.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite the potential of Big Data Analytics (BDA) and machine learning for early diabetes detection and treatment support, their adoption in routine clinical practice remains limited, necessitating assessment of healthcare system readiness.

Method: A five-day workshop with 25 key stakeholders (clinicians, data managers, policymakers, medical researchers, nutritionists, and technology providers) was conducted to assess preparedness and identify existing gaps.

Result: Findings highlight both the potential and main challenges of BDA implementation in Rwanda's healthcare system, providing insights into preparedness levels and existing gaps.

Conclusion: The study culminates in a practical BDA framework proposal for diabetes management, integrating explainable machine learning models to enhance prediction, monitoring, and clinical decision-making in Rwanda's healthcare system.

Abstract: Diabetes is a chronic metabolic disease that can lead to serious health problems if not diagnosed and managed early. Big Data Analytics (BDA) and machine learning offer practical tools for analyzing large health datasets and supporting early detection and better treatment decisions. However, their use in routine clinical practice is still limited. This study examines the readiness of Rwanda's healthcare system to adopt big data analytics for diabetes management. As the country continues to expand its use of electronic medical records and health information systems, new opportunities arise for improving prediction, monitoring, and clinical decision-making. A five-day workshop involving 25 key stakeholders, including clinicians, data managers, policymakers, medical researchers, nutritionists, and technology providers, was conducted to assess preparedness and identify existing gaps. The findings highlight both the potential and the main challenges of BDA implementation. Based on these results, the paper proposes a practical BDA framework to support diabetes management strategies using explainable machine learning models.

</details>


### [54] [Faults and Pitfalls in Implementing the Right to be Forgotten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27171)
*Chen Sun,Nikolas Guggenberger,Supreeth Shastri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文从计算视角识别了实现"被遗忘权"的不确定性与风险，提出两阶段解决方案以弥合法律意图与技术实现间的鸿沟。研究表明该方法可避免80%的RTBF违规，并发现六个长期存在的反模式。最终在Elasticsearch中成功实现了RTBF能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR实施的前五年内发生了205次RTBF违规（平均每9天一次），而法律文本仅417词即可描述该权利，说明技术上实现RTBF存在重大挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段方法以弥合法律与计算之间的内在二分法；同时将RTBF能力实际集成到Elasticsearch开源搜索引擎中以验证技术可行性。

Result: 可避免GDPR第六年80%的RTBF违规；发现六个已成为RTBF反模式的长效计算与数据管理实践。

Conclusion: 成功将RTBF能力集成到Elasticsearch搜索引擎中，为开源系统提供了可落地的技术实现方案。

Abstract: Right to be Forgotten (RTBF) in one of the oldest and prominent of the legal data rights. While its legal intention is straight forward (for example, the GDPR describes it in just 417 words), the computing community has found it challenging to implement this in practice. For example, regulators have issued 205 RTBF violations in the first five years of GDPR i.e., an RTBF failure once every 9 days, on average. In this work, we identify the uncertainties and risks in supporting RTBF from a computing perspective. Then, to mitigate these challenges, we propose a two-phase approach that bridges an intrinsic dichotomy between law and computing. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our technique by showing how it could have fully avoided 80% of RTBF violations that occurred in the year-6 of GDPR. We also discover six long-standing practices of computing and data management that have become anti-patterns for RTBF. Finally, to ground our research, we introduce RTBF capability into Elasticsearch, a popular open-source search engine.

</details>


### [55] [How Students (Mis)understand Conditionals and Loops -- A Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26966)
*Dimitri Eckert,Christian Kautz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper develops a fine-grained taxonomy for categorizing novice programmers' difficulties in understanding control flow constructs (selection and iteration) using an iterative design methodology.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding student difficulties in programming is complex due to the wide range of topics and abundant varieties of misconceptions and errors, necessitating a systematic classification approach.

Method: Iterative Extended Taxonomy Design Process (ETDP), integrating prior research with empirical data from student quizzes and interviews to construct a fine-grained taxonomy.

Result: A fine-grained taxonomy distinguishing different student difficulties with detailed analysis of misunderstandings concerning conditional statements and loops.

Conclusion: The taxonomy provides a harmonized framework for computing education researchers to classify and analyze student errors, fostering theoretical insights and informing pedagogical strategies. Future work includes applying the taxonomy to novel student data and evaluating its usability.

Abstract: Understanding student difficulties in programming is a complex challenge due to the wide range of topics and the abundant varieties of misconceptions and errors. This paper presents the design and development of a fine-grained taxonomy that categorizes novice programmers' difficulties specifically related to reading and understanding the control flow constructs selection and iteration. Building upon prior research and our own empirical data from quizzes and interviews with students, the taxonomy is constructed through the iterative methodology of the Extended Taxonomy Design Process (ETDP). Key contributions include clear distinctions between different student difficulties and a detailed analysis of common student misunderstandings concerning conditional statements and loops. The taxonomy aims to aid computing education researchers by providing a harmonized framework to classify and analyze student errors, fostering deeper theoretical insights and informing pedagogical strategies. Future work will involve applying the taxonomy to novel student data and evaluating its usability among educators and researchers.

</details>


### [56] [Building an Atlas of Social Experiments to Link Studies, Reconcile Conflicts, and Bridge Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27153)
*Jiawei Zhang,Honglin Bao,Pengda Wang,Alex Yan,Xiao Liu,James A. Evans*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: ExAtlas框架通过将实验档案转化为结构化"图谱"，利用处理-结果空间的邻近搜索和效应组合来链接一致研究、调和冲突研究、提出桥接实验，从而系统化地挖掘已有实验中的潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会与行为科学每年开展大量实验，但其发现很少积累成连贯的知识图谱——即哪些研究发现相互一致、哪些存在冲突、哪些存在可填补的空白。

Method: 提出ExAtlas框架：给定目标研究，在处理-结果空间中搜索邻近的先前研究，检验其观测效应是否可以组合以预测目标效应；提供处理效应曲面局部平滑条件下的误差界。实验采用留出法（held-out）评估，并进行人工评估验证。

Result: ExAtlas在98.6%的案例中恢复效应方向；提出的桥接实验具有可行性和连通性；冲突解释有助于理论生成。

Conclusion: ExAtlas在98.6%的案例中正确恢复效应方向；人工评估表明其提出的桥接实验可行且具有连通性，冲突解释对理论生成有用，验证了从社会实验档案中提取潜在结构的方法可行性。

Abstract: Social and behavioral science runs thousands of experiments each year, yet their findings rarely accumulate into a coherent map of what is known, what conflicts, and what remains missing. We introduce ExAtlas, a framework for turning an archive of experiments into an atlas: a structured map in which studies link, conflict, or leave bridgeable gaps. Given a target study, ExAtlas searches for prior studies that are locally close in treatment and outcome space and asks whether their observed effects can be composed to predict the target effect. This yields three cases. If the composition succeeds and agrees with the observed result, ExAtlas links the target to consistent prior evidence. If composition succeeds but disagrees, ExAtlas reconciles the conflict and proposes candidate moderators or higher-level theories that could explain it. If composition fails, ExAtlas proposes bridge experiments to close the gap. We provide an error bound for composition under local smoothness of the treatment-effect surface. On held-out targets certified as locally supported, ExAtlas recovers effect direction in 98.6% of cases. Human evaluations further suggest that its proposed bridge experiments are plausible and exhibit connectedness, and that its conflict explanations are useful for theory generation. These results suggest that the archive of social experiments contains more latent structure than current practice extracts -- and that making this structure explicit can guide both future theory and future experimentation.

</details>


### [57] [Queue & AI: When Faster Tasks Slow Down the Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27202)
*Silvia Bartolucci,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文揭示了AI辅助工作流中存在"方差楔"现象——AI工具虽能加快初稿生成使平均任务速度提升，但会导致部分错误逃过审查并引发下游返工，使系统级延迟恶化。通过队列模型证明：拥堵条件下审核者会提高风险阈值削弱审查；在过载工作流中，AI稳定效果需同时满足AI处理比例超临界值且审核注意力成本低于手动完成两个条件。因此AI部署评估应综合考虑拥堵、再加工及人类监督稳健性，而非仅依赖平均指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍采用平均任务速度或处理时间等指标衡量AI生产力提升，但在任务累积与人类注意力稀缺的工作流场景中，这类指标可能产生误导性结论。

Method: 建立队列模型进行形式化分析，推导出两个解析性命题。

Result: AI辅助可产生"生产力假象"：平均完成时间下降源于AI快速初稿，但部分AI错误逃过审查导致下游返工，使系统级延迟增加。拥堵时审核者理性提高风险阈值，削弱审查力度。AI辅助仅在满足两个条件时才能稳定过载工作流：AI处理比例超临界值，且审核注意力成本低于手动完成。

Conclusion: AI部署评估应超越平均任务速度指标，需综合考虑拥堵、再加工及负载下人类监督的稳健性。阈值条件：AI处理比例需超过临界值，且人工审核与预期返工所需注意力低于纯手动完成。

Abstract: Quantifying the workplace productivity effects of Generative Artificial Intelligence is now central to economics, management, and public policy. The deployment of AI tools in customer service, writing, software development, and consulting operations has been reported to generate large per-task productivity gains, typically measured as tasks completed per worker-hour or reductions in mean handle time. We argue that such mean-based metrics can misrepresent AI's effects in workflows where tasks accumulate and compete for scarce human attention. AI assistance can generate a deceptive productivity signature: average completion times fall because AI tools typically supply a fast first draft, yet workflow-level performance deteriorates when a subset of AI errors escapes review and returns as costly downstream rework. We call this divergence between mean task speed and system-level delay the variance wedge. Depending on the operational parameters, the most time-efficient way to complete a workflow may undergo a transition between two task-processing regimes, a fully AI-assisted and a fully manual one. We formalize the mechanism as a queueing model and derive two main implications analytically. First, under congestion, reviewers rationally raise the risk threshold for checking AI outputs, reducing scrutiny precisely when it would matter the most. Second, AI assistance can stabilize an overloaded workflow only when (i) the fraction of tasks handled by AI exceeds a critical threshold, and (ii) the human attention required for review and expected rework is lower than the attention for manual completion, a requirement substantially more stringent than faster draft generation. These results suggest that AI deployment should be evaluated not only by average task speed, but by its overall effects on congestion, rework, and the robustness of human oversight under load.

</details>


### [58] [Algorithmic Monocultures in Hiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27371)
*Rishi Bommasani,Sarah H. Bana,Kathleen A. Creel,Dan Jurafsky,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Study of 3 million job applicants screened by algorithms from a single vendor reveals significant racial disparities and outcome homogeneity, with racial minority applicants disproportionately subjected to adverse algorithmic recommendations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many employers use hiring algorithms built by the same few vendors, creating algorithmic monoculture that may lead to systematic rejection of the same individuals and racial groups.

Method: Analysis of a novel dataset of 3 million applicants submitting 4 million applications, all screened by algorithms from the same vendor. Researchers leveraged the deterministic replicability of hiring algorithms to simulate outcomes if applicants applied to all positions.

Result: 14.74% of Asian and 25.87% of Black applicants' submissions were to positions that adversely impact these groups according to U.S. employment discrimination standards. Approximately 4% of applicants who applied to 10 positions were recommended for rejection from all positions—a rate higher than expected by chance.

Conclusion: Applicants would need to apply widely in order to ensure their applications are considered by a human reviewer, indicating systemic barriers in algorithmic screening.

Abstract: Many employers screen job applicants with algorithms built by the same few algorithm vendors. We hypothesize that algorithmic monoculture leads to the same individuals and members of the same racial groups facing rejection. We acquire and analyze a novel dataset of 3 million applicants submitting 4 million applications where all the applications are screened by algorithms built by the same vendor. We find clear racial disparities in applicant outcomes. Of all applications submitted by Asian and Black applicants, 14.74% and 25.87% are submitted to positions that adversely impact Asian and Black applicants, respectively, according to U.S. employment discrimination standards. Individuals also receive homogeneous outcomes: 4% of all applicants who apply to 10 positions are recommended for rejection from all positions, a rate higher than expected by chance. To better understand this homogeneity, we leverage the deterministic replicability of hiring algorithms to generate the outcomes applicants would have received if they applied to all positions. We show that applicants would need to apply widely in order to ensure their applications are considered by a human

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [59] [Edge AI Deployment Beyond Models: A BSP-Aware Systems Framework for Industrial Embedded Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26119)
*Pitchai Muthu M*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文论证了工业Edge AI部署应被视为系统问题而非后期应用打包练习，并提出了基于五层架构（BSP感知）的工业嵌入式平台部署框架，连接底层平台工作与可测量部署结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工业Edge AI项目往往先开发模型，后期才考虑平台部署。这种顺序虽便于早期演示，但在面对嵌入式系统的长生命周期、厂商特定内核、异构加速器、安全约束和复杂I/O路径等挑战时失效。此时，模型只是执行链的一个组件，需从传感器经板级支持包(BSP)到生产服务环。

Method: 提出了一个BSP感知的五层框架：(1)硬件层；(2)BSP/操作系统适配层；(3)运行时与加速层；(4)应用/推理层；(5)运维/验证层。该框架基于Android、NXP i.MX、NVIDIA Jetson、ONNX Runtime和TensorRT等厂商架构文档，以及嵌入式AI基准测试、设备不稳定性和异构边缘集群的相关文献。

Result: 提出了一个实用的五层框架，将底层平台工作与可量化的部署结果相连接，涵盖可重复性、可诊断性、持续吞吐量和现场可靠性等关键指标。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业嵌入式平台上的Edge AI部署提供了系统性指导，将底层平台工作与可量化的部署结果（可重复性、可诊断性、持续吞吐量和现场可靠性）联系起来，对工业AI落地具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Industrial Edge AI programs often begin with the model and only later confront the platform. That sequencing is attractive because it allows early demonstrations, but it breaks down when the deployment target is an embedded system with long product lifecycles, vendor-specific kernels, heterogeneous accelerators, safety constraints, and nontrivial I/O paths. In that environment, a model is only one component of a larger execution chain that begins at the sensor, traverses the board support package (BSP), and ends in a production service loop. This paper argues that robust Edge AI deployment must be treated as a systems problem rather than a late-stage application packaging exercise. The paper presents a BSP-aware framework for industrial embedded platforms organized around five layers: hardware, BSP/operating-system adaptation, runtime and acceleration, application/inference, and operations/validation. The discussion is grounded in vendor architecture documentation for Android, NXP i.MX, NVIDIA Jetson, ONNX Runtime, and TensorRT, and in systems literature on embedded AI benchmarking, device instability, and heterogeneous edge fleets. The result is a practical framework that connects low-level platform work to measurable deployment outcomes such as reproducibility, diagnosability, sustained throughput, and field reliability.

</details>


### [60] [Semantic-aware Token Selection and Resource Optimization for Communication-efficient Split Federated Fine-tuning in Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26120)
*Xianke Qiang,Zheng Chang,Geyong Min*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ST-SFLora是一种基于语义标记的分割联邦LoRA微调框架，通过语义传输效率(STE)指标和联合资源优化算法，在降低边缘设备计算负担的同时保持模型性能，解决了大规模视觉Transformer在边缘设备部署的通信效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型Transformer视觉模型在资源受限的移动边缘设备上部署面临硬件限制和动态无线环境的双重约束；传统联邦学习仅本地微调计算量过大，而现有分割联邦学习(SFL)传输高维激活令牌导致严重的通信开销。

Method: 提出ST-SFLora框架，基于语义传输效率(STE)指标进行令牌选择，并采用交替算法求解混合整数非凸优化问题，联合优化令牌选择、上行带宽分配和传输功率。

Result: 实验结果表明，ST-SFLora在多个基准测试中实现了最低的客户端资源消耗，同时在通信效率和模型性能之间取得了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: ST-SFLora通过语义标记选择机制和资源优化算法，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低了边缘设备的计算负担，为大规模视觉模型在边缘设备上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying large Transformer-based vision models on resource-limited mobile devices at network edge is severely constrained by hardware limitations and dynamic wireless environments. While federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training without sharing raw data, strictly local fine-tuning of such massive models remains computationally prohibitive for edge devices. Split federated learning (SFL) alleviates this burden by offloading deep layers to the edge server, yet it suffers from heavy communication overhead when transmitting high-dimensional activation tokens. To address this bottleneck, we propose ST-SFLora, a semantic token-based split federated LoRA fine-tuning framework. We introduce a new metric, \emph{Semantic Transmission Efficiency} (STE), to balance semantic retention and transmission cost. Based on STE, we formulate a joint resource optimization problem that dynamically determines token selection, uplink bandwidth allocation, and transmit power under latency and energy constraints. The resulting mixed-integer nonconvex problem is efficiently solved via an alternating algorithm. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ST-SFLora achieves the lowest client-side resource consumption among baselines while delivering a favorable trade-off between communication efficiency and model performance.

</details>


### [61] [GridPilot: Real-Time Grid-Responsive Control for AI Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26384)
*Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GridPilot achieves 97.2 ms grid-responsive GPU power control (6.9x faster than required), enabling MW-scale AI/HPC facilities to provide grid flexibility services with PUE-corrected meter-level accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Global data center electricity demand is outpacing grid capacity, while grid operators increasingly require large flexible loads to absorb variable renewable generation. The core challenge is quantifying and achieving practical, measurable response times for multi-megawatt AI/HPC facilities to provide grid services.

Method: GridPilot employs a three-tier predictive controller (milliseconds, seconds, hours timescales) with deterministic safety-island bypass for rapid response. Tested on NVIDIA V100 GPU testbed with real hardware validation. Includes instantaneous PUE correction to maintain commitment accuracy at meter level.

Result: Measured end-to-end trigger-to-target response of 97.2 ms on real 3-GPU hardware, achieving 6.9x speedup over Nordic FFR requirement (700 ms). PUE-aware controller reduces cooling-overhead drag by 2.5-5.8 percentage points across six European grid scenarios (Sweden to Poland).

Conclusion: GridPilot provides a practical proof of concept that MW-scale AI/HPC facilities can be engineered as controllable, grid-responsive flexibility resources. The system is released as open source, enabling wider adoption and validation of demand-side grid support from computational facilities.

Abstract: At global scale, data-center electricity demand is growing faster than the grids that supply it, while system operators increasingly require large flexible loads that can adjust power within seconds to absorb variable wind and solar generation. For multi-megawatt AI/HPC facilities, the key unresolved question is practical and measurable: how quickly can the software stack translate a grid request into a real change in GPU power at the facility meter, where commitments are settled? We answer this on real hardware with GridPilot, a three-tier predictive controller operating across milliseconds, seconds, and hours, augmented by a deterministic safety-island bypass for fast response. On a three-GPU NVIDIA V100 testbed, GridPilot achieves a measured end-to-end trigger-to-target response of 97.2 ms, which is 6.9x faster than the 700 ms requirement of Nordic Fast Frequency Reserve. We further incorporate an instantaneous Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE) correction so dispatched commitments remain robust at meter level rather than only at IT load level. In replay experiments across six representative European grids (from Sweden to Poland), the PUE-aware controller closes 2.5-5.8 percentage points of cooling-overhead drag. GridPilot is released as open source and serves as a proof of concept that MW-scale AI/HPC demand can be engineered as controllable, grid-responsive flexibility by design.

</details>


### [62] [Characterization-Guided GPU Fault Resilience in NVIDIA MPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26461)
*Rixin Liu,Xingqi Cui,Kaijian Wang,Xinheng Ding,Zirui Liu,Yuke Wang,Jiarong Xing*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对NVIDIA MPS故障韧性弱的问题，通过系统分析GPU故障特征和端到端处理流程，提出了两种互补机制：软件可隔离故障的隔离机制和专有软件故障的快速恢复机制，实现了容错MPS，在保持GPU共享效率的同时保障了故障隔离能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA MPS通过允许多进程并发执行于同一GPU实现细粒度GPU共享，可提高GPU利用率。然而MPS存在故障韧性不足的问题：一个进程的故障会导致所有共运行进程终止，限制了其在对弹性要求严格的场景（如多租户GPU集群）中的应用。

Method: 提出两种互补机制：1) 针对主要内存相关故障，设计了可在开源GPU驱动内核模块中通过软件干预完全隔离的故障隔离机制；2) 针对存在于专有软件中的其他故障，设计了基于虚拟内存的GPU驻留状态共享的快速恢复机制。

Result: 实验评估表明，所设计的两种机制能有效处理相应类型的GPU故障，且开销极小。

Conclusion: 设计的故障隔离机制和快速恢复机制能够有效处理相应故障，且开销极小。该方案已在不同GPU和 workloads 上完成评估，展示了在实际部署中的可行性和实用性。

Abstract: NVIDIA Multi-Process Service (MPS) enables fine-grained GPU sharing by allowing multiple processes to execute concurrently on the same GPU, making it an important mechanism for improving GPU utilization. However, MPS has weak fault resilience: a fault in one process can terminate all co-running processes, limiting its adoption in resilience-critical settings such as multi-tenant GPU clusters. In this work, we design fault-resilient MPS to solve this problem. Our design is guided by insights from a systematic characterization of GPU faults and a deep analysis of their end-to-end processing pipeline. Based on these insights, we design two complementary mechanisms. A fault isolation mechanism for the dominant memory-related faults that can be fully isolated by software intervention in the open GPU driver kernel module. For other faults whose process is within proprietary software, we design a practical mechanism -- fast recovery using virtual memory based GPU-resident state sharing. Our evaluation on different GPUs and workloads shows that these mechanisms can handle corresponding faults effectively with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [63] [StreamSplit: Continuous Audio Representation Learning via Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26523)
*Minh K. Quan,Pubudu N. Pathirana*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StreamSplit解决边缘设备上大规模对比学习(CL)的资源限制冲突，通过分布流式框架和强化学习自适应分割器，在Raspberry Pi 4到Apple M2等异构ARM平台上实现4.7倍延迟降低、77.1%带宽节省和52.3%能耗降低，同时保持精度损失在2.2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模对比学习（CL）的基础要求与边缘设备资源波动性根本矛盾：本地小批次会降低模型保真度，云端卸载又带来不可接受的延迟和带宽成本。现有静态模型压缩方案无法适应边缘环境运行时波动。

Method: 提出两大创新：(1) 基于分布的流式框架，使用可处理的混合损失函数解耦表征质量与本地批次大小；(2) 不确定性引导的自适应分割器，采用轻量级强化学习策略整合实时资源监控与嵌入不确定性，动态优化精度-延迟权衡。

Result: 在Raspberry Pi 4到Apple M2等多样化硬件上，StreamSplit相比服务器中心基线实现：单样本延迟降低4.7倍、带宽减少77.1%、能耗降低52.3%，精度损失控制在2.2%以内。

Conclusion: 自适应分布式学习是现代边缘生态系统的可行路径，证明流式CL可在异构ARM客户端平台上实际部署。

Abstract: Large-batch Contrastive Learning (CL), the foundation of modern representation learning, is fundamentally incompatible with the volatile resource constraints of edge devices. This conflict creates a dilemma: small on-device batches degrade model fidelity, while offloading to the cloud incurs unacceptable latency and bandwidth costs. Existing solutions often resort to static model compression, which fails to adapt to the runtime volatility of edge environments. To bridge this gap, we present StreamSplit, a novel framework that makes streaming CL practical across heterogeneous ARM client platforms. StreamSplit resolves the conflict between the continuous nature of ambient audio and the discrete batch requirements of models like CLAP and COLA. We introduce: (1) A distribution-based streaming framework that decouples representation quality from local batch size, using a tractable Hybrid Loss to maintain fidelity despite sparse updates; and (2) An Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitter that uses a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy to dynamically partition computation. Uniquely, this policy integrates real-time resource monitoring with embedding ambiguity to optimize the accuracy-latency trade-off on the fly. We evaluate StreamSplit on diverse hardware, from the resource-constrained Raspberry Pi 4 to the high-performance Apple M2. Results demonstrate that StreamSplit reduces per-sample latency by up to 4.7x and cuts bandwidth by 77.1% and energy by 52.3% compared to server-centric baselines. Crucially, it maintains accuracy within 2.2% of server-centric models, proving that adaptive, distributed learning is a viable path for the modern edge ecosystem.

</details>


### [64] [A Formal Semantics of C with OpenMP Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26527)
*Ke Du,Anshu Sharma,Liyi Li,William Mansky*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper presents a formal semantics for C code with OpenMP directives, built on CompCert's verified compiler, to detect errors introduced by incorrect OpenMP pragmas and guarantee data race freedom.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenMP pragmas can easily introduce logic errors and race conditions into otherwise correct programs, and existing OpenMP semantics have obscured subtle interactions between directives and variable state.

Method: Built on the C semantics of the CompCert verified compiler and its extension to concurrency to create a formal semantics capturing subtle interactions between OpenMP directives and variable state.

Result: A formal semantics that captures subtle interactions previously obscured, providing a foundation for detecting both logic errors and race conditions introduced by incorrect OpenMP annotations.

Conclusion: Provides a basis for detecting undesired behaviors introduced by incorrect annotations, guaranteeing successful executions are free of data races.

Abstract: OpenMP is a popular parallelization framework that lets users transform sequential code into parallel code with a few simple annotations. Unfortunately, it is also easy to inadvertently introduce errors by adding OpenMP pragmas into otherwise correct programs, including both logic errors and race conditions. We present a formal semantics for C code with OpenMP directives, building on the C semantics of the CompCert verified compiler and its extension to concurrency. Our semantics captures subtle interactions between OpenMP directives and variable state that have been obscured by previous OpenMP semantics, and provides a basis for detecting undesired behaviors introduced by incorrect annotations: in particular, any successful execution is guaranteed to be free of data races.

</details>


### [65] [Reducing Internal State in Eigenvalue-Only Divide-and-Conquer Tridiagonal Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26599)
*Ruiyi Zhan,Shaoshuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对对称特征值分解中的Divide and Conquer算法内存瓶颈问题，提出边界行D&C算法。通过观察到征服阶段仅需边界行/列而非完整特征向量矩阵，递归传播边界行，将空间复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，同时减少计算量。该方法兼具理论保证（正确性/稳定性证明）和实用价值（优化CPU/GPU实现），在性能和内存效率上优于现有QR和D&C方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Divide and Conquer算法在对称特征值分解中存在一个问题：征服阶段需要实例化或重放大型变换矩阵（即完整的特征向量矩阵），导致内存占用高且计算冗余。虽然D&C天然具有并行性，在现代多核CPU和GPU上很有吸引力，但现有特征值专用例程仍倾向于使用QR方法。

Method: 提出边界行D&C（Boundary-row Divide and Conquer）算法。核心创新在于：征服阶段仅需矩阵边界行/列而非完整的特征向量矩阵，通过递归直接传播边界行，将内存复杂度从二次降至线性，同时消除传统延迟重放方式中的不必要矩阵-向量运算。提供了算法的正确性和稳定性证明，并实现了优化的CPU和GPU版本。

Result: 内存需求从二次降低到线性；消除了传统延迟重放中的不必要矩阵-向量运算；在标准数值库的QR和D&C例程上取得性能提升；GPU实现相比现有方法也有显著加速。

Conclusion: 论文通过理论分析和大量实验验证了该方法的有效性，在保持数值稳定性的同时，显著降低了计算和存储开销。

Abstract: Divide and Conquer (D&C) is a widely used algorithmic strategy for symmetric eigenvalue decomposition. Its natural parallelism makes D&C attractive on modern multicore CPUs and GPUs, but existing eigenvalue-only routines often default to QR-based methods because conventional D&C still materializes or replays large transformation matrices during the conquer phase. This paper proposes a boundary-row D&C algorithm for eigenvalue-only computation. The key observation is that the conquer phase only needs selected boundary rows/columns rather than the full accumulated eigenvector matrix. By propagating these boundary rows directly through the recursion, the proposed algorithm reduces the memory requirement from quadratic to linear space while also eliminating unnecessary matrix-vector work in the conventional lazy-replay formulation. We provide the algorithm, its time and space complexity analysis, correctness and stability arguments, optimized CPU and GPU implementations, and an evaluation against QR and D&C routines in standard numerical libraries.

</details>


### [66] [Nonlinear spectral clustering with C++ GraphBLAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26975)
*Dimosthenis Pasadakis,Olaf Schenk,Verner Vlacic,Albert-Jan Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于p范数(p∈(1,2])的直接多路谱聚类算法的C++ GraphBLAS实现。该实现使用稀疏矩阵和稠密向量，通过共享内存并行方式处理大规模图数据。实验表明该算法在平衡图割指标上表现优异，能有效处理高达800万节点和4800万边的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nonlinear reformulations of spectral clustering offer increased numerical benefits and solid mathematical foundations, but the estimation of multiple nonlinear eigenvectors is computationally expensive. There is a need for efficient implementations that can handle large-scale problems.

Method: A direct multiway spectral clustering algorithm in p-norm (p∈(1,2]) implemented using a novel C++ GraphBLAS API. Key operations are expressed in linear algebraic terms and executed over sparse matrices and dense vectors, parameterized in the pertinent algebra. The algorithm is implemented in shared-memory parallel fashion.

Result: The implementation demonstrates effectiveness and accuracy on artificial test cases. Comparative results against competitive methods show the algorithm achieves high-quality clusters. Strong scaling is demonstrated on large datasets.

Conclusion: The proposed implementation achieves high-quality clusters in terms of balanced graph cut metric and demonstrates strong scaling capabilities on datasets with up to 8 million nodes and 48 million edges.

Abstract: Nonlinear reformulations of the spectral clustering method have gained a lot of recent attention due to their increased numerical benefits and their solid mathematical background. However, the estimation of the multiple nonlinear eigenvectors is associated with an increased computational cost. We present an implementation of a direct multiway spectral clustering algorithm in the $p$-norm, for $p\in(1,2]$, using a novel C++ GraphBLAS API. The key operations are expressed in linear algebraic terms and are executed over the resulting sparse matrices and dense vectors, parameterized in the algebra pertinent to the computation. We demonstrate the effectiveness and accuracy of our shared-memory algorithm on several artificial test cases. Our numerical examples and comparative results against competitive methods indicate that the proposed implementation attains high quality clusters in terms of the balanced graph cut metric. The strong scaling capabilities of our algorithm are showcased on a range of datasets with up to $8$ million nodes and $48$ million edges.

</details>
