### [67] [Decoupled Delay Compensation: Enhancing Pre-trained MARL Policies via Learned Dynamics Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26286)
*Maxim Mednikov,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体强化学习中通信延迟和陈旧观测问题，提出一种基于门控过渡模型与卡尔曼滤波的模块化状态估计层。该方法可作为预训练策略的即插即用组件，无需修改原有训练流程，在协调密集型和动态不稳定任务中展现出显著的鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多智能体强化学习系统常面临陈旧观测、随机通信延迟和数据包丢失等问题。在理想同步条件下训练的策略在面对这些非理想通信条件时往往性能显著下降，因为其基于过时的反馈进行决策。

Method: 提出一个模块化的执行阶段状态估计层，将延迟的通信观测替换为当前信念状态估计。该框架集成了学习型门控过渡模型（Gated Transition Model）与递归卡尔曼滤波层，利用异步测量值估计即时状态。

Result: 该方法在多种多智能体及连续控制基准测试中一致地提升了系统对通信延迟和消息丢失的鲁棒性。在协调密集型和动态不稳定任务中性能提升最为显著，这些任务对时间一致性要求较高。

Conclusion: 所提出的状态估计层具有高度模块化特性，可作为预训练策略的即插即用组件，无需修改原始MARL训练算法、架构或奖励结构。这为将理想条件下训练的策略部署到具有通信约束的真实多智能体系统中提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems must often operate under stale observations, stochastic communication delays, and intermittent packet loss. Policies trained under idealized synchronous conditions frequently exhibit significant performance degradation in these regimes because they act on outdated feedback. We propose a modular execution-stage state-estimation layer that replaces delayed communicated observations with current belief-state estimates. The framework integrates a learned Gated transition model with a recursive Kalman filtering layer to estimate instantaneous states from asynchronous measurements. A primary advantage of this approach is its modularity, The estimator serves as a plug-in for pre-trained policies, requiring no modifications to the original MARL training algorithm, architecture, or reward structure. Evaluation across diverse multi-agent and continuous-control benchmarks demonstrates that the proposed layer consistently enhances robustness to communication latency and message loss. The most significant performance gains are observed in coordination-intensive and dynamically unstable tasks where temporal consistency is critical for control.

</details>


### [68] [Constitutional Arms Races in the Public Goods Game: Co-Evolving LLM Constitutions Under Cooperation-Defection Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26448)
*Ujwal Kumar,Arth Singh,Hershraj Niranjani,Machiko Hirota,Takehiro Takayanagi,Alice Saito,Eiji Kamioka,Phan Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究证明LLM驱动的宪章共进化在多智能体对抗环境中可行，但需满足两个关键条件：采用耦合适应度函数（S_own - S_opp）和充足的评估预算（K≥5）。松耦合评分无法触发真实对抗性，且低评估预算会引发模式回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐方法多基于单智能体或合作假设，而前沿LLM智能体在对抗性场景中展现出黑箱、破坏和泄密等行为，暴露了现有方法的局限性。需探索多智能体对抗环境下自然语言宪章的共进化机制，特别是适应度函数是否真正产生对抗压力，以及LLM突变算子在对抗性专家目标下是否可靠。

Method: 采用LLM引导的进化搜索在两人群（Blue合作者 vs. Red搭便车者）中进行30代宪章共进化；分别在公共品博弈（PGG）和空间格子世界两种环境中评估；对比三种适应度函数：松耦合独立评分（independently scored）、S_own - S_opp差分目标、纯对抗目标；系统调控评估种子数K ∈ {2, 5}以观察模式回归现象。

Result: 1) PGG环境中Blue和Red双方收敛至近均衡状态（S≈0.78），在 multipliers m ∈ {1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0} 下均稳健；2) 松耦合独立评分下双方结果统计解耦（corr(S_B, S_R) = +0.088），无法产生对抗压力；改用S_own - S_opp适应度后对抗压力恢复；3) 纯对抗适应度下，K=2时发生模式回归（退化），K=5时强对抗专家策略维持全部30代。

Conclusion: 1) 对抗性宪章共进化是可行的，但需要耦合适应度函数和足够的评估预算；2) 松耦合评分机制无法产生对抗压力，仅当使用"S_own - S_opp"差分适应度时才恢复对抗性；3) 评估种子数K控制模式回归，K≥5才能维持强专家策略30代；4) 进化的Red宪章可作为可解释的红队测试工件。

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents engage in blackmail, sabotage, and document leaks under goal conflicts in agentic settings, exposing limitations of alignment methods built around single-agent or cooperative assumptions. Recent work shows LLM-guided evolutionary search can discover effective cooperative constitutions, but two properties of the adversarial setting remain uncharacterized: whether the fitness function actually induces adversarial pressure, and whether the LLM mutation operator behaves reliably under adversarial-specialist objectives. We study adversarial constitutional co-evolution (Blue cooperators vs. Red free-riders, 30 generations) across a Public Goods Game (PGG) and a spatial grid-world. Three findings: (1) in the PGG, both factions converge to a near-parity equilibrium at S approximately 0.78, robust across tested multipliers m in {1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0}; (2) in independently scored environments, per-faction scoring leaves outcomes statistically uncoupled, with corr(S_B, S_R) = +0.088, and produces no adversarial pressure; a score-advantage fitness target S_own - S_opp restores it; (3) under pure-adversary fitness, evaluation seed count K controls mode regression: K = 2 regresses, while K = 5 sustains a strong specialist for all 30 generations. Adversarial co-evolution of natural-language constitutions is feasible, but only under coupled fitness and adequate evaluation budget; the evolved Red constitutions serve as interpretable red-team artifacts for testing future cooperative designs.

</details>


### [69] [Persistent AI Agents in Academic Research: A Single-Investigator Implementation Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26870)
*Anas H. Alzahrani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper presents the first long-duration case study of a persistent LLM agent in real academic research, introducing the PARE-M measurement framework. Results show cache-dominant resource usage and highlight the need to shift evaluation metrics from token-based to artifact-based cost models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional LLM evaluation focuses on benchmarks or short conversations, but lacks understanding of persistent agentic systems embedded in real-world research settings with memory, tools, and protocols.

Method: A structured self-observed case study (Jan 31–May 25, 2026) was conducted on a persistent human-agent research environment. Analysis units included the agent runtime, memory layer, tools, repositories, scheduled jobs, specialized roles, and governance rules. The PARE-M framework was used to measure architecture, utilization, artifact production, resource use, reproducibility, and governance.

Result: Over 96 active days: 75,671 telemetry records, 8,059 user messages, 23,710 assistant messages, 502 memory files, 17 agent directories, 57 skill files, 579.7 active hours, 482 output-proxy events, 889 failure/correction events, 627 model-completed events, and 73.95M tokens (82.9% cache reads).

Conclusion: The persistent agentic workflow proved cache-dominant, indicating that evaluation metrics should shift from cost-per-token to cost-per-completed-artifact. Future research should adopt artifact-level denominators, reproducible parsing rules, correction taxonomies, and independent governance event coding.

Abstract: Background: Large language models are typically evaluated as models, benchmarks, or short conversational episodes. Less is known about what happens when an agent is embedded persistently in a real academic research environment with durable memory, local files, external tools, scheduled routines, delegated roles, and explicit safety protocols. Methods: A structured self-observed implementation case study was conducted from January 31 to May 25, 2026. The unit of analysis was the persistent human-agent environment: researcher, agent runtime, memory layer, tools, repositories, scheduled jobs, specialized agent roles, and governance rules. Outcomes were organized using PARE-M (Persistent Agentic Research Environment Measurement), a measurement framework covering architecture, utilization, artifact production, resource use, reproducibility, and governance. Results: Recoverable main-agent telemetry contained 75,671 de-duplicated records across 96 active days, with 8,059 user-role and 23,710 assistant-role messages. The workspace included 502 memory-related files, 17 configured agent directories, and 57 skill files. Active system time was 579.7 hours (30-minute capped-gap estimate). Memory-derived records identified 482 output-proxy events and 889 failure, verification, correction, or protocol-proxy events. A strict May 2026 trajectory subset captured 627 model-completed events and 73.95 million recorded tokens, of which 82.9% were cache reads. Conclusions: The workflow was cache-dominant, suggesting that persistent agentic environments may shift the economic unit from cost per token to cost per completed artifact. Future evaluations should use artifact-level denominators, reproducible parsing rules, correction taxonomies, and independent coding of governance events.

</details>


### [70] [Cost of Structural Learning Under Censored Feedback: A Threshold-Bandit Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27076)
*Michael Ledford,William Regli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究多智能体多臂老虎机中阈值触发任务的协调学习问题，提出集中式C-TAC和去中心化D-TAC算法，证实在截断反馈下可实现对数级遗憾，且去中心化方法能大幅降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体任务中，当协作联盟规模满足未知阈值时才获得奖励反馈，否则反馈被完全截断。此截断导致可辨识性问题：智能体无法区分随机失败与协调不足。

Method: 提出TAC-MAB形式化框架；设计集中式C-TAC算法（ regrets O(log T)），分解为结构性搜索项和统计监控项；开发去中心化D-TAC协议，采用事件触发通信，仅在结构信念变化时同步。

Result: 集中式C-TAC算法达到O(log T)的累积遗憾；去中心化D-TAC在保持可行性对齐的同时实现23倍通信量 reduction。

Conclusion: D-TAC实现23倍通信量 reduction，同时保持可行性对齐，证明了无需持续同步即可实现接近集中式的通信效率，揭示了截断反馈下学习的协调成本。

Abstract: In many multi-agent applications, tasks yield rewards only when executed by a coalition meeting an unknown size threshold; otherwise, feedback is fully censored. This censorship creates an identifiability problem: agents cannot distinguish stochastic failure from insufficient coordination. We formalize this setting as the Threshold-Activated Cooperative Multi-Armed Bandit (TAC-MAB) and analyze it under both centralized and decentralized coordination. We show that a centralized algorithm (C-TAC) achieves cumulative regret O(log T), decomposed into a structural-search term that captures the cost of resolving feasibility under censored feedback and a statistical-monitoring term for value estimation. We then introduce D-TAC, a decentralized event-triggered protocol in which agents synchronize only when their structural beliefs change. Empirically, D-TAC achieves a 23x reduction in communication relative to the centralized baseline while preserving feasibility alignment under conservative belief fusion. These results characterize the coordination cost of learning under censored feedback and show that near-centralized communication efficiency is achievable without continuous synchronization.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

### [71] [Where to Split and When to Charge: Optimal Route Construction from Customer Permutations in Electric Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26816)
*Leon Stjepan Uroić,Marko Đurasević*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文针对电动汽车路径问题中排列解码的内在依赖性挑战，提出固定排列分割与充电问题形式化模型，设计精确前向标记动态规划算法，并建立多层限制性解码变体进行系统比较，揭示解码策略简化与解质量之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于排列的元启发式算法在电动汽车路径问题中广泛应用，但排列本身不能直接定义可行车辆路径，需要通过路径分割和充电站插入决策将其解码。这些决策之间存在内在依赖性——每次返回仓库既分隔连续路径，又恢复车辆电池。然而，现有方法通常采用简化解码策略，可能牺牲解的质量和可行性。

Method: 提出一种精确前向标记算法，使用带支配剪枝的动态规划来构建固定客户排列的最小距离可行解码。进一步推导出三种限制性变体，代表逐步简化的解码策略：(1) 将路径分割与充电站插入分离；(2) 进一步限制每个客户间段最多访问一个充电站；(3) 精确联合解码。

Result: 最严格变体运行时间接近启发式解码器，但具有更高解码成功率和更好解质量。精确联合解码器可作为最优参考基准，揭示了常见解码简化的权衡取舍。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明：(1) 精确解码器在实践中具有可处理性；(2) 解码策略存在明显的层次结构；(3) 最严格变体在接近启发式解码器运行时间的同时，获得更高的解码成功率和更好的解质量；(4) 逐步放宽限制可进一步提升质量和鲁棒性，但需牺牲运行时间；(5) 精确联合解码器为每个固定排列提供最优参考，明确揭示了常见解码简化策略带来的权衡取舍。

Abstract: Permutation-based metaheuristics are widely used for electric vehicle routing, where candidate solutions are represented as ordered sequences of customers. Such sequences, however, do not directly define feasible vehicle routes: they must be decoded by choosing where to split the permutation into routes and where to insert charging-station visits, subject to cargo capacity and battery constraints. These decisions are inherently interdependent, since each return to the depot both separates consecutive routes and restores the vehicle battery. This paper formalizes the task as the Fixed-Permutation Splitting and Charging Problem and proposes an exact forward labeling algorithm that constructs a minimum-distance feasible decoding of a fixed customer permutation using dynamic programming with dominance pruning. We further derive restricted variants representing increasingly simplified decoding strategies: first separating route splitting from charging-station insertion, and then additionally limiting each inter-customer segment to at most one charging-station visit. Computational experiments on benchmark and randomly generated instances, including comparisons with heuristic decoders from the literature, confirm that the exact decoder remains tractable in practice and reveal a clear hierarchy among decoding strategies. The most restrictive variant achieves runtimes close to those of heuristic decoders while delivering substantially higher decoding success rates and better solution quality. Less restrictive variants further improve quality and robustness at the cost of additional runtime. The exact joint decoder provides the optimal reference for each fixed permutation, clarifying the trade-offs introduced by common decoding simplifications.

</details>


### [72] [Parsimonious Learning-Augmented Online Metric Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26886)
*Yongho Shin,Phanu Vajanopath*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: This paper extends learning-augmented algorithms to parsimonious settings for online metric matching, where only limited predictions are available. They modify the Follow-the-Prediction framework to use virtual predictions when needed and maintain quality throughout execution, achieving both theoretical lower bounds and empirical validation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The high computational cost of generating predictions in learning-augmented algorithms creates a need to study tradeoffs between performance guarantees and the number of predictions used. Existing work on caching and metrical task systems needs to be extended to online metric matching.

Method: Extended the Follow-the-Prediction framework to the parsimonious setting by: (1) using virtual predictions when actual predictions are unavailable, (2) employing an online metric matching algorithm that maintains good intermediate matchings throughout execution.

Result: Developed parsimonious learning-augmented algorithms with performance guarantees and lower bounds for online metric matching, with empirical evaluation demonstrating practical effectiveness.

Conclusion: The proposed parsimonious learning-augmented approach for online metric matching is both theoretically sound and practically effective.

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have received significant attention in recent years, particularly in the context of online optimization. Motivated by the high computational cost of generating predictions, a growing line of work studies the tradeoff between performance guarantees and the number of predictions used in learning-augmented algorithms for problems such as caching and metrical task systems. In this paper, we extend this line of research to online metric matching by developing parsimonious learning-augmented algorithms and establishing lower bounds on their performance. Our approach extends the Follow-the-Prediction framework to the parsimonious setting by filling in a virtual prediction in the absence of an actual prediction, using an online metric matching algorithm that maintains good intermediate matchings throughout its execution. We complement our theoretical results with an empirical evaluation, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [73] [Virtual-Memory Powersort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27147)
*Finn Moltmann,Tamio-Vesa Nakajima,Sebastian Wild*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出Virtual-Memory Powersort，通过内部缓冲技术在保持原有算法效率的前提下，将排序所需的缓冲区从n/2降低至O(√(n log n))，实验证明可实现开销极小的近乎原地稳定排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Powersort实现需要n/2个对象的存储空间，虽然已知原地归并算法可将空间降低至O(1)，但直接替换标准归并算法会导致显著的性能下降。

Method: 采用内部缓冲技术实现Virtual-Memory Powersort，保留了原有Powersort实现相同的移动和比较次数（至多附加O(n)项），同时显著降低缓冲区空间需求。

Result: 成功将缓冲区大小从n/2个对象降低至O(√(n log n))个对象，在保持比较次数和移动次数不变的情况下实现了空间效率的显著提升。

Conclusion: 实验研究表明，Virtual-Memory Powersort在多种场景下能以极小的开销实现近乎原地稳定排序，为内存受限环境下的高效排序提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We give a more space-efficient implementation of adaptive mergesort: Virtual-Memory Powersort. Using internal buffering techniques, we significantly reduce the memory consumption of the algorithm; specifically, for sorting $n$ objects the required buffer area is reduced from space for $n/2$ objects to $O(\sqrt{n \log n})$ objects. While this space-efficiency can be achieved (indeed reduced to $O(1)$) conceptually very easily with known inplace merging algorithms, using these as a drop-in replacement for the standard merge algorithm incurs a substantial slow-down. Virtual-Memory Powersort, by contrast, uses the same number of moves and comparisons as previous Powersort implementations up to an additive $O(n)$ term. We report on an empirical running-time study comparing our implementation against other Powersort variants and state-of-the-art stable sorting methods, demonstrating that almost in-place stable sorting can be achieved with negligible overhead in many scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AirCast-SR是一个大气超分辨率基础模型，将全球AI天气预报从28km降尺度至1km分辨率，每小时输出67小时8个耦合地表变量的预报。采用3D U-Net结合LCM扩散框架训练，在CONUS地区验证达到近零偏差，并能零样本迁移至印度和德国。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报（NWP）以公里尺度进行运营天气预测在计算上仍不可行，限制了需要细粒度时空细节的能源、农业和灾害管理应用获取预报的途径。

Method: 采用三维U-Net架构，在潜在一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架内进行条件化处理，基于patch-based样本训练，输入为GraphCast天气预报（约28km），目标为NOAA AORC数据分析。

Result: 在所有变量和预报时效上实现近零偏差；在10-100km波长范围保持细尺度大气结构（粗模型在此频段丢失谱功率）；跨越冬、夏、春三季的CONUS案例验证成功；零样本泛化至印度和德国（使用独立地面站观测，无需重训练或微调）。

Conclusion: AirCast-SR作为开放权重的基础模型，为公里级AI天气预报提供了新范式，可作为区域微调、知识蒸馏及气候服务和危险预报下游应用的平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [75] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SilIF，通过在隔离森林的表示空间中基于轮廓系数增强异常评分，在真实交易数据上验证有效，同时诚实报告了其在合成数据上无效的情况，为Isolation Forest提供了一种可调且易部署的增强方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标签稀缺的交易欺诈检测场景中，无监督异常检测广泛应用，Isolation Forest因可扩展性和易部署性成为流行方法，但存在改进空间。

Method: 提出SilIF方法：在隔离森林的树结构诱导的表示空间中，对每个点提取每棵树的路径长度向量作为"指纹"，聚类这些指纹形成结构组，计算轮廓系数衡量点与其组的拟合程度，最后将轮廓信号与基础IF分数通过超参数alpha组合。

Result: 在IEEE-CIS欺诈检测基准数据集上，SilIF（α=1.0）相比标准Isolation Forest平均提升AUC-PR 0.0080，5次种子实验全部获胜（配对t检验p=0.046）；在Sparkov合成信用卡数据集上未显示优势，并明确了区分两类结果的适用条件。

Conclusion: SilIF作为一种可调、易部署的Isolation Forest增强方法，在特定数据集上有效，但在合成数据集上效果不显著，表明其适用性取决于数据结构特征。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [76] [Neural Bayesian Sequential Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26147)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NBSR introduces a Bayesian framework that models neural inference as sequential evidence accumulation over a DAG, enabling uncertainty-aware, resource-rational decision-making with interpretable routing traces and theoretical guarantees.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard neural networks rely on static, dense forward computation with limited visibility into evidence acquisition, uncertainty evolution, or adaptive stopping criteria. Human decision-making, in contrast, is sequential and uncertainty-aware. The paper aims to bridge this gap by developing a framework that models neural inference as active evidence accumulation.

Method: NBSR models neural inference as active evidence accumulation over a hierarchical DAG. It uses a Dirichlet-Categorical conjugate framework where neural experts query a persistent global knowledge oracle to extract positive evidence vectors (pseudo-counts) that update Dirichlet belief states via exact conjugate addition. A Gumbel-Softmax Straight-Through estimator enables hard, path-dependent routing while preserving surrogate gradients for end-to-end training.

Result: Empirical evaluations across visual categorization, structured medical diagnosis, language modeling, partially observable control, and cost-aware Bayesian experimental design demonstrate competitive predictive performance. The framework also provides uncertainty quantification, entropy-based early exiting, OOD abstention, and cost-aware evidence acquisition.

Conclusion: NBSR provides a mathematically grounded framework for interpretable, modular, and resource-rational agentic AI. It enables transparent routing traces, path-dependent evidence attribution, uncertainty-aware decision control, and cost-aware inference, with theoretical guarantees on precision monotonicity and Bayes-optimality under idealized conditions.

Abstract: Human decision-making is sequential and uncertainty-aware, yet standard neural networks often rely on static, dense forward computation with limited visibility into evidence acquisition, uncertainty evolution, or when computation should stop. We introduce \textbf{Neural Bayesian Sequential Routing (NBSR)}, a framework that models neural inference as active evidence accumulation over a hierarchical Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). Within a Dirichlet--Categorical conjugate framework, neural experts query a persistent global knowledge oracle to extract positive evidence vectors, which act as pseudo-counts and update a Dirichlet belief state by exact conjugate addition. Coupled with a Gumbel-Softmax Straight-Through estimator, this update enables hard, path-dependent routing while preserving surrogate gradients for end-to-end training. The resulting Dirichlet precision and entropy provide mechanisms for uncertainty quantification, entropy-based early exiting, OOD abstention, and cost-aware evidence acquisition. We prove that, under strictly positive evidence extraction, total Dirichlet precision increases monotonically along any valid trajectory and marginal predictive variance is bounded, formalizing sequential ``hypothesis sharpening''; under idealized capacity and optimization assumptions, the terminal Dirichlet expectation recovers the Bayes-optimal conditional distribution. Empirical evaluations across visual categorization, structured medical diagnosis, language modeling, partially observable control, and cost-aware Bayesian experimental design show that NBSR achieves competitive predictive performance while providing transparent routing traces, path-dependent evidence attribution, uncertainty-aware decision control, and resource-rational inference. Overall, NBSR offers a mathematically grounded framework for interpretable, modular, and resource-rational agentic AI.

</details>


### [77] [TSFMAudit: Data Contamination Auditing in Forecasting Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26161)
*Hongkai Li,Shifeng Xie,Lefei Shen,Zhuo Li,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaobin Zhang,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: First work on pretraining contamination auditing for time series foundation models (TSFMs). Proposes TSFMAudit using probe adaptation dynamics to detect if evaluation datasets were in pretraining corpus based on unusual adaptation efficiency. Evaluated on 187 datasets across 6 TSFMs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As time series foundation models are pretrained on large corpora, evaluation datasets may have been exposed during pretraining, leading to overly optimistic performance estimates. However, auditing such contamination in time series is challenging due to continuous/heterogeneous signals and lack of corpus documentation.

Method: TSFMAudit is based on probe adaptation dynamics, leveraging the intuition that contaminated datasets show unusually efficient adaptation—faster loss reduction with smaller backbone movement during fine-tuning.

Result: TSFMAudit is evaluated on 6 TSFMs and 187 datasets, demonstrating effectiveness compared to 10 competitive baselines adapted from LLM literature.

Conclusion: TSFMAudit outperforms 10 baseline methods, with the advantage of not requiring corpus documentation, making it practical for auditing real-world TSFMs.

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly pretrained on large corpora, raising concerns that evaluation datasets may have been exposed during pretraining and thus yield overly optimistic performance estimates. Auditing such contamination is challenging in time series because signals are continuous and heterogeneous, and often lack corpus documentation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs. We formalize the problem of pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs and propose TSFMAudit, a method based on probe adaptation dynamics. Our key intuition is that contamination manifests as unusually efficient adaptation: after a fine tuning probe, contaminated datasets tend to exhibit faster loss reduction with smaller backbone movement. We evaluate TSFMAudit on 6 TSFMs and 187 datasets using documented training source evidence as supervision, and compare against 10 competitive baselines adapted from the LLM literature.

</details>


### [78] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异步去中心化联邦学习中通信开销大、聚合偏差及模型漂移问题，提出PushCen-ADFL框架，通过质心空间耦合通信-聚合-稳定化、推-和混合校正偏差及质心正则化抑制漂移，实现精度提升6%且通信成本降低80%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步去中心化联邦学习(ADFL)虽具可扩展优势，但频繁点对点通信、有向拓扑异步更新与非IID数据共同导致通信开销过大、聚合偏差严重及模型漂移剧烈，亟需通信高效且训练稳定的解决方案。

Method: 提出PushCen-ADFL框架，核心包括：(1)质心形式消息交换机制，将模型压缩与优化形成闭环；(2)平均保真推-和混合(APS)算法，校正有向拓扑下的聚合偏差；(3)轻量级质心正则化，在统一质心空间锚定本地模型以抑制异构数据和延迟参与下的漂移；(4)有界去重发送缓冲机制，提升异步到达鲁棒性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和EMNIST数据集上的实验表明，相比基准方法，PushCen-ADFL在数据异构场景下精度提升高达6%，同时每轮通信成本降低超过80%，实现了精度与通信效率的优异权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL通过共享质心表示空间将通信、聚合与局部稳定化耦合，利用推-和混合校正聚合偏差，采用轻量级质心正则化缓解异构性与延迟导致的模型漂移，在CIFAR-10/100和EMNIST数据集上实现了通信效率与模型精度的双重优化。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [79] [When Rule Violations Are Rare: Chimera Training for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26171)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经规则评估器，用于检测违反语义约束的异常。通过将逻辑规则编译为有向无环图，并利用特征感知的子树MLP门控模块学习规则满意度概率。为解决训练数据中真实违规样本稀缺的问题，引入"嵌合体训练"——在特征层面通过拼接不同样本的子树特征构造反事实样本。实验表明该方法在CLEVRER、OpenImages和VidOR数据集上显著提升了组合型和关系型规则的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs but violations of semantic constraints (object co-occurrence, action preconditions, temporal/relational regularities). The key challenge is that real rule violations are rare or absent during training, making it difficult to learn anomaly detection from insufficient truth configurations and avoid shortcut solutions.

Method: The paper proposes a neural rule evaluator that compiles logical constraints into DAGs with feature-aware subtree MLP gates. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to parent representations and rule-satisfaction probabilities, using exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. To address insufficient training data coverage and shortcut solutions, the method introduces chimera training: counterfactual construction at the feature level by concatenating subtree features from different samples, inheriting their truth labels and applying logical operators to derive chimera targets.

Result: Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR datasets, the evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules.

Conclusion: The proposed neural rule evaluator with chimera training effectively addresses the challenge of learning anomaly detection from rare/absent rule violations. It achieves improved rule-level anomaly AUROC across multiple datasets, particularly for compositional and relational rules. The method provides both scalar anomaly scores and interpretable rule-level attributions.

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [80] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度分析量化误差，指出量化友好的激活值应同时具有较小数值范围和足够分散性。基于此提出InfoQuant方法，通过峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）优化激活分布，配合自适应异常值令牌选择提升鲁棒性，在多个LLM上实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是大语言模型高效部署的主要瓶颈，原因不仅是激活值包含异常值，更重要的是其分布与低比特均匀量化器不匹配。现有后训练量化方法抑制峰值、平衡通道或最小化重构误差，但未明确指定何种激活分布易于离散化，导致激活值表面看起来更平滑但量化误差仍然较大。

Method: 提出InfoQuant方法，采用峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活值塑造成量化友好分布，并引入自适应异常值令牌选择以提高PSOT优化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上，InfoQuant一致优于之前的后训练量化（PTQ）和端到端训练基线方法。在W4A4KV4配置下，平均保留97%浮点精度，将LLaMA-2 13B的性能差距比之前最优方法降低42%。

Conclusion: InfoQuant通过PSOT正交变换和自适应异常值令牌选择策略，将激活值塑造成量化友好的分布，在W4A4KV4配置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，并将LLaMA-2 13B的性能差距比之前最优方法降低42%。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [81] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAC，一个用于混合后训练的自适应噪声感知控制器，通过在线估计梯度方差和信号分歧度动态调整SFT与RL的混合权重，在多种任务和模型规模上实现一致提升，计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合后训练结合监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL），但固定混合策略无法适应训练过程中两个信号相对噪声的动态变化，导致训练效率受限。

Method: GAC采用噪声感知控制器设计，基于梯度方差和两个训练信号之间的分歧程度，在线估计并推导自适应混合权重。具体实现包含平滑机制、先验引导和受限更新，并复用了现有训练张量，最大限度降低实现复杂度。

Result: 在数学、代码、科学和逻辑等多种基准测试中，GAC持续优于强固定基线和基于规则的基线，且在更大模型规模上收益更显著，训练开销不足1%。

Conclusion: GAC通过自适应调整混合权重，有效解决了混合后训练中固定调度无法适应噪声变化的根本问题。在多种任务和模型规模上展现出一致的改进效果，且计算开销极小（<1%），具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [82] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对HiF8 W8A8量化感知训练中两类隐性失效模式（amax饱和与灾难性遗忘）进行系统诊断，提出保守DTS策略与BF16预热相结合的解决方案，实现精度损失控制在0.5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特浮点格式量化感知训练（HiF8 W8A8 QAT）在高效部署LLM时存在隐性失效模式，这些问题无法被标准训练指标检测到。

Method: 通过8组对照实验，系统分离两类失效模式；采用保守DTS策略（64步历史窗口）和BF16 500步预热结合QAT（lr=10^{-5}）的复合方案。

Result: 成功将MMLU、HellaSwag和ARC-Challenge的精度下降分别控制在0.43%、0.58%和0.22%，训练损失APE为0.11%。

Conclusion: 结合两种策略（64步保守DTS和500步BF16预热），最终配置将MMLU下降控制在0.43%，HellaSwag下降0.58%，ARC-Challenge下降0.22%，训练损失APE仅0.11%，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [83] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DelayMix框架，将时序数据流建模为延迟系统的动态混合，通过马尔可夫参数张量总结历史状态，实现快速适应环境变化同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序数据流中的系统变化（制度转移）和输入延迟变化会显著降低模型性能，多个小模型组合时存在准确性、鲁棒性和内存使用的权衡问题。

Method: 将流式时序数据建模为延迟系统的动态混合；利用马尔可夫参数序列构建摘要系统张量，捕捉系统动态和输入输出延迟特性；通过张量分解算法提取相关历史模型，选择最适配当前制度的系统。

Result: DelayMix在真实数据集上一致优于对比方法，实现了更高的预测精度和更快的延迟适应能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效应对非平稳环境的快速变化，在保持低内存消耗的同时实现鲁棒跟踪。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [84] [When Does Deep RL Beat Calibrated Baselines? A Benchmark Study on Adaptive Resource Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26418)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Kai Zhao,Shahryar Sarkani,John Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究证明经过properly calibrated的规则基础自动扩缩器在成本上可超越所有6种主流DRL算法，核心发现是离散动作算法优于连续动作算法，且瓶颈在于基线校准和奖励工程而非算法选择本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究问题：当DRL在自适应资源控制中何时真正优于规则基础的自动扩缩器？挑战DRL总是更好的常见假设。

Method: 在Kubernetes HPA上构建RLScale-Bench基准测试，对比6种DRL算法(PPO/DQN/A2C/SAC/TD3/DDPG)与校准规则基线，涵盖6种工作负载模式和5个随机种子(共240次实验)，并测试分布偏移泛化能力。

Result: 三个颠覆性发现：①校准控制器在所有工作负载上成本最低，但bursty/flash流量场景略逊于最佳RL智能体；②离散动作算法约束违规率比连续动作算法低1-2个数量级(存在动作空间不匹配问题)；③无单一算法在所有工作负载上占优，排名差异可达4位。

Conclusion: DRL资源控制的关键瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励函数设计和更现实的评估协议。

Abstract: A properly calibrated rule-based autoscaler can beat every one of six mainstream deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms on cost across every workload we test - so when, if ever, does DRL actually help? We study this in RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evaluation protocol for DRL on adaptive resource control, where an agent allocates compute to a dynamic workload under cost and service-level constraints. We evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG under matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions against a calibrated rule-based baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), instantiate the benchmark on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling, and probe distribution-shift generalization. Three findings challenge common assumptions: (i) the calibrated controller achieves the lowest cost on all six workloads, though it trails the best RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; (ii) discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by one to two orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and (iii) no single algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions. The bottleneck in RL-based resource control is not algorithm selection but baseline calibration, reward engineering, and realistic evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [85] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMS-Fold通过将结构蛋白质组学数据（XL-MS和HDX-MS）与预训练扩散模型相结合，开发了一种推理时引导扩散框架，成功提升了诱导接近靶点的构象预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构生成模型虽能预测单体蛋白结构，但无法正确捕获蛋白质复合物的构象状态，这对抗体和PROTAC等诱导接近模态的蛋白设计至关重要。

Method: 提出AIMS-Fold，一个推理时引导扩散框架，通过可微分物理势能主动引导生成采样轨迹，融合XL-MS空间约束和HDX-MS溶剂可及性图谱。

Result: XL-MS和HDX-MS方法单独使用均能提升预测精度，集成后产生协同改善效果；在诱导接近靶点上，AIMS-Fold精度优于未受指导的SOTA模型Boltz-2。

Conclusion: AIMS-Fold establishes an effective integrative computational framework for structure-based drug design of induced proximity drugs, outperforming state-of-the-art unguided models like Boltz-2 on challenging targets.

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [86] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoAD框架，通过协同设计统一分类与重建范式，有效解决时间序列异常检测中细微复杂异常的检测难题，性能与效率均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在检测细微和长期异常时表现不佳，基于OE的方法泛化能力差，基于MAE的方法存在掩码错位问题。

Method: 提出CoAD框架，统一Outlier Exposure（分类范式）和Masked Autoencoder（重建范式）两大范式。分类模块生成概率驱动的软掩码引导重建模块，同时解决分类粒度不当和频率信息忽视的问题。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，CoAD显著优于现有最先进的深度学习和传统数据挖掘方法，且更轻量、推理速度更快。

Conclusion: CoAD通过统一分类与重建范式，展现了深度学习在时间序列异常检测中的潜力，具有轻量级、高速度的特点，适用于大规模实时应用场景。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [87] [ReMoE: Boosting Expert Reuse through Router Fine-Tuning in Memory-Constrained MoE LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27081)
*Xiongwei Zhu,Xiaojian Liao,Tianyang Jiang,Yusen Zhang,Liang Wang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMoE是一种针对MoE模型路由器的微调框架，通过偏置路由决策倾向于近期选中的专家，增强时间局部性以提升专家缓存命中率，从而减少内存受限场景下的存储I/O开销和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的推理场景中，MoE模型面临专家缓存与I/O开销的矛盾：只有少量专家可缓存在GPU内存中，非缓存专家需从外部存储获取，导致频繁驱逐和高I/O开销。传统MoE的稀疏激活特性虽降低计算量，但与缓存局部性存在冲突。

Method: ReMoE通过路由器微调框架，偏置路由器倾向于选择最近被激活的专家，从而产生时间上稳定的路由模式。该方法在训练阶段引导路由决策，不引入推理时的额外计算开销。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen模型上，ReMoE实现专家复用率提升26%，同时保持下游任务性能。真实系统评估显示：vLLM GPU-CPU专家卸载下吞吐量提升8.4%；llama.cpp在Jetson Orin NX上TPOT降低43.6-49.8%，解码速度提升1.77-1.99倍。

Conclusion: ReMoE successfully bridges the gap between sparse MoE routing and memory constraints, providing a practical solution for edge deployment. The framework demonstrates consistent gains across different hardware platforms (vLLM GPU-CPU offloading and Jetson Orin NX with llama.cpp), confirming its real-world applicability.

Abstract: Fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models sparsely activate only a subset of experts per token, reducing activated computation while maintaining high model capacity. However, in memory-constrained inference scenarios, only a small set of experts can be cached. Experts not in the cache must be fetched from slow external storage (e.g., UFS), leading to frequent evictions and substantial I/O overhead.
  We propose ReMoE, a router fine-tuning framework designed to boost token-wise expert reuse. ReMoE biases the router toward recently selected experts, producing temporally stable routing that better matches cache locality constraints. By increasing short-horizon expert reuse, ReMoE reduces expert fetches from storage without adding inference-time computation.
  Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen models show that ReMoE improves expert reuse by 26% while maintaining downstream task performance. Real-system evaluations further confirm these benefits, improving output throughput by 8.4% under vLLM GPU-CPU expert offloading and reducing TPOT by 43.6-49.8% under llama.cpp on Jetson Orin NX, corresponding to a 1.77-1.99$\times$ decode speedup across diverse workloads. Checkpoints and usage instructions are available at https://github.com/BUAA-OSCAR/ReMoE.

</details>


### [88] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathoFM是一种用于脊髓损伤（SCI）病理步态分析的基础模型，通过三种互补的预训练目标（局部补全、时序连续性、上下文动态）研究时序表征学习的迁移问题。实证表明，动态中心的混合目标能产生最均衡的跨任务迁移效果，最佳方案是结合局部重建与时序连续性，并在可行时加入上下文条件化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时序学习面临小型异质队列和协议漂移的约束，且下游任务涵盖分类（如病理诊断）和回归（如时序预测）两类。基础模型预训练虽具吸引力，但如何设计归纳偏置以使表征在任务类型和被试间有效迁移是关键问题。

Method: PathoFM：一种以编码器为中心的Transformer架构，采用三种互补的预训练目标——局部补全（重建连续掩码区间以强制局部结构）、时序连续性（从观察前缀预测掩码的中期延续以强制平滑性和因果一致性）以及无监督上下文动态（通过注意力机制基于被试示例窗口进行支持-查询重建）。通过encoder-centric架构设计实现跨任务迁移。

Result: 动态中心的混合目标产生最均衡的迁移效果：分组/对比目标有利于判别性边界但会损害连续目标所需的幅度保真度；而仅重建目标虽能保留波形结构但在分类任务上可能表现不足。

Conclusion: 结合局部重建与时序连续性，并在样本获取可行时加入上下文条件化，能产生最具鲁棒性的跨被试泛化表示。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [89] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示hard/soft标签混合优于单独使用的本质原因——减少暴露偏差而非更好的教师匹配；提出Bridge-Garden Decomposition理论解释该现象，并基于此开发自适应混合监督方法，在模型压缩任务中实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏中，soft标签看似信息更丰富，但单独使用hard或soft标签都存在局限：hard-only避免风险但缺乏多样性，soft-only保持多样性但可能偏离关键节点。

Method: 提出Bridge-Garden Decomposition理论，将生成步骤分为必须精确的Bridges和可灵活的Gardens；开发自适应平衡hard和soft标签的混合监督方法。

Result: 混合策略同时处理Bridges和Gardens，降低了序列级别的暴露偏差；在7个师生模型对（Qwen、Llama、Gemma、DeepSeek）的推理和编码基准测试中优于基于散度的on-policy KD基线，训练效率提升9.7倍。

Conclusion: Bridge-Garden Decomposition理论揭示了hard/soft标签混合优于单独使用的根本原因；基于此理论提出的混合监督方法在7个师生模型对和推理/编码任务上显著优于基线，同时将训练成本降低9.7倍。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [90] [Unified Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26248)
*Ethan Caballero,Priyank Jaini,David Krueger,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper introduces a Unified Neural Scaling Law (UNSL) — a functional form that jointly models scaling behaviors of neural networks across multiple simultaneously varying dimensions (parameters, data, compute, steps, hyperparameters) — and demonstrates its superior extrapolation accuracy over existing scaling law approaches on large-scale vision, language, math, and RL tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing neural scaling laws typically model scaling behavior with respect to only one or a few variables at a time. In practice, however, multiple dimensions vary simultaneously during training and deployment, and a unified framework is needed to accurately capture and extrapolate these complex interactions.

Method: The authors propose a unified functional form that jointly models how evaluation metrics vary as multiple dimensions (model parameters, dataset size, training steps, inference steps, compute, and hyperparameters) are varied simultaneously across diverse architectures and tasks.

Result: The UNSL functional form yields considerably more accurate extrapolations of scaling behavior compared to other functional forms tested across large-scale vision, language, math, and reinforcement learning tasks.

Conclusion: The proposed Unified Neural Scaling Law (UNSL) provides a more accurate functional form for extrapolating scaling behaviors compared to existing approaches, applicable across diverse architectures and task domains at large scale.

Abstract: We present a functional form (that we refer to as a Unified Neural Scaling Law (UNSL)) that accurately models and extrapolates the scaling behaviors of deep neural networks as multiple dimensions all vary simultaneously (i.e. how the evaluation metric of interest varies as one simultaneously varies the number of model parameters, training dataset size, number of training steps, number of inference steps, amount of compute, and various hyperparameters) for various architectures and for each of various tasks within a varied set of upstream and downstream tasks. This set includes large-scale vision, language, math, and reinforcement learning. When compared to other functional forms for neural scaling, this functional form yields extrapolations of scaling behavior that are considerably more accurate on this set.

</details>


### [91] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV cache quantization in video diffusion models introduces a systematic bias in attention weights (Jensen bias) due to quantization noise inflating cached key contributions. The paper derives a per-attention-score correction using second-order Taylor approximation that removes this bias with negligible overhead, recovering near-BF16 quality at INT2 quantization while saving 50% memory vs INT4.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-form video generation with chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models suffers from KV cache memory bottleneck. Existing low bitwidth KV cache quantization methods degrade video quality, limiting their practical applicability.

Method: A per-attention-score correction derived from quantization step sizes and query norm, using a second-order Taylor approximation for on-the-fly computation. No additional memory beyond the existing cache.

Result: At INT2 quantization, the correction recovers most quality lost to aggressive quantization, approaching near-BF16 video quality. It can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

Conclusion: The Jensen bias correction effectively recovers quality lost to aggressive KV cache quantization, achieving near-BF16 quality at INT2 while potentially outperforming INT4 with 50% less memory. The method incurs negligible computational overhead and requires no additional memory.

Abstract: Chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models rely on a KV cache of previously generated chunks to avoid redundant computation, but this cache quickly becomes a memory bottleneck as videos grow longer. Methods that quantize the KV cache to low bitwidths reduce memory pressure but degrade video quality. We show that a key driver of this degradation is a systematic bias in attention weights: due to the convexity of the exponential in softmax attention, quantization noise inflates the contribution of cached keys, a phenomenon we call the Jensen bias. This effect causes quantized keys to steal attention mass from the unquantized current chunk. We derive a per-attention-score correction that removes this bias in expectation, computed on the fly from the quantization step sizes of the cached keys and the query norm. Using a second-order Taylor approximation, the additional computational overhead is negligible, and no additional memory is needed alongside the cache. Evaluated on MAGI-1, SkyReels-V2, and HY-WorldPlay at INT2 quantization, our correction recovers most of the quality lost to aggressive quantization, reaching near-BF16 video quality, and can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

</details>


### [92] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [93] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于两参数流的概率密度动态学习方法，无需轨迹标签即可在高维环境下学习物理时间动力学，并能自然处理旋转或循环等非梯度物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维概率密度动态学习问题，传统方法依赖标记轨迹数据，但实际场景中常缺乏轨迹信息仅有未标记样本。

Method: 提出两参数流（two-parameter flows）方法：(1) 学习从基分布到各边缘分布的采样时间传输；(2) 通过回归耦合合成轨迹提取物理时间速度场。构建于条件流匹配技术之上。

Result: 证明物理时间动力学唯一性，且继承采样时间传输的正则性；在高维场景可扩展，同时支持非梯度动力学。

Conclusion: 通过条件流匹配技术实现高维概率密度动态学习，避免逐步最优传输耦合，支持非梯度动力学（如旋转/循环现象），同时保证物理时间动力学的唯一性和规律性继承。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

</details>


### [94] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种状态化推理架构，将多智能体工具调用场景中每个轮次的O(n_t)成本降至O(Δ_t)增量成本，通过跨轮次持久化KV缓存、基数前缀缓存和提示查找推测解码器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理框架将每次工具调用视为独立请求，即使85-95%的提示内容在前一轮保持不变，仍从头重新处理整个对话，造成大量计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出状态化推理架构：(1) 持久化KV缓存跨轮次保持，仅摄入新增token推进状态；(2) 基数前缀缓存扩展支持交错多智能体流量场景；(3) 提示查找推测解码器加速结构化输出。

Result: 在全新生成的工作负载上，相比vLLM和SGLang：6轮智能体工作流提速2.1倍；35轮工作流中位轮次提速4.2倍；端到端wall time减半。性能优势源于状态化复用和推测执行，而非传统缓存机制。

Conclusion: 状态化推理架构能有效解决多智能体工具调用场景中的重复计算问题，通过增量处理机制实现显著的性能提升，为LLM系统的实际部署提供了高效的推理范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

</details>


### [95] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes Staged-Competence, a curriculum learning framework to improve DPO-based safety alignment robustness. By organizing preference data by difficulty, using competence-based sampling, and progressively updating the reference model, the method achieves significantly better OOD generalization—reducing harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak success rates by 20%—while being data-efficient (75% of training data) and extensible to other DPO variants.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO is widely used for safety alignment in LLMs but is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization.

Method: Staged-Competence: a curriculum-based framework with three key mechanisms—(1) organizing preference data by difficulty, (2) competence-based sampling, and (3) progressive reference model updates during training.

Result: Across three model families: (1) reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16%, (2) reduces jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, (3) preserves general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal, (4) achieves baseline-level safety with only 75% of training data, (5) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses.

Conclusion: Staged-Competence is agnostic to policy optimisation loss and extensible to other DPO variants and alignment domains, with code and data publicly available.

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

</details>


### [96] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

</details>


### [97] [Reparametrizing Shampoo and SOAP for Subspace Basis Updates and BFloat16 Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26327)
*Alan Milligan,Zikun Xu,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对Shampoo类优化器在高精度要求和BFP16存储下性能下降的问题，提出了一种预条件器重参数化方法，通过子空间部分QR更新在保持性能的同时提高内存和时间效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shampoo类方法（KL-Shampoo、SOAP等）虽性能优异，但依赖QR分解，存在计算开销大、内存占用高的问题。现有QR实现需要FP32精度，当预条件器矩阵较大时导致时间和内存成本高，而使用BFP16存储虽能减少内存但会降低性能。

Method: 提出了预条件器的重参数化方法：支持BFP16存储；通过结合更新的基向量与未更新的基向量形成完整基；仅通过子空间中的QR分解更新部分基向量。

Result: 在BFP16存储下提升了SOAP和KL-SOAP的性能，使KL-SOAP能达到或超越KL-Shampoo的效果，同时降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: 该方法使Shampoo类方法更加高效、实用，且可广泛应用于所有使用QR分解的Shampoo变体。

Abstract: Shampoo-based methods, such as KL-Shampoo and SOAP, have demonstrated strong performance in training neural networks and rely on QR decomposition. Because existing QR implementations require single-precision (FP32) arithmetic and remain computationally expensive, these methods become time- and memory-intensive when their preconditioning matrices are large. Moreover, using BFloat16 (BFP16) storage to reduce memory usage can degrade the performance of Shampoo-based methods. We propose a reparametrization of the preconditioner that supports BFP16 storage and forms a complete basis by combining updated basis vectors with unchanged ones. By updating only part of the basis through QR decomposition in a subspace, our approach reduces computational overhead while mitigating the performance degradation caused by BFP16 storage. Our approach applies broadly to Shampoo-based methods that employ QR decomposition, including KL-Shampoo, SOAP, and KL-SOAP. In particular, it improves the performance of SOAP and KL-SOAP under BFP16 storage, enabling KL-SOAP to match or exceed KL-Shampoo. Overall, our approach makes Shampoo-based methods more memory- and time-efficient.

</details>


### [98] [QAM-W: Joint 2D Codebook Quantization for LLM Weights via Hadamard Rotation and Activation-Aware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26339)
*Preetam Sharma,Kacper Dobek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAM-W引入块Hadamard旋转与2D联合编码，恢复权重行内坐标结构，在5-6 bpw质量保持区间内达到FP16 perplexity的±0.4%误差，较SmoothQuant W8A8节省32%权重比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows, leading to suboptimal compression.

Method: Block-Hadamard rotation + 2D coordinate pairing + Lloyd-Max codebook on circular Gaussian + activation-aware per-channel scaling

Result: At ~5.5 bpw: ±0.4% BF16 perplexity deviation; matches SmoothQuant W8A8 at 32% fewer bits; 2D coding outperforms polar by 2-15pp; KL-BF16 correlation ρ=0.99

Conclusion: QAM-W establishes a quality-preserving 5-6 bpw band, achieving SmoothQuant W8A8 quality at 32% fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding with activation-aware scaling enables consistent perplexity within ±0.4% of FP16 across diverse architectures.

Abstract: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight quantized configurations, the activation-aware variant at $\approx 5.5$ bpw stays within $\pm 0.4\%$ of BF16 WikiText-2 perplexity on every model, matching the SmoothQuant W8A8 quality envelope at $32\%$ fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding outperforms polar (amplitude $\times$ phase) coding by 2--15~pp $Δ$PPL at equal bitrate, and paired KL against BF16 tracks $Δ$PPL\% at Spearman $ρ= 0.99$ across 37 (method, model) rows, consistent with a monotone composite bound from codec distortion to KL divergence. A 3.5~bpw variant is competitive on quantization-tolerant architectures. At strict 4~bpw, the rotated-codebook frontier method QTIP outperforms QAM-W; the contribution is the quality-preserving 5--6~bpw band.

</details>


### [99] [A PAC-Bayesian View of Generalisation for Physics-Informed Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26341)
*Thien V. Nguyen,Amaury Habrard,Benjamin Guedj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Paper develops a PAC-Bayesian framework for physics-informed machine learning that provides high-probability generalization guarantees with unbounded losses, using a multi-task perspective that jointly treats data fidelity, PDE residuals, and boundary conditions. The derived bounds scale with input-gradient norms, revealing a direct link between physical regularity and generalization. Includes practical self-bounding-aware learning algorithm.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing analyses of physics-informed machine learning rely on approximation or stability arguments and do not fully capture how physical structure influences generalization from finite data, especially in regression settings with unbounded losses.

Method: Developed a PAC-Bayesian framework with multi-task perspective, deriving bounds where complexity scales with input-gradient norms of losses. Instantiated under Sobolev and Poincaré assumptions, yielding two classes of bounds.

Result: Bounds are non-vacuous, significantly tighter than union-bound baselines, and can be effectively minimized during training on standard PDE benchmarks.

Conclusion: Results provide a principled statistical foundation for understanding generalization in physics-informed models, with practical algorithms for optimizing generalization bounds during training.

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) integrates mechanistic knowledge, typically in the form of partial differential equations (PDE), into data-driven models. Despite strong empirical performance, its statistical generalisation properties remain poorly understood, particularly in the regression setting with unbounded losses. Existing analyses rely on approximation or stability arguments and do not fully capture how physical structure influences generalisation from finite data. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for PIML that provides high-probability generalisation guarantees in the presence of unbounded losses. We adopt a multi-task perspective that jointly treats data fidelity, PDE residuals, initial and boundary conditions, avoiding the looseness induced by standard union-bound approaches. Our analysis leverages the structure of physics-informed objectives to derive novel bounds where the complexity scales with input-gradient norms of the losses, revealing a direct link between physical regularity and generalisation. We instantiate this framework under Sobolev and Poincaré-type assumptions, yielding two classes of bounds that trade off statistical complexity and smoothness in different regimes. Building on these results, we propose a self-bounding-aware learning algorithm that directly optimises tractable surrogates of the derived bounds, along with a practical procedure to estimate the associated constants in realistic settings. Empirical evaluations on standard PDE benchmarks demonstrate that our bounds are non-vacuous, significantly tighter than union-bound baselines, and can be effectively minimised during training. Overall, our results provide a principled statistical foundation for the generalisation of physics-informed models.

</details>


### [100] [MechRL: Reinforcement Learning Agents Perform Circuit Discovery for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26343)
*Barsat Khadka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has identified small sets of attention heads that implement specific behaviours in transformer language models, but recovering these circuits typically requires a bespoke analytical pipeline for each new task. We recast circuit discovery as a reinforcement-learning problem. An agent operates over the 144 attention heads of GPT-2 small as a discrete action space; each action triggers a zero-ablation and a contrastive reward that subtracts the ablation's damage to general next-token prediction from its damage to the target task. A single PPO policy, trained on two tasks (induction and IOI) in a vectorised multi-task environment, attains the per-episode oracle on both training tasks and on a held-out third task (docstring completion). Its preferred heads coincide with the canonical heads of established literature on precisely the axes those papers identify as causally non-redundant under single-head ablation; the categories they identify as redundant are correctly de-prioritised by the agent.
