On the held-out task, best-of-five planning recovers 96\% of the oracle ceiling with no task signal supplied at evaluation. These results indicate that reinforcement learning over causal interventions is a viable, transferable substrate for identifying the single-head bottlenecks of mechanistic circuits, complementary to existing path-patching approaches.

</details>


### [101] [When Correct Demonstrations Hurt: Rethinking the Role of Exemplars in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26350)
*Chenghao Qiu,Chunli Peng,Yufeng Yang,Kuan-Hao Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了上下文学习(ICL)中"正确性-效用鸿沟"现象：正确的示范不一定有用，某些正确示范甚至会降低ICL准确率。通过引入"任务保持扰动"框架，作者发现扰动后的示范会改变模型用于上下文推理的证据混合，从而分离示范正确性与效用。实验表明，对于小模型、困难任务和高扰动比例，ICL性能会大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The intuition that correct input-output examples are necessary for ICL is challenged by a counterintuitive phenomenon: correctness does not guarantee exemplar utility, and some correct demonstrations can even reduce ICL accuracy.

Method: Proposed task-preserving perturbations framework, including label-updating perturbations and stricter target-preserving perturbations, to study the correctness-utility gap in ICL.

Result: Task-preserving perturbed demonstrations can substantially degrade ICL performance, especially for smaller models, harder tasks, and higher perturbation ratios.

Conclusion: Task-preserving perturbed demonstrations can substantially degrade ICL performance; robust ICL requires evaluating not only whether demonstrations are correct, but also how they influence contextual inference.

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is often motivated by the intuition that demonstrations help because they provide correct input-output examples. However, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: correctness does not guarantee exemplar utility, and some correct demonstrations can even reduce ICL accuracy. To study this correctness-utility gap, we introduce task-preserving perturbations, where only the exemplar input is changed, while the example remains a correct instance of the same task. Concretely, each perturbed exemplar is assigned the target induced by the task mapping. This framework covers both label-updating perturbations, where task-relevant semantics change and targets are recomputed, and stricter target-preserving perturbations, where the original target remains valid. We formalize the resulting failure mode as contextual evidence shift: task-preserving perturbations can change the effective mixture of evidence used by the model for contextual inference, thereby separating exemplar correctness from exemplar utility. Across sentiment classification, logical reasoning, and math word problems, we find that task-preserving perturbed demonstrations can substantially degrade ICL performance, especially for smaller models, harder tasks, and higher perturbation ratios. Our results show that robust ICL requires evaluating not only whether demonstrations are correct, but also how they influence contextual inference. Code is available at https://github.com/Chenghao-Qiu/Task-Preserving-ICL.

</details>


### [102] [Energy-Gated Attention and Wavelet Positional Encoding: Complementary Inductive Biases for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26355)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补归纳偏置解决标准Transformer注意力的缺陷：能量门控注意力(EGA)通过学习能量估计选择"关注什么"，Morlet位置编码(MoPE)通过小波编码选择"在哪里关注"。两者组合在TinyShakespeare上实现超加和性改善(+0.119)，证明显著性和局部性是互补归纳偏置。实验限制在6M参数以下、字符级基准、单种子训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力机制存在两个缺陷：将所有令牌视为同等显著，忽视输入的信息结构；所有位置被视为同等局部，无法捕捉不同尺度/频率下的位置影响差异。

Method: 提出两种简单组件：能量门控注意力(EGA)通过单线性投影学习键令牌嵌入的能量估计来门控值聚合，实现"选择关注内容"；Morlet位置编码(MoPE)用学习的高斯窗小波替换固定正弦编码，自适应调整联合位置-频率定位，实现"选择关注位置"。

Result: EGA单独使用获得+0.092验证损失改善，MoPE单独使用为-0.032，但两者组合达到+0.119——超过部分之和。该超加和性在两次独立训练运行中均观察到。消融实验确认结构化谱先验始终不如无约束学习组件。

Conclusion: MoPE与EGA的组合在TinyShakespeare上取得+0.119验证损失改善，超过两者单独使用效果之和，表明能量显著性和尺度选择局部性作为互补归纳偏置具有协同增益效应。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention computes pairwise token similarity but treats all tokens as equally salient and all positions as equally local, regardless of the informational structure of the input. We identify two complementary inductive biases that standard attention lacks: energy salience (which tokens concentrate informational energy, learned end-to-end without explicit frequency decomposition) and scale-selective locality (how far positional influence extends at each frequency, implemented via Morlet wavelet encoding). We address both with two simple components. Energy-Gated Attention (EGA) gates value aggregation by a learned energy estimate of key token embeddings, computed via a single linear projection; it selects what to attend to. Morlet Positional Encoding (MoPE) replaces fixed sinusoidal encodings with learned Gaussian-windowed wavelets that adapt the joint position-frequency localization to the corpus; it specifies where attention operates at each scale. On TinyShakespeare, EGA alone achieves +0.092 validation loss improvement over standard attention (+0.103 over Phase 1-3 baseline); MoPE alone is -0.032 (below baseline as a standalone encoding); but their combination achieves +0.119 -- more than the sum of parts. This superadditivity, observed across two independent training runs, is the central empirical finding: salience and locality are complementary inductive biases, each addressing a gap the other cannot fill alone. Ablations confirm that structured spectral priors (Morlet wavelet gates, scale-initialized heads, fixed sinusoidal PE) consistently underperform their unconstrained learned counterparts, while complementary learned components interact superadditively. All experiments are at small scale (<=6M parameters, character-level benchmarks, single seed); larger-scale multi-seed validation is the most important direction for future work.

</details>


### [103] [Balancing Plasticity and Stability with Fast and Slow Successor Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26357)
*Raymond Chua,Doina Precup,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper investigates deep RL agents in environments with gradual continual drift (as opposed to abrupt changes). Through experiments in modified Miniworld and MuJoCo environments, the authors demonstrate that stability-focused synaptic consolidation methods outperform plasticity-focused approaches like parameter resetting. They further show that consolidating Successor Features across multiple timescales is particularly effective, suggesting that stability and predictive representations are key for continual learning in realistic non-stationary settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world environments evolve gradually through continual drift, but prior RL research focuses on abrupt task changes. This distinction is critical for the stability-plasticity dilemma, as abrupt changes may demand different mechanisms than naturalistic gradual changes. The paper aims to investigate appropriate learning strategies for realistic non-stationary settings.

Method: Modified 3D Miniworld and MuJoCo environments with naturalistic continual non-stationarity; compared synaptic consolidation (stability-focused) vs. parameter resetting (plasticity-focused); applied consolidation to Successor Features across multiple timescales.

Result: Stability-favoring methods (synaptic consolidation) outperform plasticity-focused approaches (parameter resetting); consolidating Successor Features yields superior performance compared to consolidating Q-values; multi-timescale stabilization of SFs captures complementary aspects of gradual environmental change.

Conclusion: Multi-timescale consolidation of Successor Features is an effective approach for continual learning; prioritizing stability over plasticity is crucial when environmental changes are gradual rather than abrupt.

Abstract: A hallmark of intelligence is the ability to adapt in non-stationary environments, yet deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents often struggle in such settings. Prior studies introduce non-stationarity through abrupt shifts in features or dynamics, whereas real-world environments often evolve gradually through continual drift. This distinction has important implications for the "stability-plasticity dilemma" in RL, as abrupt task changes may demand more plasticity than naturalistic settings. To address this, we modify existing 3D Miniworld and MuJoCo environments to incorporate naturalistic, continual non-stationarity, and use them to examine how stability and adaptation affect performance under continuous environmental change. We find that methods favoring stability, such as synaptic consolidation, outperform approaches focused on plasticity, such as parameters resetting. Motivated by this result, and prior evidence that Successor Features (SFs) reduce interference, we investigate whether SFs are better consolidation targets than Q-values. Across both environments, applying neuro-inspired synaptic consolidation to SFs yields superior performance on continually changing settings. Moreover, consolidation is most effective when SFs are stabilized across multiple timescales, which capture complementary aspects of gradual environmental change. Together, these results suggest that stability is more critical in continual learning when changes are gradual, and that multi-timescale consolidation of predictive representations is an effective approach.

</details>


### [104] [Online Learning on Hidden-Convex Losses via Algorithmic Equivalence: Optimal Regret, Geometric Barrier, and Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26373)
*Anas Barakat,Andreas Kontogiannis,Vasilis Pollatos,Ioannis Panageas,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了隐藏凸损失下在线学习的最优遗憾恢复问题。通过改进离散时间算法等价性论证，证明了在线梯度下降法在相同假设下可达到 O(√T) 的最优对抗遗憾界。同时将充分条件从对角Jacobian条件收紧为必要且充分的Hessian相容性条件，并证明了该条件的必要性。进一步扩展至单点bandit反馈场景，提出球面平滑策略并给出 O(T^(3/4)) 遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究(Ghai et al., 2022)仅证明隐藏凸损失下 OGD 可达 O(T^(2/3)) 遗憾界，但无法确定能否恢复 OCO 中的最优 Θ(√T) 遗憾率；同时其几何条件(对角Jacobian)是否必要且充分尚不明确。

Method: 通过更精确的离散时间算法等价性论证，证明 OGD 在隐藏凸损失上可实现 O(√T) 遗憾界；建立 Hessian 相容性作为必要充分条件；构造反例验证条件必要性；扩展至单点 bandit 反馈场景并采用球面平滑技术。

Result: 理论上证明了 OGD 在隐藏凸设置下可达 O(√T) 最优遗憾界；确立了 Hessian 相容性作为算法等价的充要条件；当该条件失败时，OGD 遗憾可退化至 Ω(T)；Bandit OGD 在球面平滑下可达 O(T^(3/4)) 期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 通过Hessian相容性条件替代原有对角Jacobian条件，扩大了可容许重参数化类别。Bandit设置下提出球面平滑的 bandit OGD，达到 O(T^(3/4)) 期望遗憾界。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with hidden-convex losses, i.e., nonconvex losses that become convex after a nonlinear reparameterization. Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) proved that, under geometric and smoothness assumptions, online gradient descent (OGD) on such nonconvex losses approximately simulates online mirror descent (OMD) on the underlying convex losses with a suitable regularizer, yielding $\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret. They left open whether the optimal $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret from online convex optimization can be recovered in this hidden-convex setting. We answer this question affirmatively. More specifically, via a sharper discrete-time algorithmic equivalence argument, we prove that OGD achieves $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under the same assumptions, matching the optimal worst-case rate for adversarial online convex optimization. We also address another open question of Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) by clarifying the geometry required for this algorithmic equivalence. We replace the diagonal-Jacobian sufficient condition with a necessary-and-sufficient Hessian compatibility condition, thereby expanding the class of admissible reparameterizations. We complement our tight regret bound with a lower bound showing that the Hessian compatibility assumption is essential for OGD; when it fails, we construct a smooth reparameterization and an adversarial sequence of hidden-convex losses for which OGD suffers $Ω(T)$ regret. Finally, we extend our analysis to one-point bandit feedback and prove a $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ expected regret bound for bandit OGD with spherical smoothing, matching its classical rate on convex losses.

</details>


### [105] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判了时序因果发现中将非线性模型学习到的因果关系简化为标量边分数的做法，指出这会造成严重信息瓶颈。作者提出函数值因果影响（function-valued causal influence）概念，并用个体条件期望（ICE）框架从训练好的模型中直接估计因果响应函数。实验表明，具有相同标量分数的边可能呈现截然不同的函数行为，且该方法能揭示标量方法系统性地遗漏的特定情境因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scalar causal edge scores (common in nonlinear ML-based causal discovery) obscure the true object learned—state-dependent functions whose effects vary across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. These scalar scores conflate between-state variation with within-state residual noise, causing loss of important structural information.

Method: The authors use Neural Additive Vector Autoregression (NAVAR) as a representative architecture and introduce a framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation (ICE) for estimating causal response functions from trained models.

Result: Controlled experiments show that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors (monotonic, thresholded, saturating, sign-changing effects). Applied to democratic development data, function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates through synthetic and real-world experiments that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structures that scalar score methods systematically miss.

Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

</details>


### [106] [FM-fMRI: Event Conditioned Flow Matching for Rest-to-Task fMRI Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26423)
*Peiyu Duan,Jiyao Wang,Nicha C. Dvornek,Junlin Yang,Ziqi Gao,Lawrence H. Staib,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-fMRI，一个基于事件条件流匹配的任务态fMRI生成模型，可从静息态fMRI合成任务态ROI时间序列，在HCP和BioPoint自闭症数据集上表现优于扩散模型、GAN和VAE基线方法，并成功应用于自闭症分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务态fMRI能直接反映神经动力学，但获取成本高、难度大；而静息态fMRI数据丰富易得，因此需要研究从静息态到任务态的跨模态合成方法，以降低大规模应用的门槛。

Method: 提出FM-fMRI方法，采用事件条件流匹配（flow-matching）模型学习从静息态fMRI到任务态ROI时间序列的条件向量场，支持连续时间生成和基于ODE的快速采样，可灵活处理异质性事件计划。

Result: FM-fMRI在Human Connectome Project和BioPoint自闭症队列上实现了最强的频谱和连接一致性，分布匹配性能优于条件扩散模型、GAN和VAE基线。

Conclusion: FM-fMRI可有效合成任务态fMRI时间序列，在数据受限的临床场景中具有实用价值；通过增强BioPoint队列数据，成功提升了自闭症分类的下游任务性能。

Abstract: Task-based fMRI provides a direct readout of task-evoked neural dynamics, but it is expensive and difficult to acquire at scale, motivating rest-to-task synthesis from widely available resting-state fMRI (rsfMRI). We propose FM-fMRI, an event-conditioned flow-matching model that learns a continuous-time conditional vector field to generate task ROI time series from a subject's rsfMRI and the task event information. The formulation enables fast ODE-based sampling and flexible conditioning over heterogeneous event schedules. Rather than optimizing for pointwise reconstruction, we evaluated generated signals using complementary criteria that probe temporal and spectral structure, subject and group-level connectome consistency, and distributional alignment. On the public Human Connectome Project and internal BioPoint autism cohort, FM-fMRI achieves the strongest spectral and connectivity agreement and improved distribution-level matching over conditional diffusion, generative adversarial networks (GANs), and variational autoencoders (VAEs) baselines. Furthermore, we augment the BioPoint cohort by synthesizing task-fMRI ROI time series with our method, improving downstream autism classification and demonstrating practical utility in data-limited clinical settings. The code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [107] [Aperiodic and Low-Frequency Spectral Bias in Reconstruction based EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26434)
*Aditya Kommineni,Emily Zhou,Kleanthis Avramidis,Simon Bock Segaard,Jeppe Roden Münster,Andreas Peter Juhl Hansen,Takfarinas Medani,Tiantian Feng,Richard Leahy,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了重建目标预训练任务与EEG信号特殊频谱结构之间的根本不匹配：基于重建的EEG基础模型倾向于过度表征高频 aperiodic 成分而忽视 oscillatory 成分，且编码了过多的受试者身份信息而非任务相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EEG基础模型在大数据场景下表现良好，但在低资源环境下往往无法优于完全监督模型。

Method: 通过受控的合成生成的EEG输入进行实验，并结合真实世界BCI数据集上的线性探针评估。

Result: EEG基础模型嵌入主要捕获EEG信号的周期性成分，而对振荡成分（尤其是高频）的表征不足；嵌入编码了更多受试者身份信息而非任务相关信息。

Conclusion: Future work should incorporate auxiliary losses explicitly targeting high-frequency oscillatory structure to develop more capable and generalizable EEG representations.

Abstract: EEG foundation models, pre-trained on large-scale unlabelled EEG data, have emerged as a promising direction towards learning generalizable EEG representations. Despite showing positive results in data-rich regimes, they often fail to outperform significantly smaller supervised models in low-resource settings compared to fully supervised models. We provide a mechanistic account of this shortcoming, attributing it to a fundamental mismatch between reconstruction-based pretext tasks and the idiosyncratic spectral structure of EEG signals, which decompose into distinct high-power aperiodic and low-power oscillatory components. Using controlled, synthetically-generated EEG inputs, we demonstrate that EEG foundation model embeddings are biased to capture the aperiodic components of the EEG signal while under-representing oscillatory components, particularly at higher frequencies. Additionally, linear probe evaluations on real-world BCI datasets further reveal that embeddings encode subject identity more strongly than task-relevant information, thereby reinforcing the low-frequency and aperiodic component bias in foundation model embeddings trained primarily on reconstruction based objectives. Together, these findings elucidate a failure mode in reconstruction based EEG foundation models and motivate future work to incorporate auxiliary losses explicitly targeting high-frequency oscillatory structure as a path toward more capable and generalizable EEG representations.

</details>


### [108] [DDGAD: Trajectory Dynamics for Diffusion-Based Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26446)
*Yuxin Yang,Limei Hu,Feng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDGAD框架，利用轨迹动力学区分正常和异常节点，通过可靠性感知共识机制抑制GCN中的污染传播问题，在五个真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GCN的图异常检测方法存在污染传播的根本问题——异常节点通过消息传递污染其邻居的表示，导致检测性能下降。

Method: 提出基于轨迹动力学的扩散图异常检测框架（DDGAD）。引入分布式可靠性感知共识精炼机制缓解污染传播，定义三种互补的异常信号：邻域不一致性、可靠性权重和动力学冲突能量，并提供正常节点稳定性的初步理论分析。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: DDGAD框架通过分布式可靠性感知共识精炼机制有效抑制了污染传播，在五个真实数据集上验证了有效性。正常节点在扩散正则化和可靠性感知邻域共识的耦合作用下表现出稳定一致的表示轨迹，而异常节点则因全局流形先验与局部污染消息传递之间的方向分歧而呈现不稳定冲突的动力学行为。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify nodes or substructures whose behavior or attributes deviate significantly from the overall pattern in graph-structured data, with critical applications in financial risk control, social network analysis, and cybersecurity. However, existing GCN-based methods suffer from the fundamental problem of contamination propagation, where anomalous nodes pollute the representations of their neighbors through message passing, leading to degraded detection performance. In this paper, we propose DDGAD, a novel diffusion-based graph anomaly detection framework that leverages trajectory dynamics to distinguish normal and anomalous nodes. Our key insight is that normal nodes exhibit consistent and stable representation trajectories under the coupled effects of diffusion regularization and reliability-aware neighborhood consensus, while anomalous nodes exhibit unstable and conflicting dynamics due to the directional disagreement between the global manifold prior and locally contaminated message passing. To mitigate contamination propagation, we introduce a distributed reliability-aware consensus refinement mechanism and define three complementary anomaly signals: neighbor inconsistency, reliability weight, and dynamical conflict energy. We further provide a preliminary theoretical analysis on normal node stability under the coupled dynamics. These signals collectively characterize anomalous behaviors from the perspectives of local inconsistency, consensus reliability, and dynamical instability. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [109] [MuCon: Clipped Muon Updates for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26459)
*Albert Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了MuCon剪切操作能否在避免完整密集SVD的前提下进行近似。通过推导极分解和标量根的精确恒等式，揭示了阈值附近奇异值的病态性是主要数值障碍，并指出矩阵函数方法需配合稳定的极分解原语或显式正则化才能可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器中的剪切（clipping）操作通常需要完整的密集奇异值分解（SVD），计算成本高昂。该论文旨在探究在何种条件下可以绕过完整SVD来近似MuCon剪切步骤。

Method: 给出了两个精确恒等式：极分解/绝对值公式和标量根 formulation，后者引入了剪切正定因子的有理牛顿滤波器。同时分析了两种方法共有的数值障碍：阈值附近的奇异值导致符号决策和有理求解器病态化。

Result: 识别出MuCon剪切计算的数值障碍本质：当奇异值接近剪切阈值τ时，符号决策变得病态，导致有理方程组条件数激增。

Conclusion: 矩阵函数方法仅在与稳定的极分解/平方根原语或显式正则化结合时才能有效使用；该研究为构建无需完整密集SVD的近似MuCon剪切算法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Muon-style optimizers take a matrix-valued momentum or preconditioned update $B = U \operatorname{diag}(σ_1,\ldots,σ_r) V^\top$ and replace it with its canonical partial polar factor $\operatorname{Pol}(B) = U V^\top$. This maps every nonzero singular value to one. MuCon is the clipped-Muon variant studied here: it applies singular-value clipping to the same Muon matrix, $D^{\mathrm{MuCon}}\_τ(B) = \operatorname{MClip}\_τ(B) = U \operatorname{diag}\bigl(\min\{σ\_i,τ\}\bigr) V^\top, \qquad τ> 0$. Thus, $\operatorname{MClip}\_τ$ denotes the mathematical clipping operator, while MuCon denotes the optimizer primitive that substitutes this clipped direction for Muon's polar direction. The Muon/MuCon scaling parameterization used in this work is called $\text{SpectralP}$: it is the hidden-matrix scaling recipe under which polar Muon or clipped MuCon directions are applied. The map $\operatorname{MClip}\_τ$ is the Frobenius projection onto the spectral-norm ball $\{X : \|X\|_2 \le τ\}$: it leaves singular values at or below $τ$ unchanged and modifies only the violating singular directions. This paper asks when the MuCon clipping step can be approximated without a full dense SVD. We record two exact identities, a polar/absolute-value formula and a scalar-root formulation leading to a rational Newton filter for the clipped positive-semidefinite factor, and identify the numerical obstruction common to both: singular values near the threshold make sign decisions and rational solves ill-conditioned. Matrix-function methods are therefore useful only when paired with stable polar/square-root primitives or explicit regularization near the clipping boundary.

</details>


### [110] [Diffuse to Detect: Generative Diffusion Models for Unsupervised IC Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26468)
*Yuxuan Yin,Chen He,Todd Jacobs,Jialei He,Boxun Xu,Robert Jin,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个基于扩散Transformer的无监督异常检测框架，用于半导体潜在缺陷筛选。通过自编码器压缩原始测试数据，结合正弦位置编码和晶圆位置嵌入生成结构化token序列，利用扩散模型中频时间步的噪声预测误差计算异常分数，实现无需标注缺陷的晶圆级快速筛查，并在16nm工业IC数据上达到最优性能，同时提供可解释的失效定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Latent defect screening faces extreme challenges: near-zero failure rates, high-dimensional test data, and absence of labeled anomalies, making traditional supervised approaches infeasible.

Method: Autoencoder compression of raw measurements → structured token sequence with sinusoidal and per-device wafer-position embeddings → anomaly scoring via noise-prediction error over mid-range diffusion timesteps.

Result: Outperforms existing methods on industrial 16nm IC test data under extreme class imbalance, with interpretable failure localization through latent-space reconstruction residuals.

Conclusion: First Diffusion Transformer-based unsupervised anomaly detection for latent defect screening, achieving state-of-the-art performance on industrial 16nm IC data with interpretable failure localization.

Abstract: Latent defect screening is challenged by extremely low failure rates, high-dimensional test data, and absence of labeled anomalies. We propose the first unsupervised anomaly detection framework incorporating a Diffusion Transformer. Raw test measurements are first compressed by an autoencoder, then reshaped into a structured token sequence enriched with sinusoidal and per-device wafer-position embeddings. Anomaly scores are derived from the noise-prediction error over mid-range diffusion timesteps, enabling fast wafer-scale screening without any labeled defects or manual feature engineering. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on industrial 16nm IC test data under extreme class imbalance, offering interpretable failure localization through latent-space reconstruction residuals.

</details>


### [111] [Extra-Merge: Tracing the Rank-1 Subspace of Model Merging in Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26484)
*Wenjie Zhou,Bohan Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Chenxi Jia,Wei Chen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a lightweight paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we analyze late-stage pre-training trajectories and uncover a \textbf{Rank-1 Subspace} phenomenon: while raw optimization steps oscillate violently, consecutive \emph{merged} checkpoints collapse onto a stable, approximately one-dimensional linear manifold. We theoretically ground this observation in a \emph{river-valley} landscape analysis: averaging acts as a geometric low-pass filter that dampens high-curvature noise to reveal the optimal descent direction. Capitalizing on this insight, we propose \textbf{Extra-Merge}, a training-free strategy that extrapolates along this subspace to minimize loss without additional gradient updates. Extensive experiments across GPT-2 and LLaMA families (124M to 2B) demonstrate that Extra-Merge consistently outperforms standard merging baselines. Notably, it yields consistent zero-shot accuracy gains on Pythia-12B downstream tasks and generalizes effectively to the Muon optimizer \citep{jordan2024muon}.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Credit-assigned Policy Gradient for Early Stage Retrieval in Two-stage Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26385)
*Haruka Kiyohara,Mihaela Curmei,Ariel Evnine,Shankar Kalyanaraman,Israel Nir,Ana-Roxana Pop,Nitzan Razin,Sarah Dean,Thorsten Joachims,Udi Weinsberg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文针对两阶段检索系统中早期排序器（ESR）的训练难题，提出信用分配策略梯度（CA-PG）方法，通过边际化候选集组成来降低策略梯度方差，在保持与后期排序器对齐的前提下，显著提升大规模候选集场景下的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略梯度方法（V-PG）在大规模候选集场景下梯度方差爆炸，难以规模化应用。原因在于V-PG将梯度传播到候选集的联合概率分布，忽略了个别候选项对奖励的独立贡献。

Method: 提出信用分配策略梯度（CA-PG），通过将梯度计算从联合概率转换为目标项被选中的边际概率（即对所有包含该目标的候选集求边际），实现方差缩减。

Result: 实验证明，CA-PG在Plackett-Luce模型下显著提升收敛速度和训练稳定性，尤其在大规模候选集场景。

Conclusion: CA-PG显著改善了大规模候选集场景下ESR训练的收敛速度和稳定性，为两阶段检索系统的端到端优化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale search, recommendation, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically employ a two-stage architecture: an early-stage ranker (ESR) generates a candidate set, which is subsequently re-ranked by a late-stage ranker (LSR). While there are many reinforcement learning (RL) methods for training the LSR, end-to-end training of the ESR has proven challenging. In particular, naive application of "vanilla" policy gradient (V-PG) is not scalable for candidate-set sizes relevant for practical use due to exploding variance. This issue arises because V-PG propagates the gradient to the joint probability of the candidate sets, ignoring the contribution of each specific item in the candidate set to the reward. To mitigate this issue, we propose a novel "credit-assigned" policy gradient (CA-PG), which computes gradients with respect to the probability that the target item is chosen in any candidate set, i.e. marginalizing over all candidate sets that contain it. Our theoretical analysis reveals that CA-PG significantly reduces the variance of V-PG by marginalizing over the specific composition of the candidate set, while preserving the ability to learn the correct ranking of items under a reasonably aligned LSR policy. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that CA-PG improves the convergence speed and training stability for ESRs utilizing the canonical Plackett-Luce model, especially when the candidate-set size is large.

</details>


### [113] [Plans for Evaluating Structured Generative Search Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26400)
*Tetsuya Sakai,Jina Lee,Hanpei Fang,Young-In Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We propose a framework for evaluating structured generative search summaries that are placed atop organic web search results. A structured summary, generated by a large language model, typically consists of an overview, several sections with section titles, and a list of source documents that are cited within the summary. We then describe our plans for implementing and evaluating the framework.

</details>


### [114] [Uniboost: Global Coordination with Value Alignment for Fair and Efficient Traffic Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26424)
*Ge Fan,Nan Zhao,Kai Meng,Cong Luo,Yang Fu,Huiping Chu,Jialin Liu,Yuning Jiang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Uniboost is a unified traffic allocation framework for industrial recommendation systems that addresses score inflation and interpretability issues through value alignment and decoupled boosting, validated via A/B tests to improve both micro-level efficiency and macro-level system guidance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing recommendation system approaches suffer from three key challenges: coupled allocation plans, score inflation, and lack of interpretability, which limit their effectiveness in allocating traffic across diverse business objectives.

Method: Uniboost introduces a posterior value alignment mechanism that calibrates abstract model scores to anchor metrics with explicit business semantics, combined with an independent linear boosting paradigm to decouple complex weighting schemes for precise attribution of each plan's contribution.

Result: Three key findings: 1) Reducing overall weight of weighted scores mitigates unintended business interference for more efficient micro-level traffic allocation; 2) Post-hoc analyses and aggregated dashboards provide macro-level insights for overall traffic allocation mechanism design; 3) The "Effective Completion Score" serves as a reliable anchor post-metric for content recommendation pipelines.

Conclusion: Uniboost provides an efficient and controllable traffic regulation solution for large-scale industrial recommendation systems, improving both micro-level traffic allocation efficiency and recommendation performance while offering macro-level guidance for system iteration.

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of internet services, recommendation systems have become indispensable. In particular, the blending (re-ranking) stage plays a pivotal role in allocating traffic across diverse business objectives. However, existing approaches often suffer from coupled allocation plans, score inflation, and a lack of interpretability. To address these challenges, we propose Uniboost, a unified traffic allocation framework. Uniboost introduces a posterior value alignment mechanism that calibrates abstract model scores to anchor metrics with explicit business semantics, significantly enhancing interpretability. Furthermore, it employs an independent linear boosting paradigm to decouple complex weighting schemes, enabling precise attribution of each plan's contribution. We validate the effectiveness of Uniboost through online A/B tests and in-depth data analysis, demonstrating three key findings: 1) Reducing the overall weight of weighted scores effectively mitigates unintended business interference, yielding a more efficient micro-level traffic allocation strategy; 2) Post-hoc analyses and aggregated dashboards provide intuitive, macro-level insights that guide the design of the overall traffic allocation mechanism; 3) The proposed "Effective Completion Score" serves as an easily obtainable post-metric that offers a reliable anchor for content recommendation pipelines. Collectively, our experiments show that Uniboost not only improves traffic allocation efficiency and recommendation performance at the micro level but also provides macro-level guidance for system iteration. Thus, this work provides an efficient and controllable traffic regulation solution for large-scale industrial recommendation systems.

</details>


### [115] [Is Position Bias in Dense Retrievers Built In-or Learned from Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26578)
*Daegon Yu,SeungYoon Han,Woomyoung Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究了训练数据中证据位置分布如何影响密集检索器的位置偏差。研究通过构建位置定向的合成训练集，对8种不同架构的预训练模型进行微调，发现训练分布的位置偏斜会显著影响检索偏差方向，位置平衡训练可将位置敏感性降低57-87%，表明平衡数据策划是缓解位置偏差的实用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于密集检索器位置偏差的研究主要从架构角度解释，但忽视了训练数据中证据位置分布的影响。

Method: 构建了证据位置分别在文档开头、中间或结尾的合成位置定向训练集；对8种架构多样的预训练模型分别在使用位置偏斜和平衡的训练分布下进行微调；在排序层面和表征层面进行多维度分析。

Result: 发现跨模型存在强烈的方向性模式：偏斜的训练分布偏好对应位置的证据；位置平衡训练使位置敏感性降低57-87%，同时保持具有竞争力的平均检索性能；微调通常会重塑学习到的位置偏好，但部分模型中仍保留预训练的架构特异性倾向。

Conclusion: 训练数据的位置分布是影响检索级位置偏差的主要可控因素，平衡数据策划是实际可行的缓解策略。

Abstract: Dense retrievers exhibit positional bias, favoring documents whose query-relevant information appears near the beginning and degrading retrieval performance when the information appears later. While prior work on positional bias in dense retrievers has largely focused on architectural explanations, we study how the positional distribution of evidence in training data affects retrieval-level bias direction. To test this, we construct synthetic position-targeted training sets in which query-relevant evidence appears at the beginning, middle, or end of documents, and fine-tune eight architecturally diverse pretrained models under position-skewed and balanced training distributions. At the ranking level, we observe a strong directional pattern across the examined models: skewed training distributions favor evidence at the corresponding positions. Position-balanced training reduces positional sensitivity by 57--87\% on position-aware benchmarks, with competitive mean retrieval performance in our controlled setting. Representation-level analyses further suggest that fine-tuning often reshapes learned positional preferences, although pre-existing architectural or pretraining-specific tendencies persist in some models. These results identify training-position distribution as a major controllable factor in retrieval-level position bias and suggest balanced data curation as a practical mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [116] [L2Rec: Towards Dual-View Understanding of LLMs for Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26717)
*Pingjun Pan,Tingting Zhou,Peiyao Lu,Tingting Fei,Hongxiang Chen,Chuanjiang Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: L2Rec通过DPMoE机制实现参数级behavioral-semantic统一适配，解决传统方法分布对齐问题，工业验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐方法在input-level或output-level整合behavioral和semantic信号，存在distribution gap或缺乏端到端监督的问题。

Method: 提出Dual-view Personalized MoE (DPMoE)机制，通过 view-specific 低秩扰动实现参数级统一；设计 adaptive cross-view fusion module 整合双视图输出。

Result: 四个数据集实验表明L2Rec一致优于SOTA基线；大规模工业平台A/B测试验证关键 engagement metrics 显著提升。

Conclusion: L2Rec通过单一LLM主干实现 behavioral 和 semantic 双重适应，融合模块有效整合双视图输出，在工业场景验证有效。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) for personalized recommendation requires aligning their general-purpose capabilities with user-specific preferences while effectively leveraging both behavioral and semantic signals. Existing approaches typically integrate these signals at either the input level (e.g., injecting behavioral embeddings into the token space) or the output level (e.g., contrastive alignment of separate encoders), suffering from distribution gaps or lack of end-to-end task supervision. In this work, we introduce L2Rec, which unifies behavioral and semantic understanding at the parameter level of LLMs. Our key insight is that the same set of Transformer parameters can serve as a shared medium for both views: by applying view-specific, personalized low-rank perturbations via a Dual-view Personalized Mixture-of-Experts (DPMoE) mechanism, L2Rec enables a single LLM backbone to produce complementary behavioral and semantic adaptations for each user with minimal representation-level misalignment. An adaptive cross-view fusion module further integrates the dual-view outputs into a unified user preference. Experiments on four datasets show that L2Rec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and online A/B testing on a large-scale industrial platform validates significant improvements in key engagement metrics.

</details>


### [117] [RAGEAR: Retrieval-Augmented Graph-Enhanced Academic Recommender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26819)
*Francesco Granata,Lorenzo Lamazzi,Misael Mongiovì,Francesco Poggi,Valeria Secchini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RAGEAR是一种神经符号系统，通过融合讲座文本语义检索与知识图谱结构化推理，实现学术课程推荐。其创新点在于图感知聚合函数，有效整合多层次证据提升推荐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有课程推荐系统多依赖元数据，缺乏对细粒度教学内容的深度利用，无法有效处理学生对具体学习内容的语义查询需求。

Method: 提出RAGEAR系统，结合密集检索（对完整讲座文本进行语义检索）与符号知识图谱（建模课程、课时、文本块、学分、学习计划等实体及关系）。核心贡献是图感知聚合函数，通过三个因子将文本块级别的证据传播聚合到课程级别：(1)课程相关检索相似度的占比；(2)相关文本块的排名强度；(3)证据在课程各课时中的分布。

Result: 在152个类学生查询上的评估表明：(1)讲座文本转录内容优于仅基于元数据的检索；(2)RAGEAR相较于基于文本的SumP基线方法进一步提升了排序质量，尤其在top-ranked推荐中效果显著。

Conclusion: RAGEAR通过图感知聚合函数有效整合了文本检索与结构化知识，显著提升了课程推荐的质量，特别是在排名靠前的推荐结果上表现更佳。

Abstract: We present RAGEAR (Retrieval-Augmented Graph-Enhanced Academic Recommender), a neurosymbolic recommender system for academic course recommendation. RAGEAR combines dense retrieval over full lecture transcripts with a symbolic Knowledge Graph modelling courses, lessons, transcript chunks, credits, study plans, and curricular information. The Knowledge Graph supports symbolic filtering and contextualisation based on structured constraints, such as credits, academic disciplines, study plans, and prerequisites. Unlike metadata-based approaches, it exploits fine-grained instructional content by retrieving transcript chunks semantically aligned with a student's query. The main contribution is a graph-aware aggregation function that propagates chunk-level evidence to course-level recommendations. The score combines three factors: the share of retrieved similarity associated with a course, the rank-based strength of its relevant chunks, and the distribution of evidence across lessons. We evaluate RAGEAR on 152 student-like queries through a human evaluation sample and a large-scale LLM-based relevance assessment. Results show that lecture transcripts improve over metadata-only retrieval, and that RAGEAR further improves ranking quality over a transcript-based normalized SumP baseline, especially for top-ranked recommendations.

</details>


### [118] [ICICLE: Expanding Retrieval with In-Context Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26902)
*Yu-Chen Den,Yung-Yu Shih,Zhi Rui Tam,Kuan-Yu Chen,Pu-Jen Cheng,Yun-Nung Chen,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ICICLE框架，将增量生成检索重新定义为上下文内检索问题，通过COPY路由、偏好校准和大上下文适应实现无需参数更新的新文档索引。实验表明该方法有效，但路由失败是扩展性的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成检索(GR)将查询直接映射到文档标识符，但语料库扩展代价高昂——新增文档需要更新模型参数来编码新的文档-docid关联，导致重复训练和灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出ICICLE框架，采用[COPY]路由机制、偏好校准和大上下文适应，将源感知docid生成与上下文提供的文档-docid对结合，实现新文档的上下文内检索。

Result: 在MS MARCO和NQ320K上，ICICLE改善了新引入文档的检索性能，同时保持了已见文档的保留率，无需针对语料库的重新训练。

Conclusion: ICICLE实现了无需重新训练的增量索引，MS MARCO和NQ320K上的实验表明该方法有效改善了新文档检索同时保留已见文档性能；路由失败是扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) maps queries directly to document identifiers (docids) using parametric knowledge, However, this design makes corpus expansion costly: adding new documents requires updating model parameters to encode new document-docid associations incurs repeated training and catastrophic forgetting of previously indexed documents. In this work, we revisit incremental GR as an in-context retrieval problem, where newly added documents are supplied as inference-time document-docid evidence. We propose ICICLE, an in-context indexing framework that performs source-aware docid generation over both parametric memory and context-provided document-docid pairs. ICICLE combines a `[COPY]`-based routing mechanism, preference-based calibration, and large context adaptation to distinguish context-grounded retrieval from parametric retrieval. Experiments on MS MARCO and NQ320K show that ICICLE improves retrieval of newly introduced documents while preserving seen-document retention without corpus-specific retraining. Our analysis further shows that high-shot degradation is mainly caused by routing failure, highlighting source-selection calibration as a key bottleneck for scaling in-context generative retrieval.

</details>


### [119] [The 2nd EReL@MIR Workshop on Efficient Representation Learning for Multimodal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26941)
*Junchen Fu,Xuri Ge,Xin Xin,Alexandros Karatzoglou,Ioannis Arapakis,Xi Wang,Qijiong Liu,Qian Li,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper proposes organizing the EReL@MIR workshop at MM 2026 to address efficiency challenges when adapting large multimodal foundation models for information retrieval tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While large multimodal foundation models (Qwen, LLaVA, CLIP) achieve strong performance on various multimodal information retrieval (MIR) tasks like web search, cross-modal retrieval, and recommender systems, their massive parameter counts create significant efficiency bottlenecks that hinder practical deployment in real-world IR applications.

Method: The workshop will serve as a platform bringing together academic and industry researchers to discuss and develop solutions, focusing on efficiency challenges in adapting large multimodal models for IR tasks during training, deployment, and inference stages.

Result: Workshop organized at MM 2026 with official website https://erel-mir.github.io/

Conclusion: The proposed EReL@MIR workshop at MM 2026 aims to bridge the gap between the impressive capabilities of multimodal foundation models and their practical deployment in IR systems by fostering collaboration, developing efficiency-focused solutions, and establishing new benchmarks and metrics for the field.

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has attracted increasing attention in AI, driven by the strong performance of large, pretrained multimodal foundation models such as Qwen, LLaVA, and CLIP. These models deliver impressive performance on a range of multimodal information retrieval (MIR) tasks, including web search, cross-modal retrieval, and recommender systems. Yet their massive parameter counts create major efficiency bottlenecks when adapting their representations for IR tasks during training, deployment, and inference. These limitations hinder the practical use of foundation models for representation learning in information retrieval. To address these issues, we propose organizing the EReL@MIR workshop at MM 2026, bringing together researchers from academia and industry to discuss emerging solutions, open challenges, and new efficiency metrics and benchmarks for multimodal IR representation learning in the foundation-model era. The workshop's official website is available at https://erel-mir.github.io/.

</details>


### [120] [MuChator: Enabling Active Music Discovery via Conversational Music LLMs in Douyin Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27103)
*Jiahao Liang,Linzhi Huang,Xuannan Liu,Xukai Wang,Xuanpu Luo,Yongchun Zhu,Jingwu Chen,Feng Zhang,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MuChator是抖音音乐推出的交互式音乐大模型框架，通过音乐知识预训练、上下文感知指令微调和混合奖励模型偏好对齐三个组件，使LLM能够理解情境化音乐意图，实现主动音乐发现。实验证明其性能优于Gemini-3-Pro，线上A/B测试用户活跃天数提升46.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐平台（如抖音）采用被动推荐的沉浸式信息流范式，用户被限制在推荐结果中，难以显式表达听歌意图。实际主动音乐发现往往是情境化和口语化的，涉及模糊或不明确的请求。LLM直接应用于音乐发现受限于领域知识不足、缺乏音乐-查询协作推理能力以及对个性化偏好的浅层理解。

Method: MuChator包含三个核心组件：（1）音乐知识预训练，采用三阶段渐进式方案向LLM注入客观音乐知识、主观音乐知识和个性化音乐偏好；（2）上下文感知指令微调，通过自动化合成流水线构建高质量用户-查询-音乐三元组，对齐LLM与主动情境化用户意图；（3）混合奖励模型偏好对齐，联合建模意图相关性、个性化偏好和基本约束，使用GRPO-based强化学习优化。

Result: 在工业级音乐推荐数据集上的评估表明，MuChator优于Gemini-3-Pro等领先专有模型。

Conclusion: MuChator已在字节跳动抖音音乐App中成功部署，在线A/B测试显示用户活跃天数提升46.49%，证明了该交互式MusicLLM框架在主动音乐发现场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Douyin Music, a large-scale platform with millions of daily users, adopts an immersive, feed-based discovery paradigm, where users passively explore music through continuous recommendations. While effective for passive music discovery, this paradigm restricts users to recommendation results and provides limited support for explicitly specifying listening intents. Unlike conventional search, where users express well-defined intents through explicit queries such as specific songs or artists, real-world active music discovery is often situational and colloquial, involving vague or underspecified requests. While LLMs enable natural language interaction, their direct use in music discovery remains limited by insufficient music-domain knowledge, lack of music-query collaborative reasoning, and shallow understanding of personalized preferences. To address these challenges, we introduce MuChator, an interactive MusicLLM-based framework that enables users to actively express situational music intents in natural language. MuChator incorporates three key components: (1) Music Knowledge Pre-training, a three-stage scheme that incrementally injects objective music knowledge, subjective music knowledge, and personalized music preferences into LLMs; (2) Context-aware Instruction Tuning, which constructs high-quality user-query-music triplets through an automated synthesis pipeline to align LLMs with active and situational user intents; and (3) Preference Alignment with Hybrid RM, which jointly models intent relevance, personalized preferences, and basic constraints, and is optimized using GRPO-based reinforcement learning. Extensive evaluations on industrial music recommendation datasets demonstrate that MuChator outperforms leading proprietary models, such as Gemini-3-Pro. The model has been deployed on Douyin Music App within ByteDance, with 46.49\% improvement of user active days in online A/B test.

</details>


### [121] [Lost in the Evidence? Reproducing Document Position and Context Size Effects in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27105)
*Jorge Gabín,Anxo Perez,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究对RAG系统中的文档排序和上下文大小效应进行了系统性可复现性研究，发现主题采样是方差的主要来源，并证明理想化实验设置中的结论不一定能迁移到现实世界的RAG流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work on position-based effects (e.g., "lost in the middle") in RAG systems shows inconsistent and hard-to-reproduce findings across models, datasets, and evaluation protocols.

Method: Controlled reproducibility study: (1) repeated subset sampling across multiple topic budgets for calibration; (2) fixed topic sets for reproducing position sensitivity results; (3) realistic RAG evaluation with retriever-mediated relevance vs oracle access; (4) analysis of retrieval order and context size effects under imperfect retrieval conditions.

Result: Topic sampling is a major source of variance; small topic sets can mask or exaggerate ordering effects. Conclusions from idealised setups do not always transfer to real-world RAG pipelines. Both retrieval order and context size interact strongly with retrieval quality and model choice.

Conclusion: Released code and configurations for reproducibility and future robust RAG evaluation work.

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems rely on retrieved documents being concatenated into a model's input context, making both document ordering and context size critical yet controversial design choices. Prior work reports position-based effects such as lost in the middle and related long-context phenomena. However, empirical findings remain inconsistent and hard to reproduce across models, datasets, and evaluation protocols. In this paper, we present a systematic reproducibility study that revisits these claims and examines how they evolve with contemporary LLMs under a controlled evaluation framework. We first show that topic sampling is a major source of variance: small topic sets can mask or exaggerate ordering effects. Based on repeated subset sampling across multiple topic budgets, we provide a practical calibration procedure that identifies topic counts yielding stable trends at feasible cost. Using these fixed topic sets, we then reproduce and extend results on position sensitivity, re-evaluating lost in the middle and positional biases in modern LLMs. Then, we also study a more realistic RAG scenario in which relevance is mediated by a retriever rather than oracle access to ground-truth documents. In this setting, we re-examine a recent industry study and identify discrepancies to evaluation choices such as limited topic coverage and reliance on LLM-based judges. Finally, we conduct an analysis of how retrieval order and context size affect downstream LLM performance under imperfect retrieval. Our results demonstrate that both factors interact strongly with retrieval quality and model choice, and that conclusions drawn from idealised setups do not always transfer to real-world RAG pipelines. We release all code and configurations to support reproducibility and future work on robust RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Rethinking Agentic RAG: Toward LLM-Driven Logical Retrieval Beyond Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27123)
*Yuqi Zeng,Qixiang Deng,Yulei Wan,Ruiquan Jiang,Xiaoqing Zheng,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出将控制权更多委托给LLM来主导检索过程，采用轻量级倒排索引后端替代复杂检索架构的Agentic RAG框架，实现成本降低和幻觉减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统过度关注检索后端工程（如稠密检索、混合检索、图检索等复杂架构），而忽视了让LLM主导检索过程的重要性。作者认为应该将更多控制权交给LLM，同时使用提供细粒度控制的轻量级检索接口。

Method: 提出AgentChain框架，让LLM通过逻辑表达式表达检索意图，后端采用轻量级的倒排索引系统。LLM能够多轮交互地迭代优化查询，根据中间结果调整检索策略。

Result: 实验表明该框架在性能上与强大的Agentic混合检索基线相当，同时大幅降低了构建和服务成本。此外，基于逻辑查询锚定检索过程显著减少了生成响应中的幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将检索控制权交给LLM，同时简化后端架构，实现了性能与效率的良好平衡。这一研究为Agentic RAG系统设计提供了新思路，强调简洁的检索接口配合智能的查询控制可能比复杂的检索后端更为有效。

Abstract: Recent advances in RAG have shifted toward an agentic paradigm, where LLMs interact with retrieval systems over multiple turns and iteratively refine queries based on intermediate results. At the same time, LLMs have demonstrated a strong ability to construct structured queries that precisely express their information needs. However, contemporary RAG systems remain heavily focused on engineering complex retrieval backends, including dense, hybrid, and graph-based retrieval architectures. In this study, we argue that agentic RAG should delegate greater control to the LLM to steer the retrieval process, while relying on a lightweight retrieval interface that provides fine-grained control and faithfully executes the LLM's structured intent. Guided by this principle, we propose an agentic RAG framework that enables LLMs to formulate retrieval intents using logical expressions while simplifying the retrieval backend to an inverted-index-based system. Extensive experiments show that our framework matches a strong agentic hybrid baseline, while substantially reducing construction and serving cost. Moreover, we show that anchoring the retrieval process in logical queries substantially reduces hallucinations in generated responses.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [From user-understandable to technical process model: a model-driven approach using cuta4bpm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26117)
*Kathrin Kirchner,Siniša Nešković,Dejan Stojimirović*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参与式正向工程方法，使用块导向的CUTA4BPM语言支持与领域专家的高层过程建模，再通过模型驱动工程自动转换为图导向的BPMN模型用于详细设计，从而弥合可理解性与技术灵活性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前块导向语言虽结构化程度高、易于领域专家理解，但技术灵活性不足；而图导向语言虽技术灵活却缺乏结构性，双方存在可理解性与技术通用性之间的矛盾。

Method: 采用模型驱动工程(MDE)技术，将CUTA4BPM块导向语言的过程模型自动转换为BPMN图导向模型。

Result: 实现了从CUTA4BPM高层建模到BPMN详细设计的高效平滑过渡，兼顾了领域专家的可理解性需求和技术设计人员的灵活性需求。

Conclusion: 通过CUTA4BPM到BPMN的自动模型转换，实现了从高层领域建模到详细技术设计的平滑过渡，弥合了可理解性与技术灵活性之间的差距。

Abstract: For business process modeling, we can choose between graph-oriented and block-oriented languages. Block-oriented languages are more structured and therefore better understandable for domain experts, while graph-oriented languages allow more modeling freedom and technical versatility for process designers. To bridge this gap between understandability and technical versatility, we propose a participative forward engineering approach. It uses our block-oriented CUTA4BPM language to support high level process modeling together with domain experts, while graph-oriented BPMN is used for further detailed process design and automation. To support smooth transition from the high level modeling step to the detailed one, CUTA4BPM process models are automatically transformed to BPMN models using model driven engineering techniques.

</details>


### [124] [VISTA: An End-to-End Benchmark for Visual Spec-to-Web-App Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26144)
*JunJia Guo,Yuhang Yao,Jiawei,Zhou,Jingdi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VISTA是一个评估LLM智能体端到端Web应用生成能力的基准，通过多维度的信息条件和评估方法揭示了视觉保真度与功能正确性的解耦特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准聚焦于算法任务，缺乏对真实UI开发场景的评估；脚本化测试工具（如Playwright）在开放式代码生成场景中存在局限性，无法准确评估视觉保真度和功能正确性。

Method: 设计了5种提示信息条件（沿视觉/结构保真度和技术栈约束两个轴向变化），结合DOM参考匹配、浏览器行为测试和CLIP视觉相似度三种评估方法，对4个智能体系统进行评估。

Result: 发现视觉保真度与功能正确性在输入条件和智能体间呈现部分解耦；智能体的编辑风格差异显著但与任务质量基本正交。

Conclusion: VISTA为LLM智能体在UI开发任务中的能力评估提供了标准化、可复现的基准，推动基于智能体的软件工程研究的发展。

Abstract: We present VISTA (VIsual Spec-To-App Benchmark), a benchmark for evaluating the end-to-end web-app generation capabilities of LLM-based agents. Unlike prior code generation benchmarks that focus on algorithmic tasks, VISTA targets realistic UI-centric development, where agents must produce functional, visually coherent applications from underspecified inputs. We define five prompt-information conditions that vary along two axes, visual/structural fidelity and stack constraint: (1) text only with free stack choice, (2) text with reference screenshots under three specified stacks, (3) text with reference screenshots under free stack choice, (4) text with screenshots and pruned Figma structure under a single specified stack, and (5) text with screenshots and pruned Figma structure under free stack choice. To enable robust evaluation, each page in the benchmark is manually annotated with interactive UI components and around three visual anchor points, addressing the well-known limitations of script-based testing tools such as Playwright in open-ended code generation settings. Evaluation combines DOM-grounded reference matching, behavior-specific browser tests, and CLIP-based visual similarity, jointly measuring structural alignment, behavioral completeness, and overall visual fidelity. We use VISTA to assess four agent systems drawn from two model families and two harnesses, finding that visual fidelity and functional correctness are partially decoupled across both input conditions and agents, and that agent editing style varies sharply but is largely orthogonal to task quality. VISTA establishes a rigorous and reproducible foundation for advancing agent-based software engineering research.

</details>


### [125] [Augment Engineering: A Methodology for Multi-Tool AI Orchestration Across Professional Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26146)
*Elias Calboreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This paper defines "Augment Engineering" as the discipline of orchestrating multiple purpose-built AI tools across distinct professional domains, leveraging domain-portable meta-skills (prompt engineering and context engineering). A 5-month single-practitioner case study spanning seven domains provides exploratory quantitative evidence supporting the framework's predictions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Organizations increasingly deploy separate purpose-built AI tools across professional domains, often hiring domain specialists for each, recreating the staffing models AI was expected to transform. The paper identifies that meta-skills (prompt engineering and context engineering) are domain-portable, enabling a practitioner to apply them across any purpose-built AI tool.

Method: 5-month formative case study (November 2025 to March 2026) documenting a single practitioner applying skills across a ten-component orchestration stack spanning seven professional domains. Two quantitative tests: Cochran-Armitage trend test (n=200 interactions across two chat LLMs) and Wright's Law fit (n=82 artifacts). Six-phase orchestration methodology and four portability metrics were developed.

Result: Cochran-Armitage trend test shows first-pass acceptance rising with prompt-sophistication level (p<0.01); Wright's Law fit shows production acceleration across the artifact portfolio (p<0.01). Note: all observations from single practitioner, statistics are exploratory and hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory.

Conclusion: Augment Engineering completes a three-discipline progression: Prompt Engineering (one tool) → Context Engineering (reproducible pipelines) → Augment Engineering (portfolio of tools across domains). This framework enables a single practitioner to leverage domain-portable meta-skills across multiple purpose-built AI tools in different professional domains.

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy separate purpose-built AI tools across professional domains, often hiring domain specialists for each, recreating the staffing models AI was expected to transform. Yet the meta-skills that make these tools effective, prompt engineering (interaction-level optimization) and context engineering (structured input pipeline design), are domain-portable: a practitioner who masters them can apply them to any purpose-built AI tool in any domain. This paper defines Augment Engineering as the discipline of orchestrating multiple purpose-built AI tools across distinct professional domains, applying prompt and context engineering as portable competencies that transfer across tool boundaries. We present a six-phase orchestration methodology and four portability metrics. A 5-month formative case study (November 2025 to March 2026) documents a single practitioner applying these skills across a ten-component orchestration stack spanning seven professional domains, producing work products that would traditionally involve separate domain specialists. Two quantitative observations are consistent with the framework's predictions: a Cochran-Armitage trend test (n = 200 interactions across two chat LLMs, p < 0.01) shows first-pass acceptance rising with prompt-sophistication level, and a Wright's Law fit (n = 82 artifacts, p < 0.01) shows production acceleration across the artifact portfolio. Because all observations come from a single practitioner, the inferential statistics are exploratory and hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory; portability across the full portfolio awaits multi-practitioner replication. Augment Engineering completes a three-discipline progression: Prompt Engineering (one tool), Context Engineering (reproducible pipelines), Augment Engineering (a portfolio of tools across domains).

</details>


### [126] [Gamified Requirement Elicitation for a Multi-Modal Decision Support System. The Case of SYNCHROMODE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26164)
*Dimitris Tzanis,Alexandros Dolianitis,Viktoria Petkani,Areti Kotsi,Evangelos Mitsakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The SYNCHROMODE project focuses on the requirement elicitation phase for a multimodal transport decision support system. The study compares traditional use case methods with a gamification-based serious game approach for gathering requirements from diverse stakeholders in a workshop setting.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Developing complex multimodal transport management systems requires careful requirement elicitation to establish foundational system needs and expectations, particularly for interconnected services in the SYNCHROMODE toolbox.

Method: The study employs two parallel requirement elicitation approaches: (1) traditional methods using use cases during the design phase, and (2) a gamification-based approach using a serious game conducted during a physical workshop in Thessaloniki (October 2023). Stakeholders from diverse backgrounds (traffic management experts, public transport authorities, MaaS providers, first responders, ITS technology providers, and local authorities) participated in the gamification workshop.

Result: A set of predefined requirements was identified through use cases. A requirement elicitation serious game was designed, developed, and successfully deployed in a workshop with diverse stakeholders.

Conclusion: The paper compares requirements gathered through traditional use case methods with those obtained through gamification-based elicitation, providing insights into the effectiveness of different requirement gathering approaches for complex multimodal transport management systems.

Abstract: SYNCHROMODE is a Horizon Europe project that aims to develop a data-driven ICT toolbox for improving the management of transport operations from a multimodal perspective. This is, in essence, a multimodal decision support system that will take the form of interconnected pieces of software. Developing complex systems requires careful planning and management. Their "life cycle" consists of several phases, starting with the critical stage of requirement elicitation, which establishes the system's foundational needs and expectations. This paper focuses on the requirement elicitation phase of core interconnected services that are to be offered through the SYNCHROMODE toolbox. Elicitation is achieved both through traditional methods and specifically through the use of use cases as well as through the use of gamification. During the design phase of the toolbox and as a result of the use cases a set of predefined requirements were identified. Moreover, a requirement elicitation serious game was designed and developed. Subsequently, the game was presented in person during a physical workshop, which took place in Thessaloniki in October 2023, to a diverse range of stakeholders, including experts in traffic management, public transport authorities, MaaS providers, first responders, ITS technology providers, and local authorities. Both requirement elicitation processes are summarized in the context of this paper. Finally, the requirements generated through traditional means and through gamification are then analyzed and compared.

</details>


### [127] [A Universal Cliff and a Design Fingerprint: Cross-Section Defect Detection Under LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26174)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了LLM编排系统在检测跨文档分区缺陷上的系统性失败：检测悬崖是机制衍生的且不可通过scale解决；最对齐的系统表现出报告标准偏移（漏检减少但误报增加）；集成报告置信度对跨分区缺陷无信息量，自动化检测不可靠。这挑战了"对齐=安全"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多智能体编排系统对单一智能体可见的缺陷类别的影响：文档中两个远距离段落之间的矛盾（跨分区缺陷）。当系统通过不可见的worker agents编排来回答请求并重新组合成一份综合报告时，这种缺陷无法被单一worker检测到。

Method: 固定文档、缺陷机制、评分和种子，仅变化模型——来自同一开发者的五代十种系统，以及来自不同对齐范式的五个提供商。使用信号检测分解分析模型行为，区分报告标准和辨别能力的轴向变化。

Result: 发现两层现象：1）普遍检测悬崖：所有在单智能体下能检测跨分区缺陷的模型，在编排下失去该能力（检测率下降三分之二以上），且该悬崖源于机制本身，不能通过规模化或扩展推理关闭；2）行为分化：六个高于随机水平的模型中，仅有一个开发者的模型沿报告标准轴移动——对齐强化时，漏检减少但误报增加（p<0.001），其他开发者几乎无此现象。底层机制上，模型的私有记录能准确重建结构性故障，但综合报告签字认可其正确性。

Conclusion: 编排架构在跨段落缺陷检测上存在结构性缺陷，无法通过扩展推理或规模化解决。集成报告的置信度无法反映分区跨越缺陷，对齐最强的系统并非最安全的系统。检测器的自动化评判不稳定（精确率17-50%），且无法通过关键词与普通一致性区分。

Abstract: Production language-model systems answer a request by partitioning it across an invisible orchestration of worker agents that recompose one integrated report. We ask what this does to a class of defect no single worker can see: a contradiction in the relation between two distant sections of a document. Holding the documents, defects, mechanism, scoring, and seed fixed, we vary only the model -- ten systems across five generations from one developer and five providers from distinct alignment paradigms.
  Two layers separate. First, a universal detection cliff: every model that finds these cross-section defects under a single agent loses that ability under orchestration, detection falling two-thirds or more across every paradigm tested. The cliff is mechanism-derived and not closed by scale or extended reasoning. Second, how models behave once fallen. A signal-detection decomposition shows that, among the six models discriminating above chance, only one developer's generations move along the reporting-criterion axis: as alignment is strengthened, the model misses fewer defects yet raises more false alarms on clean documents -- two faces of one criterion shift, scaling with generation within that developer (p < 0.001) and near-absent elsewhere.
  At the floor the missed defect is often not out of view: the model's private record reconstructs the structural fault accurately, while the integrated report signs off on its soundness, its concern spent on the artifact and an absent collaborator. This resists quantification -- an automated judge is unstable (precision 17-50%) and keywords cannot separate it from ordinary agreement -- a resistance we report as a finding. We release all runs, probes, defect keys, scorer prompts, and scripts. An integrated report's confidence is uninformative about partition-spanning defects, the most aligned systems are not the safest, and the cliff is structural.

</details>


### [128] [SetupX: Can LLM Agents Learn from Past Failures in Functionality-Correct Code Repository Setup?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26186)
*Zihang Zhou,Ziqian Ren,Yukai Wu,Yingjie Xiong,Wei Zhou,Chao Peng,Dong Zhang,Bingheng Yan,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SetupX is an experiential learning-based framework for automating repository setup configuration. It addresses three key limitations of existing LLM agents through dual-modality experience representation (XPU), speculative execution with Docker-based rollback (LIFO snapshot stack), and a separated verification protocol (Prosecutor-Judge). The system achieves 92% pass rate with 19% improvement over state-of-the-art baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Functionality-correct repository setup presents significant challenges due to diverse repository-specific failures (dependency incompatibilities, missing toolchains, incomplete installations, verification-strategy mismatches). Existing LLM agents fail to robustly resolve these issues in three aspects: cross-repository experience transfer, multi-step trial-and-repair under non-invertible state changes, and robust verification to distinguish setup-induced failures from repository bugs.

Method: SetupX proposes three core mechanisms: (1) XPU (Self-Evolving Experience Representation) - a dual-modality knowledge unit encoding setup signals, textual guidance and executable actions for cross-repository knowledge transfer; (2) Experience-Augmented Speculative Execution backed by LIFO Docker snapshot stack for safe trial-and-repair with rollback capability; (3) Prosecutor-Judge Verification Protocol separating evidence collection from final judgment for robust outcome verification.

Result: SetupX achieves 92% pass rate on carefully-crafted benchmarks, outperforming the strongest baseline by over 19%. The framework particularly excels in complex multi-repository setup requiring coordination of multiple interconnected services across different containers.

Conclusion: SetupX demonstrates significant practical value for automating complex repository setup tasks, particularly excelling in multi-repository scenarios requiring coordination of interconnected services. The open-source availability of the framework enables further research and application.

Abstract: Functionality-correct repository setup aims to configure execution environments (e.g., dependencies, build scripts) to successfully execute a repository's documented features. It presents significant challenges due to diverse, repository-specific failures, including dependency incompatibilities, missing toolchains, incomplete installations, and verification-strategy mismatches. Existing LLM agents struggle to robustly resolve these issues, specifically failing to support (1) cross-repository experience transfer, (2) multi-step trial-and-repair under non-invertible state changes, and (3) robust verification of setup outcomes to distinguish setup-induced failures from repository bugs. To address this, we introduce SetupX, an experiential learning-based setup framework. First, we construct a Self-Evolving Experience Representation (XPU), a dual-modality knowledge unit encoding setup signals, textual guidance, executable actions to dynamically transfer verified environment fixes to unseen repositories. Second, we employ Experience-Augmented Speculative Execution backed by a LIFO Docker snapshot stack, enabling the agent to proactively trial fixes and safely roll back to known-good states. Third, we introduce a Prosecutor-Judge Verification Protocol that separates evidence collection from final judgment, enabling more reliable setup verification beyond superficial build-time metrics. Evaluation results on carefully-crafted benchmarks show SetupX achieves highest performance (e.g., 92% pass rate) and outperforms the strongest baseline by over 19%. Crucially, SetupX excels in complex multi-repository setup requiring coordinating multiple interconnected services across different containers. The code repository is available at https://github.com/OpenDataBox/SetupX.

</details>


### [129] [Verus-SpecGym: An Agentic Environment for Evaluating Specification Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26457)
*Anmol Agarwal,Natalie Neamtu,Pranjal Aggarwal,Seungone Kim,Jannis Limperg,Cedric Flamant,Kanna Shimizu,Bryan Parno,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究规范自动形式化问题，即LLM智能体能否将非形式化编程问题转化为忠实形式规范。提出Verus-SpecBench（581个任务）和Verus-SpecGym环境，通过exec_spec机制实现自动化评估。Gemini 3.1 Pro达77.8%准确率，开源模型仅21-25%。研究表明前沿模型的规范生成能力已可实用，但在边缘情况和评判机制上仍存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体虽能生成带形式化证明的代码，但形式化规范本身可能偏离用户意图。规范自动形式化(spec autoformalization)——将非形式化编程问题转化为忠实的形式化规范——缺乏系统性研究，主要瓶颈在于评估困难：专家编写参考规范成本高，LLM评判可能遗漏细微错误。

Method: (1) 构建Verus-SpecBench基准：581个源自Codeforces问题的规范编写任务，基于Rust验证器Verus；(2) 开发Verus-SpecGym智能体环境，支持模型与Verus、bash及文件系统交互；(3) 扩展Verus的exec_spec机制使生成的规范可作为Rust代码执行；(4) 利用官方Codeforces测试用例及"hacks"对抗性用例进行自动化评估，规避依赖专家参考规范和高成本LLM评判的问题。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro解决77.8%任务，其他前沿模型51.1-57.8%，开源模型仅21.5-25.5%。失败模式包括：遗漏关键输入假设、接受错误输出、拒绝正确输出。LLM评判机制会遗漏评估器捕获的26%失败案例。

Conclusion: Spec autoformalization对于前沿模型已可实现（Gemini 3.1 Pro达77.8%），但对开源模型仍有较大差距（21.5-25.5%）。生成的规范存在遗漏输入假设、接受错误输出、拒绝正确输出等问题，且LLM评判机制会遗漏26%的失败案例，表明该任务在模型已能生成正确代码的问题上仍具脆弱性。

Code: https://github.com/formal-verif-is-cool/verus-spec-gym

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly used to write real-world software, but ensuring that their outputs are correct remains a fundamental challenge. Formal verification offers a promising path: an agent generates code together with a machine-checked proof, guaranteeing that the code satisfies a formal specification. However, there is no guarantee that the formal spec itself matches the user's intent. In this work, we study specification autoformalization: whether LLM agents can translate informal programming problems into faithful formal specifications. We introduce Verus-SpecBench, a benchmark of 581 spec-writing tasks derived from Codeforces problems targeting Verus, a verifier for Rust, and Verus-SpecGym, an agentic environment in which models interact with Verus, bash, & the filesystem to develop these specs. The central challenge is evaluation: expert-written reference specs are expensive to write, & LLM judges can miss subtle mistakes. We address this by (a) extending Verus's exec_spec mechanism so that generated specs can be executed as Rust code, & (b) testing them against official Codeforces tests & adversarial cases extracted from Codeforces "hacks", which are edge cases written by competitors to break incorrect solutions. On Verus-SpecBench, the strongest model, Gemini 3.1 Pro, solves 77.8% of tasks, other frontier models solve 51.1--57.8% & OSS models reach only 21.5--25.5%. Our analysis of failure modes shows that model-generated specs can omit important input assumptions, accept incorrect outputs, & reject valid ones. We also find that LLM-as-a-judge evaluation misses 26% of the failures our evaluator catches. Overall, our results suggest that spec autoformalization is within reach for frontier agents but remains brittle even on problems where they can already generate correct code. The code, data, & logs can be found at https://github.com/formal-verif-is-cool/verus-spec-gym

</details>


### [130] [Testing Agentic Workflows with Structural Coverage Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26521)
*Nafiseh Kahani,Mojtaba Bagherzadeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对多智能体工作流规格的结构测试方法，通过协调图覆盖率驱动与DSPy场景实现，验证工作流声明的结构（智能体、工具访问规则、限制、委托路径）是否被实际执行，为多智能体测试提供除语义和端到端评估之外的充分性评估层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统评估主要依赖端到端任务成功率、基准分数或最终响应质量，缺乏对声明式协调结构（智能体、工具、访问规则、限制、委托路径）是否真正被执行的有效验证，难以评估测试套件充分性或检测结构性的工具访问、退避和智能体间委托回归。

Method: 将工作流表示为类型化协调图（typed coordination graph），从中导出覆盖义务（包括可达智能体、可允许工具边、受限工具边和委托边）；采用覆盖率驱动的生成方法，利用DSPy框架实现场景生成，将这些义务转化为自然语言场景，并通过运行时检查验证见证者（witnesses）。

Result: 在10个SDK衍生基准上测试（49个可达智能体、47个工具、403个结构义务），生成场景在有限优化预算内见证了54/75个可允许工具义务和36/48个委托义务；对抗性受限工具标准触发23/248个受限调用违规，成功区分了限制在探测下成立的系统与存在具体错误路由故障的系统。

Conclusion: 结构覆盖率（structural coverage）为多智能体工作流测试提供了一个有用的充分性评估层。它并不替代语义或端到端评估，但能揭示所声明的智能体、工具访问规则、限制和委托路径是否真正被执行。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems increasingly expose explicit workflow structure: agents, tools, tool-access rules, restrictions, and delegation paths. Existing evaluations rely largely on end-to-end task success, benchmark scores, final-response quality, or prompt-level checks, which provide limited evidence that this declared coordination structure has actually been exercised. This makes it difficult to assess test-suite adequacy or detect structural regressions in tool access, restrictions, and inter-agent delegation. We address this gap with a structural testing approach for multi-agent workflow specifications. The approach represents each workflow as a typed coordination graph, derives coverage obligations over reachable agents, allowed tool edges, restricted tool edges, and delegation edges, and uses coverage-driven generation with DSPy-based scenario realization to produce executable tests. The graph fixes what must be covered; DSPy realizes those obligations as natural-language scenarios whose witnesses are checked at runtime. We implement the approach for OpenAI Agents SDK-style workflows and evaluate it on ten SDK-derived benchmarks comprising 49 reachable agents, 47 tools, and 403 structural obligations. Generated scenarios witness 54/75 allowed-tool obligations and 36/48 delegation obligations within a bounded refinement budget. The adversarial restricted-tool criterion elicits 23/248 restricted-call violations, separating workflows whose restrictions hold under probing from workflows with concrete misrouting failures. These results show that structural coverage provides a useful adequacy layer for multi-agent workflow testing: it does not replace semantic or end-to-end evaluation, but reveals whether declared agents, tool-access rules, restrictions, and delegation paths have been exercised.

</details>


### [131] [Governed Evolution of Agent Runtimes through Executable Operational Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27328)
*Mariano Garralda-Barrio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This paper addresses the governance gap in agentic systems where code is treated as executable runtime substrate. It proposes a framework for governed runtime evolution, formalizing agent artifacts as persistent capabilities and introducing HarnessMutation as a lifecycle-aware adaptation mechanism with validation, traceability, evaluation, and rollback constraints.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work (Code as Agent Harness) treats agent-generated artifacts as runtime entities, but governance, lifecycle management, and operational evolution remain under-specified.

Method: Formalizes agent-generated artifacts as persistent runtime capabilities; introduces HarnessMutation as a governed mechanism operating under validation, traceability, evaluation, and rollback constraints; models evolution as bounded and observable process over persistent operational memory.

Result: Proposes a framework enabling runtime adaptation while maintaining explicitness, auditability, and constraints.

Conclusion: Provides a conceptual foundation for adaptive infrastructures whose evolution remains explicit, auditable, and constrained; operationalized through modern agent runtimes and governance-oriented orchestration systems.

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic systems increasingly treat code as an executable operational substrate rather than as a disposable output artifact. Prior work such as \emph{Code as Agent Harness} frames validated agent-generated artifacts as runtime entities that can be created, executed, revised, persisted, and reused within long-running cognitive loops. However, the governance, lifecycle management, and operational evolution of such artifacts remain under-specified.
  This paper proposes a framework for governed runtime evolution in multi-agent systems through executable operational cognition.
