We formalize agent-generated artifacts as persistent runtime capabilities that progressively become part of the operational substrate rather than transient intermediate outputs. Building on this perspective, we introduce \emph{HarnessMutation} as a governed mechanism for lifecycle-aware runtime adaptation operating under explicit validation, traceability, evaluation, and rollback constraints.
  Rather than treating runtime adaptation as unrestricted self-modification, the proposed framework models evolution as a bounded and observable process over persistent operational memory. It further shows how these ideas can be operationalized over modern agent runtimes and governance-oriented orchestration systems, providing a conceptual foundation for adaptive infrastructures whose evolution remains explicit, auditable, and constrained.

</details>


### [132] [TrajAudit: Automated Failure Diagnosis for Agentic Coding Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26563)
*Minxing Wang,Xiaofei Xie,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajAudit，首个用于仓库级编码轨迹的失败诊断框架。该框架通过信息过滤和先验知识注入解决长轨迹噪声问题，实验表明其在准确率和效率上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化失败诊断方法在轨迹长度和复杂度增加时效果显著下降。仓库级编码任务轨迹充满噪声（冗余程序结构、冗长代码上下文），且轨迹非常长，而长上下文推理正是LLM的已知弱点。

Method: TrajAudit框架包含一个调查员智能体(investigator agent)，配备两个核心模块：(1)通过模式匹配和关键词检测过滤失败无关信息；(2)从测试失败报告中生成初步诊断作为先验知识。调查员智能体还可按需调用工具检索过滤后的内容，保留关键信息同时最小化噪声。

Result: 在RootSE基准测试上，TrajAudit在定位准确率上超过所有基线方法24.4个百分点以上，同时将token消耗降低至少18%。

Conclusion: TrajAudit demonstrates practical effectiveness for failure diagnosis in agentic software engineering, outperforming baselines while reducing token consumption. The work draws community attention to failure management in agentic SE and provides RootSE as a foundational resource for future research.

Abstract: Agentic systems have been widely studied to automate software engineering jobs such as bug fixing. As these systems increasingly tackle complex tasks, understanding where and why they fail becomes essential for iterative refinement and operational reliability. Existing automated failure diagnosis approaches leverage task execution trajectories, yet their effectiveness degrades substantially as trajectory length and complexity increase. For repository-level coding tasks specifically, trajectories are laden with noise, such as redundant program structure and verbose code context. Moreover, these trajectories are very long, while long-context reasoning remains a known weakness of LLMs.
  To address these two challenges, we propose TrajAudit, the first failure diagnosis framework for repository-level coding trajectories. TrajAudit employs an investigator agent supported by two modules: one filters failure-irrelevant information through pattern matching and keyword detection, and the other generates a preliminary diagnosis from test failure reports as prior knowledge, helping the agent handle noisy long contexts. The investigator agent can further invoke tools to retrieve filtered content on demand, ensuring that critical information is preserved while noise is minimized. We also introduce RootSE, a benchmark of 93 real-world agentic failure instances sourced from software maintenance tasks, representing the most complex trajectory diagnosis benchmark to date. Experiments on RootSE show that TrajAudit outperforms all existing baselines by over 24.4 percentage points in localization accuracy, while reducing token consumption by at least 18%, demonstrating its practical effectiveness. We hope this work draws community attention to failure management in agentic software engineering and provides a foundational resource for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Energy-Aware Decision Making in Software Stack Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26609)
*Mirko Stocker,Michael Wahler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种系统化方法，通过自动化基准测试测量Spring Boot和JVM等核心软件组件不同版本对应用能耗的影响；研究发现能耗在不同版本间差异显著，较新JVM版本和虚拟线程可带来显著节能，表明软件升级决策应纳入能耗考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件栈升级虽常见，但升级对能耗的影响尚不清晰，而能耗正成为组织追求可持续发展的关注重点。

Method: 通过自动化基准测试评估框架版本、运行时版本和执行平台的组合，使用Spring Petclinic REST应用作为案例研究。

Result: Spring Boot和JVM版本间的能耗存在显著差异，部分情况下出现意外的能耗回退；较新的JVM版本和虚拟线程（Java 21和23引入）带来显著节能，无需修改应用代码。

Conclusion: 软件升级会对能耗产生实质影响，能耗测量为软件维护和演进的决策提供了有价值的数据支撑；组织在进行软件栈升级时，应将能耗纳入评估维度。

Abstract: Software stack upgrades are a routine part of software maintenance and evolution, typically motivated by improved performance, stability, or functionality. Yet their impact on energy consumption - a growing concern for organizations pursuing sustainability - remains poorly understood. This paper presents a systematic method for measuring how different versions of core software stack components, such as Spring Boot and the Java Virtual Machine (JVM), influence the energy consumption of applications. Our approach evaluates combinations of framework versions, runtime versions, and execution platforms through automated benchmarking. Using a case study based on the Spring Petclinic REST application, we show that energy consumption varies substantially across Spring Boot and JVM versions, in some cases producing unexpected regressions. Notably, newer JVM releases and virtual threads (introduced in Java 21 and 23) yielded significant energy savings without requiring application changes. These results demonstrate that software upgrades can meaningfully affect energy usage and that measuring energy consumption provides valuable evidence for decision making in software maintenance and evolution.

</details>


### [134] [Software Engineering Podcasts: An Empirical Study of Their Potential as a Research Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26793)
*Marvin Wyrich,Marcos Kalinowski,Adolfo Neto,Sven Apel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究系统性地探索软件工程播客领域，分析其内容，并对研究人员进行调查，以评估播客作为推进实证软件工程研究的资源价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 播客在软件工程社区中日益流行，但目前对SE播客的实际内容以及研究人员如何看待和使用它们知之甚少，亟需系统性的了解。

Method: 系统性地分析SE播客内容，并对研究人员进行调查。

Result: 分析SE播客景观，评估其作为研究资源的潜力。

Conclusion: SE播客不仅是从业者的有价值资源，也可能成为研究人员理解该领域演变的有效工具。

Abstract: Podcasts have become an increasingly popular medium for knowledge sharing within the software engineering (SE) community, offering insights into industry developments and the perspectives of professionals with different backgrounds. As this medium grows, it presents a potentially valuable resource not only for practitioners but also for researchers seeking to understand the evolving field. However, little is known about the actual content of SE podcasts or how they are perceived and used by researchers. This study systematically explores the SE podcast landscape, analyzing its content and surveying researchers to assess how podcasts can serve as a meaningful resource for advancing empirical software engineering research.

</details>


### [135] [HTMLCure: Turning Browser Experience into State Guided Repair for Interactive HTML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26807)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Tuney Zheng,Wei Zhang,Haowen Wang,Yihang Lou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HTMLCure通过交互式浏览器评估和闭环修复机制，将97K提示词扩展为63K质量清理页面并最终筛选出40K高质量SFT数据，使27B模型达到与Kimi-K2.6/GPT-5.4相当的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成的HTML页面仅在初始渲染时正确，但在滚动、悬停、点击、调整大小或交互操作时容易失败。基于截图的评估方法无法检测这些交互故障，而简单过滤会丢弃大量仍可修复的页面。

Method: HTMLCure是一个浏览器体验框架，包含评估引擎和闭环修复引擎。评估引擎在多种视口和交互状态下执行页面，记录确定性浏览器证据并提取curated keyframes；修复引擎基于诊断结果选择状态特定的修复方案，对候选页面进行迭代测试和筛选。

Result: 从97K提示词扩展到63,703个质量清理后的候选页面，最终构建40K高质量SFT数据集。在HTMLBench-400上达到50.6分（45.2%确定性测试通过率），在MiniAppBench验证集上达到81.2平均分，较原始27B SFT提升15.3点。

Conclusion: HTMLCure enables a 27B model to reach performance comparable to strong reference systems (Kimi-K2.6, GPT-5.4) through quality-cleared SFT data, demonstrating that systematic interaction evaluation and targeted repair significantly enhance generated web pages without architectural changes.

Abstract: LLMs can now produce full HTML pages, but many of those pages are only superficially correct: they render once, then fail under scroll, hover, click, resize, or gameplay. Evaluation from screenshots can miss these failures, and filtering discards many pages that are still repairable. We introduce HTMLCure, a browser experience framework that evaluates HTML after the system has interacted with it. The evaluator executes the page across viewports and interaction states, records deterministic browser evidence, and gives the VLM curated keyframes from the executed trajectory rather than isolated screenshots. The same state signal drives a closed loop repair engine: HTMLCure diagnoses the current page, chooses a state specific repair family, runs each candidate again, and exports quality cleared pages for SFT. On a 97K prompt corpus, this expands the directly usable seed into a candidate pool of 63703 quality cleared pages, from which we construct the final refined SFT set of 40K pages. Under the same backbone and training recipe, HTMLCure-27B-Refined reaches 50.6 on HTMLBench-400 with 45.2% deterministic test case pass, placing it in the same performance band as strong reference rows such as Kimi-K2.6 and GPT-5.4. On the released MiniAppBench validation split, it reaches 81.2 average, improving raw 27B SFT by 15.3 points and approaching the level of strong reference systems.

</details>


### [136] [On the GitHub Actions Language: Usage, Evolution, and Workflow Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26825)
*Aref Talebzadeh Bardsiri,Alexandre Decan,Tom Mens*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过对49K仓库中260K个工作流进行大规模实证分析，揭示GitHub Actions语言构造的使用模式和演化趋势，为改善工作流自动化实践提供实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在维护GitHub Actions工作流配置时面临困难，近期研究表明工作流执行失败现象频繁发生，亟需深入理解语言特性对工作流可靠性和可维护性的影响。

Method: 对49K GitHub仓库中的260K个工作流进行定量分析，时间跨度从2019年7月到2025年8月。将197个语言构造映射到14个功能特性，分析其使用模式及演变趋势。

Result: 仅少量语言构造和特性被高频使用；更大更复杂的工作流关联更高失败率和更多维护工作量；识别出与可靠性和可维护性风险相关的特定特性。

Conclusion: 研究发现，只有少部分语言构造和特性被高频使用，而更大更复杂的工作流与更高的失败率和维护成本相关。识别出特定特性与可靠性和可维护性风险相关，为从业者和研究人员提供了改进GitHub Actions自动化实践的见解。

Abstract: Developers often struggle with maintaining GitHub Actions workflow configurations in GitHub-hosted repositories, with recent studies showing frequent execution failures. This paper empirically explores how the adoption and evolution of GitHub Actions language constructs impacts workflow reliability and maintainability. To do so, we quantitatively analyse 260K workflows from 49K GitHub repositories to understand how they are used in practice and how their usage has evolved from July 2019 to August 2025. We identify 197 language constructs available in the GitHub Actions language and map them to 14 features reflecting workflow capabilities. We observe that only a small set of constructs and features are used very frequently, and that larger and more complex workflows are associated with higher failure rates and more maintenance effort. We identify specific features that are more likely to be linked with reliability and maintainability risks. These insights can help practitioners and researchers improve their understanding and usage of the GitHub Actions language, which can help in improving and sustaining workflow automation practices.

</details>


### [137] [LLM-based Mockless Unit Test Generation for Java](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26851)
*Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Lionel Briand,Zhaoqiang Guo,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MocklessTester通过上下文增强和约束强制修复策略解决了LLM生成无模拟器单元测试时的幻 hall闭问题，在多个基准上显著提升覆盖率但计算成本较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的Java单元测试生成方法仍依赖模拟框架，而无模拟器测试可执行更多真实底层代码，但面临幻 hall闭生成、语言约束不遵循、依赖感知不足等挑战，这些挑战源于"不知道"（缺乏足够上下文）和"不遵循"（即使提供约束也无法遵守）两类问题。

Method: 提出MocklessTester，采用上下文增强生成策略（从现有代码中挖掘真实使用模式）和约束强制修复策略（符号级、协议级、迭代级三层约束的两阶段修复），并结合ClassIndex、Markov类型状态模型和经验记忆库。

Result: 在Defects4J和Deps4J上，MocklessTester分别提升行覆盖率19.99%/22.69%、分支覆盖率24.90%/15.78%、突变评分13.67%/0.17%；额外覆盖依赖类378和55行代码；Defects4J上平均每方法耗时108.97秒/26.59k tokens，Deps4J上69.85秒/25.46k tokens；消融实验验证各主要组件均正向贡献。

Conclusion: MocklessTester显著提升了无模拟器测试生成的质量，覆盖率和突变评分均大幅改善，虽然计算成本较高但仍在实际范围内，且各主要组件均对性能有正向贡献。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for automated test generation, yet most approaches to generating Java unit tests still rely on mocking frameworks to handle dependencies. Mockless test generation could exercise more real low-level code, but it faces challenges such as invalid test code generation due to hallucination, strict language constraints, and inadequate dependency awareness. We identify two causes behind these hallucinations: not knowing, where the LLM lacks sufficient context, and not following, where the LLM fails to comply with constraints even when they are provided.
  We present MocklessTester, a mockless unit test generation approach built around two strategies: context-enriched generation and constraint-enforced fixing. To mitigate not knowing, context-enriched generation mines real usage patterns from existing code to generate tests. To mitigate not following, constraint-enforced fixing performs two-stage repair under symbol-, protocol-, and iteration-level constraints, using a ClassIndex, a Markov typestate model, and experience memory.
  We evaluate MocklessTester against the state-of-the-art baseline on Defects4J and Deps4J. Results show that MocklessTester improves line coverage by 19.99% and 22.69% and branch coverage by 24.90% and 15.78% on the two benchmarks, respectively, and improves mutation score by 13.67% and 0.17%. Beyond the class under test, MocklessTester also exercises more real dependency code, covering 378 and 55 additional lines in dependency classes, respectively. The improvement in test quality comes with higher total token and time costs than the baseline. Nevertheless, the cost per method remains practical, averaging 108.97 seconds and 26.59k tokens on Defects4J, and 69.85 seconds and 25.46k tokens on Deps4J. Ablation results confirm that all major components contribute positively to the final performance.

</details>


### [138] [Strategies for Guiding LLMs to Use Software Design Patterns: A Case of Singleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26898)
*Viktor Kjellberg,Farnaz Fotrousi,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何引导LLM在生成的代码中融入设计模式，以Singleton模式为案例，在13个LLM上测试了四种提示策略。结果表明迭代二元反馈总体效果最佳，且即使简单策略也能有效指导LLM应用设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能根据自然语言提示生成功能性源代码，但往往无法一致地遵循高层架构结构或设计模式。随着LLM在软件工程领域的应用日益广泛，其将既定设计原则应用于生成代码的能力对软件产品的长期成功至关重要。

Method: 设计计算实验，在164个Java编码挑战题（来自HumanEval-X）上评估13个LLM使用四种提示策略生成符合Singleton设计模式代码的能力。四种策略包括：指令式、二元自动反馈、扩展自动反馈，以及结合少样本提示的扩展反馈。

Result: 最佳引导策略高度依赖于模型类型。总体而言，迭代二元反馈在保持或提升代码功能性同时，实现了与Singleton模式的最佳对齐。Llama 3.3通过指令引导在100%的情况下生成Singleton类，测试通过率提升34.1个百分点。Qwen 3 (8B)使用二元反馈后，Singleton对齐度达99.2%，功能性达58.6%。

Conclusion: 即使简单的策略也能有效引导LLM在生成的代码中使用设计模式。研究表明，不同模型对策略的响应差异较大，但迭代二元反馈总体上能实现Singleton模式对齐与代码功能性的最佳平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate functional source code from natural-language prompts, but often fail to consistently follow higher-level architectural structures or design patterns. Since LLMs are increasingly used in software engineering, their ability to apply established design principles to generated code is crucial to the long-term success of software products. Therefore, the goal of this paper is to identify strategies for guiding LLMs to incorporate design patterns into the generated source code. We designed a computational experiment to evaluate the ability of 13 LLMs to generate code that follows the Singleton design pattern, using four prompting strategies: instructions, binary automated feedback, extensive automated feedback, and extensive feedback with few-shot prompts, in 164 Java coding challenges from HumanEval-X. Our results shows that the optimal strategy to guide LLMs to include design patterns depends heavily on the type of model. Still, overall, iterative binary feedback provides the best alignment with Singleton while preserving or improving the code's functionality. With guiding with instructions, Llama 3.3 generated Singleton classes in 100% of cases and improved code functionality, increasing the number of tests passed by 34.1 percentage points. It achieved a similar result with guidance through instructions and binary feedback. Qwen 3 (8B) increased the alignment with Singleton to 99.2% and the functionality to 58.6% using binary feedback. Our result suggests that even simple strategies can be used to guide LLMs to use design patterns.

</details>


### [139] [EviACT: An Evidence-to-Action Framework for Agentic Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27238)
*Qianru Meng,Xiao Zhang,Zhaochun Ren,Joost Visser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EviACT提出了一种基于证据驱动的智能程序修复框架，通过三个阶段的防护机制（检索脚手架、编译门控、测试驱动门控）来协调证据到行动的转化，显著提升了自动程序修复的解决率和成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing agentic APR systems struggle to effectively use execution evidence to guide the key repair stages: localization, patch generation, and validation.

Method: EviACT coordinates three evidence-driven guardrails across repair stages: (1) retrieval scaffold for grounding repair context, (2) compile gate for filtering invalid edits, and (3) test-driven gate for checking target-test recovery before full regression.

Result: Across four benchmarks, EviACT improves resolve rate over the strongest comparable baselines by 1.6-6.0 percentage points and achieves 70.1-88.6% lower per-bug API cost.

Conclusion: The coordinated evidence-to-action chain in EviACT enables more effective and efficient agentic APR, with improvements in both resolve rate (1.6-6.0 percentage points) and cost efficiency (70.1-88.6% lower API cost).

Abstract: LLM-based agents have moved automated program repair (APR) from fixed-context patch generation to interactive repository-level repair. However, existing agentic APR systems still struggle to use execution evidence to guide localization, patch generation, and validation. We propose EviACT (Evidence-to-Action), an agentic APR framework that coordinates three evidence-driven guardrails across repair stages. The retrieval scaffold grounds repair context, the compile gate filters invalid edits, and the test-driven gate checks target-test recovery before full regression. Across four benchmarks, EviACT improves resolve rate over the strongest reported comparable baselines by 1.6-6.0 percentage points and shows 70.1-88.6% lower reported per-bug API cost where baseline costs are available. Ablations and diagnostics suggest that these gains are associated with the coordinated evidence-to-action chain, making agentic APR more effective and efficient.

</details>


### [140] [EdgeFlow: Edge-Map Augmented VLM-Based Flowchart Processing for Industrial Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27332)
*Zhifei Dou,Shabnam Hassani,Ou Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EdgeFlow通过将Canny边缘图作为结构先验增强VLM输入，无需训练数据或微调即可显著提升工业需求流程图的拓扑保持转换性能，为基于模型的测试提供更好支持。但该方法在合成基准数据集上无显著提升，凸显了当前评估框架缺乏工业数据多样性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flowcharts are widely used in industrial requirements but remain embedded as static images. Vision Language Models show promise for converting these flowcharts into machine-readable models for RE activities, yet they often fail on topology-critical visual details when directly applied to flowchart conversion.

Method: EdgeFlow augments a Vision Language Model's original input with a deterministically extracted Canny edge map that serves as a structural prior, without requiring annotated training data or domain-specific model fine-tuning. This edge map provides topology-critical visual details that VLMs typically miss when processing flowcharts.

Result: On the IndusReqFlow dataset (real-world requirements), EdgeFlow improves node-level F1 by 17.39 percentage points, edge-level F1 by 16.94 percentage points, and path-level F1 by 11.06 percentage points compared to off-the-shelf VLMs, enabling better support for model-based testing. However, cross-dataset evaluation on a public synthetic benchmark shows no significant improvement.

Conclusion: EdgeFlow provides a training-free solution for improving topology-preserving flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion in industrial RE, with significant improvements across node-level, edge-level, and path-level F1 scores on real-world industrial data. However, the method's ineffectiveness on synthetic benchmarks indicates that current evaluation frameworks lack the diversity needed to comprehensively assess VLM-based RE tools for industrial applications.

Abstract: Flowcharts are widely used in industrial requirements, but usually remain embedded as static images. Vision Language Models (VLMs) show promise in the conversion of these flowcharts into machine-readable models for RE activities, yet, when directly applied to flowchart conversion, they often fail on topology-critical visual details. To address this, we propose EdgeFlow that augments a VLM's original input with a deterministically extracted Canny edge map-acting as a structural prior-to improve flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion, without requiring annotated training data or domain-specific model fine-tuning.
  We evaluate EdgeFlow on IndusReqFlow, a dataset sourced from real-world requirements. Compared with off-the-shelf VLMs, EdgeFlow improves node-level F1 by 17.39 percentage points and edge-level F1 by 16.94 percentage points. At the path level, EdgeFlow improves path F1 by 11.06 percentage points, enabling better support for model-based testing. These results demonstrate that EdgeFlow provides a practical, training-free means to improve topology-preserving flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion for industrial RE. Cross-dataset evaluation results on a public synthetic benchmark show no significant improvement; this highlights the need for diverse benchmarks incorporating industrial data for the comprehensive evaluation of future VLM-based RE tools.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Generalized Range Filtering Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Containment and Overlap [Technical Report]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26474)
*Yingfan Liu,Tong Wu,Jiadong Xie,Yang Zhao,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Jiangtao Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本论文研究了带范围-范围（RR）谓词的近似最近邻搜索问题（RRANN），每个向量关联一个数值范围，查询需要找出满足任意RR谓词条件的最近邻向量。为此提出了多段树图（MSTG）方法，通过避免遍历不满足谓词的节点来高效支持任意RR谓词，在真实数据上实现最高12.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索方法无法支持任意定义的RR谓词（如包含、重叠或其组合），而实际应用中对价格区间、时间间隔等范围查询需求广泛，需要一种通用方法来处理用户自定义的任意RR谓词条件。

Method: 采用多段树图（MSTG）结构，通过避免遍历不满足谓词的节点来高效处理任意RR谓词。该方法保持了与RFANN最优方法相当的索引大小和构建时间复杂度。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在RRANN查询上实现了最高12.5倍的加速且保持相同精度；在RFANN上达到可比性能，在IFANN和TSANN上显著优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的多段树图（MSTG）方法，能够高效处理任意RR谓词，保持与现有最优方法相当的索引大小和构建时间，在RRANN查询上实现最高12.5倍的加速，同时在RFANN、IFANN和TSANN等变体搜索任务上也表现优异或更优。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search with range filters has recently garnered significant attention. This paper delves into a generalized form of this problem, i.e., ANN search with exact range-range (RR) predicates on a range-valued attribute, named RR filtering ANN (RRANN). Specifically, given $n$ vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, each vector $v_i$ is associated with a numeric range $[l_i, r_i]$, symbolizing aspects like a price range or time interval. An RRANN query $(v_q, l_q, r_q)$ aims at finding $k$ vectors closest to $v_q$ within the vectors satisfying an arbitrary RR predicate defined between the query range $[l_q, r_q]$ and the object range $[l_i, r_i]$. The RR predicate remains unspecified, enabling user-defined conditions. It may encompass containment ($[l_i, r_i] \subseteq [l_q, r_q]$ or $[l_q, r_q] \subseteq [l_i, r_i]$), overlap ($l_i \le l_q \le r_i \le r_q$ or $l_q \le l_i \le r_q \le r_i$), or a disjunction of them. RRANN has broad applications in queries related to price ranges or time intervals, and it generalizes existing variants of ANN search with range filters. However, existing dedicated approaches for these problems lack the capacity to support queries with arbitrary RR predicates. Hence, we introduce a new approach, labeled multi-segment tree graph. It efficiently handles arbitrary RR predicates by avoiding traversal through non-predicate-satisfied nodes, and keeps equivalent index size and construction time to state-of-the-art methods for RFANN. Extensive experiments on real-world data demonstrate the efficacy of our approach in RRANN queries, achieving up to 12.5x speedups with the same accuracy as the baselines. Moreover, our approach attains comparable RFANN search performance and notably superior IFANN and TSANN search performance compared to the respective state-of-the-art approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/FanEDG/MSTG.

</details>


### [142] [RT-RkNN: Reverse k Nearest Neighbor Queries as a Graphics Ray Casting Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26671)
*Zhengyang Bai,Peng Chen,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文将RkNN查询问题创新性地转化为GPU光线投射问题，利用GPU专用光线追踪硬件实现高效查询，在传统R树方法表现不佳的挑战性场景中展现出显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于R树及其变体的空间剪枝技术的剪枝有效性在以下场景中显著下降：设施数量少、用户群体密集、或k值较大。这些挑战性场景限制了传统方法的应用范围。

Method: 将二维空间中的RkNN查询问题重新建模为图形光线投射问题：用户建模为射线(rays)，设施表示为几何图元(geometric primitives)。基于此公式化，开发了首个利用现代GPU专用硬件光线追踪核心的算法和实现方案。

Result: 该方法在大k值、密集用户群体和高度稀疏设施分布等场景下均优于现有最优算法，尤其是在传统剪枝策略失效的情况下表现出色。

Conclusion: 首次利用现代GPU专用硬件光线追踪核心实现RkNN查询算法，在大k值、密集用户群和稀疏设施分布等挑战性场景中显著优于现有最优方法。

Abstract: Reverse k nearest neighbor (RkNN) queries are fundamental in spatial databases, location-based analytics, and recommendation systems. Existing state-of-the-art techniques rely on spatial pruning supported by R-trees and their variants. However, their pruning effectiveness degrades significantly in challenging scenarios where the number of facilities is small, the user population is dense, or the value of k is large. To overcome these limitations, this work reformulates the RkNN query problem in two-dimensional geometric spaces as a graphics ray-casting problem, where users are modeled as rays and facilities are represented as geometric primitives. Based on this formulation, the first algorithm and implementation exploiting dedicated hardware ray-tracing cores on modern GPUs are developed. This novel approach preserves strong filtering performance even for large values of k, dense user populations, and highly sparse facility distributions. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms across diverse settings, particularly in scenarios where traditional pruning strategies become inefficient.

</details>


### [143] [Knowledge Graphs as the Missing Data Layer for LLM-Based Industrial Asset Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26874)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该文通过知识图谱增强工业资产运营智能体性能，颠覆传统LLM推理范式：让LLM生成结构化查询而非直接推理原始数据，确定性图谱执行。该方法将准确率从65%提升至99-100%，证明在结构化运营领域数据层是主要瓶颈而非LLM编排模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KDD 2026的AssetOpsBench表明GPT-4智能体在工业维护场景中准确率仅65%。现有研究比较LLM编排范式，但未探索数据模型对性能的影响。该文提出正交问题：工具背后的数据模型如何影响智能体性能？

Method: 构建知识图谱层（781节点、955边、16种关系类型）；提出三种架构对比：确定性图谱处理器、LLM生成Cypher查询、原始工具增强LLM基线；在AssetOpsBench的139个原始场景和467个扩展场景上评估。

Result: 确定性图谱处理器达99%(137/139)，扩展后达100%(467/467)；LLM生成Cypher达82-83%；原始基线仅65%。引入40个图原生场景（多跳依赖、向量相似性、PageRank关键性分析）。

Conclusion: 知识图谱数据层比LLM编排模式更重要；确定性图谱处理（无需LLM）达100%准确率。知识图谱作为原始工业数据与LLM推理之间的集成层，可有效解决结构化运营领域的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for industrial asset operations show limited accuracy when reasoning over flat document stores. AssetOpsBench (KDD 2026) establishes that GPT-4 agents achieve 65% on 139 industrial maintenance scenarios backed by CouchDB, YAML, and CSV. It compares LLM orchestration paradigms (Agent-As-Tool vs Plan-Execute) on a fixed data layer; we ask a complementary, orthogonal question: how much does the data model behind the tools affect agent performance?
  Building on the same scenarios, we introduce a knowledge graph layer (781 nodes, 955 edges, 16 relationship types) and evaluate three architectures: (1) deterministic graph handlers (no LLM) at 99% (137/139); (2) LLM-generated Cypher over the graph at 82-83% with the same GPT-4 model the baseline uses; and (3) the original tool-augmented LLM baseline at 65% (91/139, matching the published KDD 2026 leaderboard ceiling).
  Our key finding is inverted LLM usage: rather than asking the LLM to reason over raw data, we ask it to generate structured queries from a typed schema. The graph executes deterministically. We additionally contribute 40 graph-native scenarios (multi-hop dependency, vector similarity, PageRank criticality), and evaluate against the expanded HuggingFace AssetOpsBench release (467 scenarios, 6 domains), where deterministic handlers achieve 100% (467/467) with average score 0.848.
  These results suggest that for structured operational domains, the data layer -- not the LLM orchestration -- is the primary bottleneck, and that knowledge graphs serve as an integration layer between raw industrial data and LLM-based reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [LearnedCache: An eBPF-Integrated Perceptron-Based Eviction Policy for the Linux Page Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26168)
*Zejia Qi*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出LearnedCache，一种基于单层感知机的Linux页面缓存驱逐策略，通过eBPF集成到Linux内核中，在真实工作负载下可将插入率提升最高10%，同时开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux页面缓存是操作系统性能的核心支柱，但现有驱逐策略受限于固定启发式规则。随着AI工具的发展和工作负载多样化，基于机器学习的缓存驱逐策略具有显著潜力，但此前研究仅停留在用户空间应用（如CDN），尚未在Linux内核层面实现。

Method: 1) 设计基于单层感知机的缓存驱逐模型；2) 使用真实内核数据对多样化工作负载进行训练；3) 通过eBPF将模型嵌入Linux内核进行实时性能评估；4) 对50组配对试验进行统计检验。

Result: 模型中位数AUC接近80%；在特定工作负载下，插入率相比FIFO基线提升最高10%，且开销极小。

Conclusion: 机器学习驱动的缓存驱逐策略在Linux内核中具有实际可行性，可在代表性经验工作负载下以统计显著优势超越传统FIFO策略。

Abstract: Linux is the foundation of the digital age, accounting for the majority of the cloud and mobile OS markets. Any device that runs Linux uses the Linux page cache, a central pillar in OS and application performance, serving to reduce extraneous disk access. Many page cache eviction policies have been developed but remain bound by the rigidity of heuristics. The rise of AI-driven tools in recent years, melded with the ever-increasing variety of workloads for Linux devices, sets the stage for machine-learning-driven cache eviction policies. Promising research has been done in this field, but only in the field of user-space applications such as CDNs. We develop LearnedCache, an eBPF-integrated single-layer perceptron-based cache eviction policy for the Linux page cache, trained on real kernel data from diverse workloads. We demonstrate median AUCs of nearly 80% over multiple linear models modeling page reuse time, then take a step further by embedding these models inside the Linux kernel for real-time performance evaluation. Through statistical testing over 50 paired trials against a baseline of FIFO for each workload, LearnedCache reveals that machine-learning-derived cache eviction policies are practical in the Linux kernel under representative empirical workloads and are able to surpass conventional FIFO by statistically significant margins of up to 10% in insertion rate, a frequency-adjusted derivation of cache hit rate, in specific workloads while incurring minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [On the Detection of Commutative Factors in Factor Graphs: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26908)
*Malte Luttermann,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现当前检测可交换因子的最先进算法存在理论缺陷——其核心定理仅为必要条件而非充分条件，可能导致错误结果。作者通过证明修正版定理并提出修正算法（保持效率）和更紧致最坏情况界限的补充算法来解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升概率图模型（如因子图）中可交换因子的检测能力，这是提升概率推理算法的关键，但现有最先进算法存在理论缺陷可能产生错误结果。

Method: 理论分析发现原定理仅提供必要条件而非充分条件；通过证明修正版定理作为必要条件来修复理论缺陷；相应修正算法设计。

Result: 识别出原定理仅为必要条件而非充分条件，实际应用中可能产生不正确结果；提供修正定理及相应修正算法，保证正确性且保持效率；额外提出具有更优最坏情况界限的补充算法。

Conclusion: 提出了修正版本的算法（保持效率且确保正确性）以及具有更紧致最坏情况界限的补充算法，填补了原算法的理论缺陷。

Abstract: Exploiting the indistinguishability of objects in a probabilistic graphical model such as a factor graph is key to lifted probabilistic inference algorithms and allows for tractable probabilistic inference problems with respect to domain sizes. A central building block for the exploitation of indistinguishable objects in factor graphs is the identification of commutative factors, i.e., factors whose output values are invariant under permutations of input values assigned to a subset of their arguments. In this paper, we revisit the theoretical foundations underlying the state-of-the-art algorithm to detect commutative factors. Specifically, we show that in its current form, the state-of-the-art algorithm relies on a central theorem that is mistakenly regarded as a sufficient condition to identify commutative factors, while it actually only implies necessary condition. Consequently, the state of the art might, as we show in this paper, deliver incorrect results. To fix the flaws currently present in the state of the art, we prove a slightly modified version of the aforementioned theorem, which serves as a necessary condition to identify commutative factors. Moreover, we present a corrected version of the state-of-the-art algorithm, which keeps its efficiency while ensuring correctness and introduce a complementary algorithm with tighter worst-case bounds.

</details>


### [146] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrickAnything是一种几何条件自回归框架，用于从各种3D表示生成可构建的砖块结构。该方法使用点云作为统一接口，通过结构感知树分词化表示砖块结构的局部连接关系，使序列生成更符合物理构建过程。结合偏好对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚策略，提高了结构的稳定性和几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有砖块生成方法存在局限性：启发式优化在目标3D形状无法满足预定义约束时会失效；而生成砖块序列的方法未显式建模底层3D几何和装配关系。

Method: 提出一种几何条件自回归框架BrickAnything，使用点云作为统一几何接口，预测重建目标形状的砖块序列；引入结构感知树分词化方法，通过局部连接关系表示砖块结构；采用基于偏好的对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚策略来增强可构建性。

Result: BrickAnything能生成几何保真且物理可实现的砖块结构；所提出的分词化方法相比传统排序策略有效减少了回滚和重新生成。

Conclusion: BrickAnything能生成几何准确且物理可实现的砖块结构，其提出的分词化方法相比传统排序策略有效减少了回滚和重新生成的情况。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [147] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑大语言模型是否真正具备元认知监控能力，通过重审两项评估范式发现：模型在干预检测中无法区分内部状态篡改与输入操作，在自我预测任务中仅用输入特征的分类器即可达到同等表现。结论认为现有行为证据不足以确立LLMs具有真正的内省能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为大语言模型能够检测和报告自身内部状态，但作者认为这一结论可能为时过早。原因在于：需要区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配；且仅凭行为证据无法建立强有力的内省主张。

Method: 批判性重审两个评估范式：1) 干预检测范式——检验模型是否能区分内部状态篡改与输入操作；2) 自我预测范式——对比模型自身预测与仅能访问输入的分类器的表现。此外引入重新标注的对照设置，排除语义线索干扰。

Result: 在两个范式中均发现不支持强元认知主张的证据：1) 干预检测范式中，模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操作，表明其成功源于一般性异常检测而非内部状态感知；2) 自我预测范式中，仅访问输入的分类器达到与模型自身预测相当的表现，且在语义线索被控制的版本中模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 现有证据不足以证明大语言模型具备元认知监控能力；当前的评估范式存在缺陷，无法区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [148] [Is Agent Memory a Database? Rethinking Data Foundations for Long-Term AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26252)
*Abdelghny Orogat,Essam Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GEM（Governed Evolving Memory）范式，将长期智能体记忆定义为一种新的数据管理工作负载，其正确性是状态轨迹的属性而非单条记录的属性。通过四个状态级操作符和六个正确性条件，解决传统系统在记忆管理上的四个 recurring failure modes。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体记忆系统和数据库范式将记忆视为存储，存在四个 recurring failure modes： unregulated growth（无监管增长）、missing semantic revision（缺失语义修订）、capacity-driven forgetting（容量驱动的遗忘）和 read-only retrieval（只读检索）。这些系统无法满足长期记忆的需求。

Method: 提出GEM范式，用四个状态级操作符（摄取、修订、遗忘、检索）替代记录级数据库操作；通过六个正确性条件管理状态演化；结构观察证明任何记录级系统都无法满足这些条件；在属性图后端上实现MemState原型。

Result: 证明了记录级系统无法满足GEM的六个正确性条件，无论采用何种存储模型；MemState验证了可行性。

Conclusion: MemState原型验证了可行性并暴露了与原生引擎的差距；提出了三个定义以内存为中心的数据管理作为新工作负载的研究方向。

Abstract: Long-running AI agents need persistent memory. Memory supports learning across sessions, reduces repeated context injection, and enables auditing of past decisions. Current agent memory systems and database paradigms treat memory as storage. They localize correctness at records, embeddings, or edges. Each supplies only some of the capabilities that long-term memory requires. The result is four recurring failure modes: unregulated growth, missing semantic revision, capacity-driven forgetting, and read-only retrieval. In our vision, long-term agent memory is a new data-management workload. Its correctness is a property of the state trajectory, not of individual records. We formalize this as Governed Evolving Memory (GEM). GEM replaces record-level database operations with four state-level operators: ingestion, revision, forgetting, and retrieval. Six correctness conditions govern how the state evolves. Three structural observations establish that no record-level system can satisfy these conditions, regardless of the storage model. We realize the abstraction in MemState, a prototype on a property-graph backend. MemState validates feasibility and exposes the gap to a native engine. We outline three research directions that define memory-centric data management as a workload.

</details>


### [149] [Personalizing Embodied Multimodal Large Language Model Agents over Long-term User Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26256)
*Jeongeun Lee,Chanyoung Park,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLAR是一个多模态记忆增强框架，通过构建包含语义记忆和情景记忆的多模态知识图，实现个性化具身智能体的长期交互能力。该方法可与多种MLLM backbone结合，在跨交互推理、多跳推理和用户上下文跟踪等任务上表现出显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current embodied agents based on multimodal large language models can follow generic instructions and recognize object categories, but they lack the ability to leverage personalized context accumulated over time. In real-world scenarios, intended targets are often specified implicitly through prior interactions, requiring agents to maintain and utilize long-term user-specific information.

Method: POLAR organizes prior interactions into a multimodal knowledge graph with two memory components: (1) semantic memory for personalized context and visual concepts, (2) episodic memory for embodied experiences (e.g., agent trajectories). During task execution, relevant memories are retrieved to interpret user requests and guide task completion.

Result: The proposed memory mechanism consistently improves performance across multiple MLLM backbones and diverse evaluation scenarios. The improvement is particularly significant for tasks requiring cross-interaction reasoning, multi-hop inference, and tracking user-specific context updates over time.

Conclusion: POLAR provides a generic memory-augmented solution that can be combined with various MLLM backbones to enable personalized embodied assistance over long-term interactions.

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based embodied agents have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks in physical environments. However, personalized assistance requires more than following generic instruction or recognizing object categories. In real-world scenarios, the intended target is often specified only implicitly through prior interactions, requiring agents to leverage personalized context accumulated over time. In this work, we propose POLAR, a multiomodal memory-augmented framework for personalized embodied agents over long-term user interactions. POLAR organizes prior interactions into a multimodal knowledge graph that captures semantic memory for personalized context and visual concepts, and episodic memory for embodied experiences such as agent trajectories. To execute embodied tasks, POLAR retrieves relevant memories to interpret the current request and guide task execution. We evaluate POLAR across multiple MLLM backbones and diverse evaluation scenarios to study the role of memory in long-term personalization. Results show that the proposed memory mechanism consistently improves performance by enabling more effective use of information accumulated over prior interactions. The gains are especially pronounced when the agents are required to reason across multiple interactions, perform multi-hop inference, or tracking updates in user-specific context over time.

</details>


### [150] [Your Agents Are Aging Too: Agent Lifespan Engineering for Deployed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26302)
*Jianing Zhu,Yeonju Ro,John Robertson,Kevin Wang,Junbo Li,Haris Vikalo,Aditya Akella,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgingBench是一个面向智能体寿命工程的长程可靠性基准，揭示了智能体在长期部署中的多维度衰退现象，证明仅靠增强基座模型不足以保证部署可靠性，需要建立生命周期评估、机制级诊断和靶向修复的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估范式存在根本缺陷：智能体被当作"即时部署的静态模型"评估，但实际部署是持续运行的动态系统。智能体的有效状态不断变化（交互历史压缩、记忆库增长、事实更新修订、定期维护），可靠性是系统的全生命周期属性而非模型快照属性。

Method: 构建Agent生命周期 aging 的四类机制分类体系：压缩aging、干扰aging、修订aging和维护aging；采用时序依赖图和配对反事实探测方法，诊断记忆管道的写入、检索和利用三阶段的故障；跨7个场景、14个模型、多种记忆策略，进行~400轮实验（8-200个会话）。

Result: 智能体aging并非单一维度：行为测试保持通过时事实精度可能已衰减；派生状态追踪可在单个模型内急剧崩溃；同一错误答案的修复策略需根据诊断画像定制而非通用处理。

Conclusion: 可靠智能体部署需要生命周期评估、机制级诊断和阶段性靶向修复，而非仅依赖更强的基座模型。行为测试通过并不代表事实准确性未衰减，同一错误答案可能需要不同的修复策略。

Abstract: Long-lived AI agents are increasingly deployed as persistent operational systems, yet they are still evaluated like freshly initialized models. Day-one benchmarks miss a basic systems question: how long does an agent remain reliable after deployment? Even when model weights are frozen, an agent's effective state keeps changing as it compresses interaction history, retrieves from a growing memory store, revises facts after updates, and undergoes routine maintenance. Reliability therefore becomes a lifespan property of the full agent harness, not only a snapshot property of the base model. We introduce AgingBench, a longitudinal reliability benchmark for agent lifespan engineering: measuring not only whether deployed agents degrade, but what form the degradation takes and where repair should target. AgingBench organizes agent aging into four mechanisms: compression aging, interference aging, revision aging, and maintenance aging. To diagnose these failures, AgingBench uses temporal dependency graphs and paired counterfactual probes that produce diagnostic profiles for the write, retrieval, and utilization stages of the memory pipeline. Across 7 scenarios, 14 models, multiple memory policies, and both runner-controlled and autonomous agents, over ~400 runs spanning 8 - 200 sessions show that agent aging is not one-dimensional: behavioral tests can remain clean while factual precision decays; derived-state tracking can collapse sharply within a single model; and the same wrong answer can require different repairs depending on what the diagnostic profile points to. These results suggest that reliable agent deployment requires lifespan evaluation, mechanism-level diagnosis, and stage-targeted repair, not only stronger day-one models.

</details>


### [151] [Experiments in Agentic AI for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26305)
*Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两个基于Local Body, Remote Brain混合架构的自主AI Agent框架，通过Cellular RAG、远程数据检查和分布式并发控制等工程方案，突破现有LLM在上下文和推理上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿系统在上下文窗口和推理能力上存在局限性，无法有效支持科学工作流程的严格要求，因此需要开发具备自主性的AI Agent框架。

Method: 采用Local Body, Remote Brain混合架构，通过Google Colab运行；实现Cellular RAG（细粒度属性提取）、远程数据检查和分布式并发控制；开发了时间序列去重 curation pipeline和视觉 lecture-to-report 转换 pipeline。

Result: 成功构建两个自主Agent：DeepTS/DeepCollector实现大规模时间序列数据集的自动化采集、提取与去重；DeepScribe实现将视觉密集、数学复杂的物理学讲座转换为结构化科学报告。

Conclusion: 两项框架证明了混合架构结合Python编排器与LLM云后端的有效性，DeepTS具备向深度知识图谱（如DeepQCD高能物理应用）扩展的潜力。

Abstract: This paper details two novel frameworks for developing autonomous, agentic AI in scientific workflows. Both systems leverage a hybrid Local Body, Remote Brain architecture via Google Colab, utilizing Python-based local orchestrators to invoke large language model (LLM) cloud backends. The first agent, DeepTS/DeepCollector, automates the large-scale curation, extraction, and deduplication of time-series datasets. The second, DeepScribe, is an autonomous presentation analyzer that converts visually dense, mathematically complex physics lectures into structured scientific reports. Through practical systems engineering-such as granular attribute extraction (Cellular RAG), remote data inspection, and distributed concurrency controls-we demonstrate how agentic AI can overcome the context and reasoning limitations of current state-of-the-art systems to rigorously support scientific workflows. Finally, we outline a generalization of DeepTS to support deep knowledge graphs and discuss the application of this conceptual approach to high-energy physics (DeepQCD).

</details>


### [152] [Anchor: Mitigating Artifact Drift in Agent Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26321)
*Maksim Ivanov,Abhijay Rana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper introduces Anchor, a task-generation pipeline that formalizes business workflow specifications into constraint optimization programs to jointly produce instructions, environments, ground-truth solutions, and verifiers — addressing "artifact drift" in AI agent evaluation. Applied to produce ERP-Bench, a benchmark of 300 tasks in procurement and manufacturing, demonstrating that frontier models still struggle to reach optimal solutions (17.4% success rate).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current training and evaluation environments for enterprise AI agents suffer from "artifact drift" — loosely coupled creation of instructions, environments, oracles, and verifiers leads to unsolvable, reward-hackable, or inconsistent environments.

Method: Anchor formalizes business workflow specifications into constraint optimization programs, jointly generating instructions, environment configurations, solver-certified ground-truth solutions, and state-based verifiers from parametric specifications.

Result: ERP-Bench contains 300 long-horizon tasks across procurement and manufacturing workflows; generation parameters predict realized difficulty; frontier models satisfy explicit constraints in 26.1% of trials but reach fully optimal solutions in only 17.4% of trials.

Conclusion: Anchor provides a concrete recipe for building auditable evaluation environments for economically valuable agent work; ERP-Bench dataset and task generator released at erpbench.ai.

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to complete valuable, long-horizon business operations tasks, but training and evaluation environments for enterprise work still struggle to balance realism, verifiability, and scale. Environment and task creation frequently suffers from a failure mode we call artifact drift: when instructions, environments, oracles, and verifiers are created by loosely coupled processes, they frequently disagree on what a task requires, producing environments that are unsolvable, reward-hackable, or inconsistent. We introduce Anchor, a task-generation pipeline that formalizes domain experts' specifications of business workflows into constraint optimization programs. From a single parametric specification, the pipeline jointly produces a natural-language instruction, environment configuration, solver-certified ground-truth solution, and state-based verifier. With Anchor, altering parameters yields new tasks with controlled difficulty and known optimal solutions, producing harness-agnostic environments whose rewards depend solely on end-state business correctness. We apply Anchor to produce ERP-Bench: a benchmark of 300 long-horizon tasks spanning procurement and manufacturing workflows in a production-grade ERP system. We find that generation parameters predict realized difficulty, and that frontier models satisfy explicit task constraints in 26.1% of trials but reach a fully optimal solution in only 17.4% of trials. Overall, we show that Anchor and ERP-Bench offer a concrete recipe for building auditable evaluation environments for economically valuable agent work. We release the task generator and ERP-Bench dataset at erpbench.ai

</details>


### [153] [ScientistOne: Towards Human-Level Autonomous Research via Chain-of-Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26340)
*Rui Meng,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Chun-Liang Li,Palash Goyal,Mihir Parmar,Yiwen Song,Yale Song,Rajarishi Sinha,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Burak Gokturk,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自主研究智能体的可验证性问题提出系统性解决方案。通过Chain-of-Evidence框架、ScientistOne端到端系统和CoE Audit审计方法三措并举，有效解决了AI生成研究中常见的引用伪造、分数不可复现和方法实现不一致等问题，在75篇论文的广泛评估中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous research agents produce outputs with verifiability failures (fabricated citations, unreproducible scores, method-implementation divergence) that are undetectable by surface-level evaluation, yet these systems create competitive solutions and professional-looking manuscripts.

Method: Threefold approach: (1) Chain-of-Evidence (CoE) framework requiring traceable evidence for every claim; (2) ScientistOne, an end-to-end autonomous research system maintaining evidence chains throughout literature review, solution discovery, and paper writing; (3) CoE Audit post-hoc methodology with four integrity checks (score verification, specification violation, reference verification, method-code alignment) applied uniformly across all systems.

Result: Across 75 papers spanning five systems and five frontier tasks: baseline systems exhibit systematic failures with hallucinated reference rates reaching 21%, score verification pass rates as low as 42%, and method-code alignment ranging 20-80%. ScientistOne significantly outperforms baselines on all verifiability metrics.

Conclusion: ScientistOne achieves zero hallucinated references (0/337), perfect score verification (12/12), and highest method-code alignment (14/15). It matches or exceeds human expert performance across all five tasks and generalizes to six additional tasks, achieving state-of-the-art on Parameter Golf and gold medals on MLE-Bench where baselines fail entirely.

Abstract: Autonomous research agents produce competitive solutions and professional-looking manuscripts, yet their outputs contain verifiability failures undetectable by surface-level evaluation: fabricated citations, unreproducible scores, and method descriptions that diverge from the implementation. We address this through three contributions. First, Chain-of-Evidence (CoE), a verifiability framework requiring every claim to be traceable to its evidence source. Second, ScientistOne, an end-to-end autonomous research system that maintains evidence chains by construction throughout literature review, solution discovery, and paper writing. Third, CoE Audit, a post-hoc audit whose four integrity checks -- score verification, specification violation, reference verification, and method-code alignment -- apply uniformly to all systems. Across 75 papers spanning five systems and five frontier research tasks, every baseline exhibits at least one systematic failure mode: hallucinated reference rates reach 21%, score verification passes in as few as 42% of papers, and method-code alignment ranges from 20% to 80%. ScientistOne achieves zero hallucinated references (0/337), perfect score verification (12/12), and the highest method-code alignment (14/15), while matching or exceeding human expert performance on all five tasks. ScientistOne further generalizes to six additional tasks spanning medical imaging, fine-grained recognition, 3D perception, and language modeling, achieving state-of-the-art on Parameter Golf and gold medals on MLE-Bench tasks where baselines fail entirely.

</details>


### [154] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniToM是一个新型ToM基准，通过两阶段评估（信念提取+七维标签标注）直接评估LLM的心理状态表征能力。该基准包含895个故事和22,343个标注信念命题。研究发现当前LLMs在角色信念追踪方面存在特异性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM评估范式仅通过最终答案判断性能，掩盖了模型是否真正构建了稳健推理所需的心理状态表征，尤其在涉及分歧、演变或错误信念的场景中难以揭示模型的真实推理能力。

Method: OmniToM采用两阶段评估范式：Stage 1从故事中提取与社交动态相关的信念；Stage 2使用七维模式标签对每个信念进行标注（递归阶数、真值状态、知识获取途径、外显性、内容类型、心理来源、上下文）。该基准基于895个故事和22,343个标注信念命题，通过人工校准的LLM辅助标注流程构建。

Result: 零样本评估显示，当前LLMs在将叙事事实转化为角色信念和共享心理状态时，存在知识获取和表征决策方面的瓶颈。

Conclusion: OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck in current LLMs, showing they struggle with knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [155] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UnityMAS-O框架，通过RL优化LLM多智能体系统，将完整工作流作为优化单元而非单响应，支持逻辑角色与物理模型参数解耦，在多种任务上验证了多智能体RL优化人工指定工作流的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统依赖人工编排的提示词、工具和控制规则，缺乏统一RL优化接口；现有RL后训练框架主要针对单策略优化，缺少多智能体工作流抽象、角色级信用分配和可配置参数共享机制。

Method: 提出UnityMAS-O框架，将完整工作流作为优化单元，通过逻辑角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励和智能体-模型映射四个一等对象表示工作流；扩展verl框架，采用Ray星型拓扑运行时架构。

Result: 在Natural Questions、HotpotQA和代码任务上，多智能体RL优化后性能提升，尤其是小模型和严格代码all-passed指标上增益显著。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O可作为将多样化LLM多智能体工作流转化为可训练多智能体RL系统的可重用基础框架，在小模型和严格代码评估指标上效果尤为显著。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [156] [JobBench: Aligning Agent Work With Human Will](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26329)
*Yuetai Li,Yichen Feng,Zhangchen Xu,Zixian Ma,Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Xinghua Sun,Rulin Shao,Zichen Chen,Yue Huang,Xinyang Han,Brian Lee,Kayla Xu,Shenglai Zeng,Hang Hua,Xiangliang Zhang,Basel Alomair,Ranjay Krishna,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Luyao Niu,Xiang Yue,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JobBench introduces a human-centered benchmark for occupational AI agents, evaluating 130 tasks across 35 occupations on what humans actually want delegated rather than economic value. Current state-of-the-art models achieve only 45.9% accuracy, highlighting the need to shift AI development from replacement to enhancement focus.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing occupational AI benchmarks are narrowly scoped by economic values, framing AI as a human replacement rather than an enhancement tool. This replacement-centric framing misaligns with what humans actually want to delegate. The authors argue for a needs-based evaluation paradigm that empowers humans.

Method: JobBench evaluates AI agents on 130 agentic tasks across 35 occupations. Tasks are presented as realistic workspaces with heterogeneous reference files requiring agents to navigate cluttered professional information. Evaluation uses fact-anchored rubric chains averaging 35.6 binary criteria per task. 36 models were tested.

Result: The strongest model, Claude Opus 4.7 under Claude Code, achieves only 45.9% accuracy, indicating significant room for improvement in professional workflow automation.

Conclusion: JobBench provides a more human-centered evaluation framework for occupational AI agents. The benchmark demonstrates that current AI systems are still far from human-level performance in real professional workflows (45.9% accuracy). The authors advocate for shifting AI development priorities from replacement-focused economic metrics to enhancement-focused delegation preferences.

Abstract: Current benchmarks for occupational AI agents are scoped primarily by economic values, telling a replacement story. We introduce JobBench, which evaluates AI agents on the workflows that experts identify as high-priority for delegation, empowering humans based on their needs instead of replacing them with GDP value. JobBench covers 130 agentic tasks across 35 occupations. Each task is packaged as a workspace of heterogeneous reference files, requiring the agent to reason through the cluttered information streams of real professional work. Outputs are graded by a fact-anchored chain of rubrics, averaging 35.6 binary criteria per task. We evaluate 36 models; the strongest, Claude Opus~4.7 under Claude Code, reaches only 45.9 %. We hope JobBench shifts the community's target labour-market effect from replacement to enhancement: building agents that do what humans actually want delegated, not only what is most economically valuable.

</details>


### [157] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个管理 LLM 生成虚拟实验室程序性知识不确定性的框架，通过结构化领域表示与状态转换样本提取候选规则，转化为显式约束以修复不确定步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育虚拟实验室可提升实验训练的可扩展性、适应性和可及性，但创作新的模拟实验程序成本高昂——教育者需描述设备、定义交互方式、指定执行流程。LLM 可辅助生成实验程序，但其输出不能直接作为执行计划，可能遗漏必要动作、步骤顺序错误或产生逻辑错误/设备不兼容的指令。

Method: 使用结构化领域表示和不确定的 LLM 生成状态转换样本，通过以下流程处理：(1) 提取候选程序规则；(2) 转换为显式可检验的约束；(3) 利用约束修复不确定的程序步骤。

Result: 提出一个管理 LLM 生成程序性知识不确定性的原型框架。

Conclusion: 该框架虽源于教育虚拟实验室场景，但可泛化至结构化交互环境中的动作规划问题，为不确定性 procedural knowledge 管理提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [158] [Modeling Agentic Technical Debt and Stochastic Tax: A Standalone Framework for Measurement, Simulation, and Dashboarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27320)
*Muhammad Zia Hydari,Raja Iqbal,Narayan Ramasubbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper proposes a formal framework distinguishing "Agentic Technical Debt" (stock of accumulated design/governance liability) from "Stochastic Tax" (recurring flow of operating burden when using stochastic agents). These two constructs interact—debt amplifies tax, but tax persists even when debt is minimized. The model provides dashboard expression, structural formulation, variable definitions, estimation methods from operational data, and an accounts-payable simulation with companion spreadsheet for practical application.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI systems combining probabilistic reasoning with delegated action require clear governance and cost modeling. A formal and managerially usable model was needed to distinguish Agentic Technical Debt (accumulated design/governance liability) from Stochastic Tax (recurring operating burden from stochastic agents).

Method: Developed a formal model starting from a compact dashboard expression, expanded into a fuller structural model with defined variables and parameters; demonstrated cost estimation from operational data with accounts-payable simulation and spreadsheet illustration.

Result: Agentic Technical Debt and Stochastic Tax are related but distinct constructs: debt can amplify the tax, while the tax can remain positive even when debt is minimized.

Conclusion: The framework distinguishes two distinct cost constructs with practical applicability, supported by an accounts-payable simulation and companion spreadsheet for operational use.

Abstract: Agentic AI systems combine probabilistic reasoning with delegated action through tools, context, memory, orchestration, and external workflow integration. This note develops a formal and managerially usable model that distinguishes Agentic Technical Debt from Stochastic Tax. Agentic Technical Debt is a stock of accumulated design and governance liability. Stochastic Tax is a recurring flow of operating burden that arises when stochastic agents are used in business workflows. The two constructs are related, but they are not the same: debt can amplify the tax, while the tax can remain positive even when debt is minimized. The note starts from a compact dashboard expression, expands it into a fuller structural model, defines all variables and parameters, shows how each cost category can be estimated from operational data, and illustrates the framework with an accounts-payable simulation and companion spreadsheet.

</details>


### [159] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper addresses automatic layer selection for hallucination detection in LLMs by proposing FEPoID, a training-free criterion that identifies optimal intermediate layers by detecting the first effective peak of intrinsic dimension, achieving superior detection performance when combined with a novel truncation strategy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hallucination-related signals are stronger in intermediate layers of LLMs, but automated layer selection remains an unresolved problem; existing principled methods lack consistent performance.

Method: Evaluated multiple criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse architectures, scales, and benchmarks; proposed FEPoID (First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension) as training-free criterion with negligible computational overhead.

Result: FEPoID outperforms prior criteria and baselines; truncation strategy amplifies hallucination signals and further improves detection.

Conclusion: FEPoID consistently identifies optimal or near-optimal layers, outperforming existing criteria and hallucination detection baselines; combined with truncation strategy, achieves substantially improved detection performance.

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [160] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARL通过对比学习对齐上下文与动作序列，利用局部动态一致性发现可复用技能，在OGBench基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRL通过发现和复用时间扩展技能来解决长horizon任务，但如何获得真正可复用的技能仍是开放问题。核心假设是不同全局上下文中的局部转移需要相似的动作序列——即局部动态一致性。

Method: 提出CARL算法，利用对比学习框架学习基于动作的表示，将具有相似局部动态的不同全局上下文与相应的动作序列对齐，从而识别可复用的技能及其适用场景。该表示可与HIQL等现有HRL算法集成。

Result: 在复杂人形环境中展示了有意义的技能聚类定性效果；在OGBench基准上与HIQL集成后实现显著的下游性能提升。

Conclusion: CARL通过对比动作表示学习可复用的局部控制技能，证明了局部动态一致性可以作为HRL中技能发现的的有效信号。该方法在OGBench基准上与HIQL集成后显著提升了下游性能，同时在复杂人形环境中产生了有意义的技能聚类。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [161] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-CreativityBench 基准评估多模态大模型的创造性工具使用能力，揭示了当前模型在 grounded 探索上的不足，并通过 DPO 偏好学习和 affordance 知识库监督提出有效的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在感知推理上进展迅速，但缺乏对开放式环境中视觉 grounded 创造性问题解决能力的评估，这种能力是人类智能的核心。

Method: 提出 MM-CreativityBench 基准测试评估 affordance-grounded 创造性工具使用；提出 Affordance-Grounded Alignment 方法，采用 DPO 进行偏好学习，鼓励模型基于视觉证据推理，并引入 affordance 知识库监督引导实体探索和多轮规划。

Result: 当前 LMMs 存在遗漏实体、检查不充分、幻觉等问题；所提方法在实体/部件选择准确性上一致提升，显著减少幻觉和 grounding 错误。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉 grounded 推理纳入偏好学习框架，并利用 affordance 知识库提供结构化监督，可有效提升模型在创造性场景中的 grounded 探索能力。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [162] [From Static Context to Calibrated Interactive RL: Mitigating Distribution Shift in Multi-turn Dialogue with Aligned Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26403)
*Xiaohua Wang,Jiakang Yuan,Zisu Huang,Muzhao Tian,Changze Lv,Kaitao Song,Tao Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对LLM对话智能体中上下文分布偏移问题进行理论分析，识别出策略诱导偏移和模拟器诱导偏移两种来源，并提出Calibrated Interactive RL统一框架，通过模拟器对齐来耦合交互式强化学习，从而缓解复合分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两类范式（Static Context RL和Interactive RL）都受到上下文分布偏移问题的根本性限制，导致对话质量随轮次二次衰减。

Method: Theoretical analysis of context distribution shift; Proposes Calibrated Interactive RL framework combining interactive RL with simulator alignment to calibrate simulated behaviors with real human interaction patterns.

Result: 实验验证：(i) Interactive RL通过缓解策略分布偏移显著优于Static Context基线；(ii) 模拟器校准进一步缩小sim-to-real差距，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Calibrated Interactive RL achieves state-of-the-art performance by coupling interactive RL with simulator alignment, effectively mitigating both policy-induced and simulator-induced distribution shifts.

Abstract: A long-standing goal of the research community is to develop highly interactive LLM-based dialogue agents. Recent research focuses on optimizing policies based on fixed offline logs (Static Context RL) or using a prompt-based simulator (Interactive RL). In this work, we theoretically show that both paradigms are fundamentally limited by context distribution shift--a mismatch between dialogue histories observed during training and those encountered in real conversations. This shift compounds quadratically over turns and severely degrades dialogue quality. Specifically, we attribute this shift to two distinct sources: (i) policy-induced shift, arising from training on static histories rather than self-generated trajectories; and (ii) simulator-induced shift, stemming from discrepancies between simulated and real human behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Calibrated Interactive RL, a unified framework that couples interactive RL with simulator alignment. By aligning the simulator with human interaction patterns, our approach reduces the sim-to-real gap and mitigates compounding distribution shifts. Experiments across multiple dialogue tasks confirm our theoretical analysis: (i) Interactive RL significantly outperforms the Static Context baseline by mitigating policy distribution shift; and (ii) calibrating simulators with our alignment method further bridges the sim-to-real gap, yielding state-of-the-art downstream performance.

</details>


### [163] [The MiniMax-M2 Series: Mini Activations Unleashing Max Real-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26494)
*MiniMax,:,Aili Chen,Aonian Li,Baichuan Zhou,Bangwei Gong,Binyang Jiang,Boji Dan,Changqing Yu,Chao Wang,Cheng Ma,Cheng Zhong,Cheng Zhu,Chengjun Xiao,Chengyi Yang,Chengyu Du,Chenyang Zhang,Chi Zhang,Chuangyi Huang,Chunhao Zhang,Chunhui Du,Chunyu Zhao,Congchao Guo,Da Chen,Deming Ding,Dianjun Sun,Dongyu Zhang,Enhui Yang,Fei Yu,Guang Zheng,Guodong Zheng,Guohong Li,Haichao Zhu,Haigang Zhou,Haimo Zhang,Han Ding,Hao Zhang,Haohai Sun,Haolin Lyu,Haonan Lu,Haoyu Wang,Huajie Shi,Huiyang Li,Jiacheng Chen,Jian Zhang,Jiaqi Zhuang,Jiaren Cai,Jiaxin Pan,Jiayao Li,Jiayuan Song,Jichuan Zhang,Jie Wang,Jihao Gu,Jin Zhu,Jingwei Dong,Jingyang Li,Jingyu Zhang,Jingze Zhuang,Jinhao Tian,Jinli Liu,Jinyi Hu,Jun Tao,Jun Zhang,Junbin Ruan,Junhao Xu,Junjie Yan,Junteng Liu,Junxian He,Kang Xu,Ke Ji,Ke Yang,Kecheng Xiao,Keyu Duan,Keyu Li,Le Han,Letian Ruan,Li Yuan,Lianfei Yu,Liheng Feng,Lijie Mo,Lin Li,Lingye Bao,Lingyu Yang,Lingyuan Zhou,Loki,Lu Chen,Lunbin Ceng,Ming Li,Ming Zhong,Mingliang Tao,Mingyuan Chi,Mujie Lin,Nan Hu,Ningxin Chen,Peiyin Zhu,Peng Gao,Pengcheng Gao,Pengfei Li,Penglin Li,Pengyu Zhao,Qibin Ren,Qidi Xu,Qihan Ren,Qile Li,Qin Wang,Quanliang Chen,Qunhong Ceng,Rong Tian,Rui Dong,Ruitao Leng,Ruize Zhang,Shanqi Liu,Shaoyu Chen,Sheng Jia,Shun Yao,Shuoran Zhao,Shuqi Yu,Sichen Li,Sicheng Pan,Songquan Zhu,Tengfei Li,Tian Xie,Tiancheng Qin,Tianrun Liang,Wei Liu,Weiqi
