Xu,Weitao Li,Weixiang Chen,Weiyu Cheng,Weiyu Zhang,Wenhu Chen,Wenqian Zhao,Xiancai Chen,Xiangjun Song,Xiangyuan Wang,Xiao Luo,Xiao Su,Xiaobo Li,Xiaodong Han,Xiaojie Wu,Xihao Song,Xingyi Han,Xinyu Guan,Xuan Lu,Xun Zou,Xunhao Lai,Xutong Li,Yan Gong,Yang Wang,Yang Xu,Yangsen Wang,Ye Tang,Yicheng Chen,Yinran Qiu,Yiqi Shi,Yiting Guo,Yiwen Huang,Yixuan Wang,Yongyi Hu,Yu Gao,Yu Zhang,Yuanxiang Ying,Yuanzhen Zhang,Yubo Wang,Yuchen Song,Yufeng Yang,Yuhang Meng,Yuhang Miao,Yuhao Li,Yujie Liu,Yulin Hu,Yunan Huang,Yunji Li,Yunyi Huang,Yusen Zhang,Yusu Hong,Yutao Xie,Yutong Zhang,Yuwen Liao,Yuxuan Shi,Yuze Wenren,Zebin Li,Zehan Li,Zejian Luo,Zeyu Jin,Zeyuan Sun,Zhanpeng Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhendong Li,Zhengmao Zhu,Zhengyuan Peng,Zhenhua Fan,Zhi Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Zhiheng Lv,Zhikang Xu,Zhitao He,Zhiwei He,Zhongyuan Li,Zibo Gao,Zijia Wu,Zijian Song,Zijian Zhou,Zijun Sun,Zishan Huang,Ziying Chen,Ziyue Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax-M2 is an efficient MoE model (229.9B params / 9.8B active) designed for agentic AI applications, using agent-driven data pipelines and a scalable RL system called Forge. M2.7 achieves early self-evolution capability, reaching frontier performance across agentic benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to build efficient Mixture-of-Experts language models that maximize real-world intelligence with minimal activation, specifically designed end-to-end for agentic deployment scenarios.

Method: Three core components: (i) Agent-driven data pipelines generating verifiable trajectories across agentic coding and cowork, grounded in executable workspaces with artifact-aligned rewards; (ii) Forge, a scalable agent-native RL system featuring windowed-FIFO scheduling, prefix-tree merging, inference optimization, and training-inference-agent decoupling; (iii) M2.7 self-evolution capability for autonomous debugging and scaffold modification.

Result: The flagship M2 model contains 229.9B total parameters with only 9.8B activated per token. Across M2 through M2.7, this architecture achieves frontier-tier performance on agentic coding, deep search, office-task, and reasoning benchmarks.

Conclusion: MiniMax-M2 series demonstrates that mini-activation MoE models combined with agent-native data pipelines and scalable RL training can achieve frontier-tier agentic performance. The M2.7 checkpoint takes an early step toward self-evolution by autonomously debugging and modifying its own scaffold.

Abstract: We introduce the MiniMax-M2 series, a family of Mixture-of-Experts language models built around the principle that mini activations can unleash maximum real-world intelligence. The flagship M2 contains 229.9B total parameters with only 9.8B activated per token. Designed end-to-end for agentic deployment, the M2 series rests on three components: (i) agent-driven data pipelines producing large-scale, verifiable trajectories across agentic coding and agentic cowork, each grounded in an executable workspace and an artifact-aligned reward; (ii) Forge, a scalable agent-native RL system that adapts to long-horizon agent trajectories, paired with windowed-FIFO scheduling, prefix-tree merging, inference optimization, and a clean training-inference-agent decoupling that supports both white-box and black-box agents; (iii) the latest M2.7 checkpoint takes an early step toward self-evolution -- autonomously debugging training runs and modifying its own scaffold. Across M2 through M2.7, this combination translates a mini-activation footprint into frontier-tier performance on agentic coding, deep search, office-task, and reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [164] [PolyFusionAgent: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Autonomous AI Assistant for Polymer Property Prediction and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26543)
*Manpreet Kaur,Xingying Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolyFusionAgent is an AI framework for polymer discovery that integrates multimodal representation learning (PolyFusion) with literature-grounded design reasoning (PolyAgent), enabling evidence-linked, interactive generation and prediction of novel polymers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polymer discovery faces challenges due to massive chemical design space and fragmented representations of structure-property relationships. Current AI models lack connection to physical/experimental reality, limiting their utility for actionable design decisions.

Method: PolyFusionAgent combines a multimodal foundation model (PolyFusion) and a tool-augmented design agent (PolyAgent). PolyFusion aligns polymer representations (sequence, topology, 3D geometry, fingerprints) across millions of polymers in a shared latent space, enabling cross-chemistry transfer. PolyAgent retrieves evidence from polymer literature to contextualize and validate design hypotheses.

Result: PolyFusion improves thermophysical property prediction accuracy and enables property-conditioned generation of chemically valid, structurally novel polymers beyond existing design spaces. PolyAgent closes the design loop by linking prediction with inverse design through literature-grounded evidence retrieval.

Conclusion: PolyFusionAgent represents a significant advancement in AI-driven polymer discovery by bridging the gap between computational prediction and experimental reality through evidence-linked, interactive design.

Abstract: Polymer discovery is central to fields ranging from energy storage to biomedicine, but it is hindered by an astronomically large chemical design space and fragmented representations of structure, properties, and prior knowledge. This fragmentation leaves many AI models disconnected from physical and experimental reality, restricting their ability to support directly actionable design decisions. Here we introduce PolyFusionAgent, an interactive framework coupling a multimodal polymer foundation model (PolyFusion) with a tool-augmented, literature-grounded design agent (PolyAgent). PolyFusion aligns complementary polymer views including sequence, topology, 3D geometry, and fingerprints across millions of polymers to learn a shared latent space transferable across chemistries and data regimes, improving thermophysical property prediction and enabling property-conditioned generation of chemically valid, structurally novel polymers beyond the reference design space. PolyAgent closes the design loop by linking prediction and inverse design with evidence retrieval from the polymer literature, proposing, evaluating, and contextualizing hypotheses with explicit precedent in one workflow. Together, PolyFusionAgent enables interactive, evidence-linked polymer discovery combining large-scale representation learning, multimodal chemical knowledge, and verifiable scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [165] [MedGuideX: Internalizing Decision Logic from Executable Guidelines into Large Language Models for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26567)
*Yuhao Shen,Lang Cao,Simo Du,Yuqing Wang,Juexiao Zhou,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode evidence-based decision logic that clinicians apply by evaluating patient variables, conditional criteria, and recommendation rules. However, existing methods often use CPGs as free-text training data or retrieval sources, underutilizing their procedural decision structure. To better exploit this structure, we introduce a guideline-derived training pipeline that transforms CPG recommendations into executable clinical decision logic and uses it to generate factual and counterfactual question-answering data. Theses data teach models both guideline-supported decisions and how decisions change under different patient conditions. Post-training a medical LLM on the generated data yields MedGuideX. Across four clinical reasoning benchmarks, MedGuideX achieves a 10.28% relative improvement in average accuracy. Physician evaluation further shows that MedGuideX better recovers clinician authored reasoning steps and produces physician-preferred rationales in faithfulness, validity, completeness, and clarity. Overall, our results show that executable decision logic from CPGs can be transformed into scalable supervision for building reliable medical LLMs.

</details>


### [166] [AGORA: Adapter-Grounded Observation-Action Retention for Inference-Free Prompt Compression in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26596)
*Haoran Zhang,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现用于LLM智能体的token级压缩器存在"动作语法破坏"问题——低自信息排名的动作语义token被错误删除。针对此问题，提出了AGORA方法，通过结构化解析器+always-keep机制+125M relevance scorer实现step级压缩，在17个实验配置中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token-level extractive compressors widely used for general LM context are structurally inappropriate for LLM agents, causing "action-grammar destruction" -- tokens carrying action semantics (identifiers, brackets, action verbs) have the lowest self-information ranks, so general-purpose compressors reliably remove them, causing environment rejection.

Method: AGORA is an inference-free step-level compressor combining: (1) structural prompt parser, (2) always-keep floor for format- and recency-critical content, and (3) a 125M-parameter relevance scorer trained on counterfactual next-action-change labels (~2ms/step, zero per-step LLM toll).

Result: Across 17 (env, backbone, method) cells spanning two independent token-level method families, AGORA is the only method retaining >= 75% uncompressed performance in 8 of 9 cells (lone exception at 73%). Ablation confirms structural floor as the dominant quality lever and learned scorer as source of 1.0-11.5x adaptive end-to-end compression.

Conclusion: AGORA is the only method across 17 experimental cells that retains >= 75% uncompressed performance in 8 of 9 cells (lone exception at 73%), with ablation confirming the structural floor as the dominant quality lever and the learned scorer enabling 1.0-11.5x adaptive end-to-end compression from a single fixed keep ratio.

Abstract: The token-level extractive compressors widely used for general LM context are structurally inappropriate for LLM agents: across 17 (env, backbone, method) cells spanning two independent token-level method families, every cell collapses to mean reward <= 0.05 despite 1.3-13.3x realized compression. We name and characterize this failure mode as action-grammar destruction -- the tokens carrying action semantics (identifiers, brackets, action verbs) are exactly those self-information ranks lowest, so a general-purpose compressor reliably removes them and the environment rejects the residual. The diagnosis points to step-granularity compression. We introduce AGORA, an inference-free step-level compressor combining a structural prompt parser, an always-keep floor for format- and recency-critical content, and a 125M-parameter relevance scorer trained on counterfactual next-action-change labels (~2ms/step, zero per-step LLM toll). Across the compared inference-free and LLM-based methods, AGORA is the only one retaining >= 75% uncompressed performance in 8 of 9 cells (with the lone exception at 73%); a four-way component ablation isolates the structural floor as the dominant quality lever and the learned scorer as the source of 1.0-11.5x adaptive end-to-end compression from a single fixed keep ratio.

</details>


### [167] [A governance horizon for ethical-use constraints in open-weight AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24383)
*Weiwei Xu,Hengzhi Ye,Haoran Ye,Kai Gao,Vladimir Filkov,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对Hugging Face Hub上214万个模型仓库进行审计，测试基于披露的治理基础设施能否在深度模型衍生物中维持可追溯性。研究发现限制证据的半衰期为1.31个衍生步骤，超过7代下游，80%以上模型缺乏治理决策所需证据，定义了"治理视界"概念。平台级干预表明政策设计（而非仅执行）是关键因素：仅继承设计需近乎完全执行才能移动视界，而强制声明设计可在适度执行下即可生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重AI模型的伦理约束预期会向下游衍生模型传播，但这些约束是以自愿元数据披露的形式实现的，需要在每次复用时重新声明。然而，这种基于披露的治理基础设施能否在深度模型谱系中维持可追溯性，目前尚不清楚。

Method: 对2,142,823个Hugging Face Hub上的模型仓库进行审计，追踪许可元数据在模型衍生物中的传播情况，测试不同平台级干预措施的效果，并与PyPI（采用机器可读声明传递治理信号）进行比较。

Result: 限制证据的半衰期为1.31个衍生步骤（R²=0.98）；超过7代下游至少80%后继模型缺乏治理决策所需的公开证据。继承式设计需近乎完全执行才能移动治理视界，而强制声明设计可在适度执行下生效。孤儿组件（无可继承上游意图的谱系）在任何继承式策略下都无法判定。PyPI的比较验证了这种崩溃是开放权重衍生特有的拓扑问题，而非开放生态系统的固有特性。

Conclusion: 基于披露的治理在开放权重AI中的覆盖范围浅薄且由结构决定。实现深度供应链问责需要通过衍生过程本身传播治理信号的溯源机制，而非依赖继承式元数据声明。

Abstract: Ethical constraints on open-weight AI models are both a reflection of societal concerns and a foundation for AI governance policy. They are expected to propagate to downstream derivatives while implemented as voluntary metadata disclosures that must be restated at each generation of reuse. We audit 2,142,823 model repositories on Hugging Face Hub to test whether this disclosure-based governance infrastructure can sustain traceability across deep model lineages. Restriction evidence decays with a half-life of 1.31 derivation steps ($R^2$=0.98), and beyond seven downstream generations at least 80% of descendant models lack sufficient public evidence for a governance determination, a depth boundary we formalize as the governance horizon. Platform-level interventions to restore missing licence metadata reveal that policy design (not enforcement alone) is the binding factor: inheritance-only designs require near-complete enforcement to move the horizon, whereas a mandatory-declaration design that explicitly resolves orphan lineage components shifts the horizon already at moderate enforcement. The structural bottleneck is lineages with no inheritable upstream intent: such orphan components remain undecidable under any inheritance-only policy regardless of enforcement rate, and unresolved upstream nodes additionally create direct downstream undecidability bottlenecks that inheritance rules alone cannot recover. Comparison with PyPI, where governance signals are carried by explicit machine-readable declarations, corroborates that the collapse is topology-specific to open-weight derivation rather than inherent to open ecosystems. These results establish that disclosure-based governance has a shallow, structurally determined reach in open-weight AI, and that achieving deep supply-chain accountability requires provenance mechanisms propagating governance signals through derivation itself.

</details>


### [168] [Tail-Aware HiFloat4: W4A4 Post-Training Quantization for Wan2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26628)
*Zhanfeng Feng,Shuai Guo,Xin Di,Long Peng,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Aware HiFloat4方法，通过适配ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程至Wan2.2框架，采用W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化主线性层、保持边界模块高精度、引入激活尾部分位数校准模块等策略，解决了低比特文本到视频生成中的量化挑战，在保持运行时流程不变的情况下有效降低了稀有校准异常值的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战，需要在保持生成质量的同时降低计算复杂度，同时减少稀有校准异常值的影响。

Method: 采用HiFloat4数值格式，对Wan2.2 transformer模块中的主线性层进行W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化；对数值敏感的边界模块保持高精度；引入激活尾部分位数校准模块进行通道掩码构建；结合紧凑PTQ状态恢复。

Result: 成功将ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程适配到Wan2.2框架，在HiFloat4格式下实现了主线性层的低比特量化，有效控制了稀有校准异常值的干扰。

Conclusion: 通过激活尾部分位数校准和PTQ状态恢复，在保持运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变的前提下，有效降低了稀有校准异常值的影响。

Abstract: This report describes Tail-Aware HiFloat4, our submission to the low-bit text-to-video generation quantization challenge. Our method adapts the public ViDiT-Q post-training quantization pipeline to Wan2.2 under the HiFloat4 numerical format. We quantize the main linear layers in both Wan2.2 transformer modules with W4A4 HiFloat4 fake quantization, keep numerically sensitive boundary modules in high precision, and introduce an activation-tail-aware percentile calibration module for channel-mask construction. Together with compact PTQ-state restoration, this design reduces the influence of rare calibration outliers while keeping the runtime HiFloat4 arithmetic and sampling pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [169] [Completion vs Optimality: Policy Gradient in Long-Horizon Cumulative-Damage Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26657)
*Wolfgang Maass,Sabine Janzen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文识别了policy-gradient方法在cumulative damage long-horizon决策问题上的两个正交失败模式(completion和optimality)，提出分解框架，验证了PPO在此类问题上的局限性及其四个可检验预测，为理解和改进此类问题提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage将局部吸引动作与全局不利结果耦合，传统policy-gradient方法在此类问题上失败，需要识别其正交失败模式。

Method: 将问题分解为completion(到达terminal horizon而非通过隐式terminal约束退出)和optimality(给定completion条件下匹配动态规划基准)两个正交失败模式；采用PPO结合线性soft penalty；在两个不同领域、horizon、活动集和标定数据的环境中评估：49步bricklayer职业和20赛季NBA power-forward职业。

Result: 仅授予horizon访问权限会降低completion率(penalty均衡使dominant-activity share趋向零)；action-space限制结合horizon访问可实现completion但留下optimality gap(ΔM_final=0.271)，该gap可追溯至damage origin处的first-phase贪婪承诺；四个可检验预测在bricklayer和NBA两个环境中均得到定性复现；horizon不变性预测在H=15处例外，与H*边界(H*∈[6,14])一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，PPO与线性软惩罚相结合时，horizon访问会降低completion率；结合action-space限制可实现completion但存在最优性差距(ΔM_final=0.271)，该差距源于damage origin处的first-phase贪婪承诺。四个可检验预测在两个标定环境中均得到定性复现，horizon不变性预测在四个测试horizon中通过三个。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage couple locally attractive actions to globally adverse outcomes. We identify two orthogonal failure modes for policy-gradient methods on this class and propose a decomposition that separates them: \emph{completion} (reaching the terminal horizon rather than exiting via an implicit terminal constraint) and \emph{optimality} (matching the dynamic-programming reference given completion). Under PPO with a linear soft penalty, granting horizon access alone reduces the completion rate: the penalty's equilibrium drives the dominant-activity share to zero, while action-space restriction combined with horizon access achieves completion but leaves an optimality gap ($ΔM_{\text{final}} = 0.271$) that we trace to first-phase greedy commitment at the damage origin. We derive four testable predictions and evaluate them in two separately calibrated environments that share the same abstract structure but differ in domain, horizon, activity set, and calibration data: a 49-step bricklayer career and a 20-season NBA power-forward career. All four predictions replicate qualitatively. The horizon-invariance prediction is met at three of four tested horizons, with the exception at $H = 15$ consistent with the $H^*$ boundary ($H^* \in [6, 14]$ under the NBA parameters).

</details>


### [170] [MemFail: Stress-Testing Failure Modes of LLM Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26667)
*Ishir Garg,Neel Kolhe,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MemFail，一个诊断基准测试，用于隔离现代LLM记忆系统的故障模式。研究团队将记忆系统形式化为总结、存储和检索三个操作的组合，针对每个操作识别潜在故障模式，并构建了五个对抗性设计的数据集来测试特定操作。通过评估四种最先进的记忆系统，证明了MemFail能够实证揭示不同架构间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越依赖外部记忆系统来保持跨长时间交互的一致性，但现有研究缺乏对记忆系统具体故障模式和设计选择的实证理解。现有的基准测试仅报告聚合的问答准确率，并将记忆系统视为黑箱，无法将错误答案归因于系统的特定故障模式。

Method: 首先将记忆系统形式化为三个规范操作的组合（总结、存储、检索），识别每个操作可能诱导的故障模式；然后基于这些假设的故障模式构建了跨越四个任务的五个数据集，每个数据集都经过对抗性设计以测试记忆系统的特定操作；最后使用这些数据集对四种最先进的记忆系统进行评估。

Result: MemFail能够隔离现代LLM记忆系统的故障模式，并可用于实证理解不同记忆系统架构之间产生的权衡。

Conclusion: MemFail提供了一个可诊断的基准测试，能够隔离现代LLM记忆系统的故障模式，并为理解和改进记忆系统架构设计提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory systems to remain consistent across long-horizon interactions, but little empirical work has been done to understand the specific failure modes and design choices that these systems present. Existing benchmarks report aggregate question-answering accuracy and treat memory systems as black boxes, making it impossible to attribute an incorrect answer to a particular failure mode of the system. We introduce MemFail, a diagnostic benchmark that isolates the failure modes of modern LLM memory systems. We begin by formalizing memory systems as the composition of three canonical operations -- summarization, storage, and retrieval -- and identify the potential failure modes induced by each. Based on these hypothesized failure modes, we construct five datasets spanning four tasks, each adversarially designed to test a specific operation of a memory system. Using these datasets, we evaluate four state-of-the-art memory systems on MemFail and demonstrate how MemFail can be used to empirically understand the tradeoffs induced by differences in memory system architectures.

</details>


### [171] [Neuro-Symbolic Verification of LLM Outputs for Data-Sensitive Domains (extended preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26942)
*Paul Sigloch,Christoph Benzmüller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号混合验证架构，通过形式符号方法与神经语义分析的互补结合，为LLM生成内容提供可靠性保障。该架构采用逻辑推理进行输入验证、嵌入相似度进行输出验证，并在真实医学设备评估系统中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域部署的LLM面临根本性可靠性挑战：幻觉、不一致性和隐私漏洞引入不可接受的风险，错误可能导致法律、财务或安全后果。

Method: 提出一种混合验证架构，结合形式符号方法与神经语义分析。具体包括：利用逻辑推理进行输入验证（通过完备性属性对结构化需求提供可判定保证）；使用基于嵌入的语义相似度进行输出验证（检测形式方法缺乏表达力的上下文幻觉）。通过并行、基于Actor的管道实现该分离，解决基于提示的自验证方法的局限性。

Result: 基于HAIMEDA医学设备损坏评估报告系统验证，结果显示：结构化实体幻觉检测率达83%以上，语义捏造检测率达72%，报告创建时间减少30%。

Conclusion: 神经符号架构可以为数据敏感领域的LLM部署提供原则性保障。

Abstract: LLMs deployed in high-stakes domains face fundamental reliability challenges: hallucinations, inconsistencies, and privacy vulnerabilities introduce unacceptable risks where errors carry legal, financial, or safety consequences. This paper presents a hybrid verification architecture combining formal symbolic methods with neural semantic analysis to provide complementary guarantees for LLM-generated content. This architecture employs logical reasoning for input verification, leveraging completeness properties to provide decidable guarantees on structured requirements. For output validation, embedding-based semantic similarity detects contextual hallucinations where formal methods lack expressiveness. This separation is realized in a parallel, actor-based pipeline, addressing limitations of prompt-based self-verification approaches, which inherit the distributional biases that produce hallucinations. The proposed architecture and type-aware verification method are validated with HAIMEDA, a real-world medical device damage assessment reporting system developed through Action Design Research. Evaluation shows hallucination detection rates of over 83% for structured entities and 72% for semantic fabrications, with a 30% reduction in report creation time, demonstrating that neuro-symbolic architectures can provide principled safeguards for LLM deployment in data-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [172] [Mind the Tool Failures: Achieving Synergistic Tool Gains for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26691)
*Yunhui Gan,Tan Pan,Kaiyu Guo,Limei Han,Weimiao Yu,Guangnan Ye,Chen Jiang,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对医疗AI智能体中工具可靠性问题，提出一种基于GRPO强化学习的实例级工具协同选择框架，通过概率风险最小化和分歧感知学习实现对错误工具共识的修正，从而提升医疗决策的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI工具调用方法假设工具在其预期范围内均可靠运行，但在真实临床场景中，即使相关工具也可能在困难实例上失败，导致下游决策不安全。

Method: 提出基于GRPO的强化学习框架，融合概率风险最小化奖励与分歧感知协同学习，并采用熵引导采样策略增强高分歧实例的学习信号，两组件互补以缓解实例级异质性。

Result: 在2个任务和7个医疗基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比多种基线方法取得了一致且稳定的改进。

Conclusion: 实例级协同感知工具调用对构建可靠医疗智能体系统具有重要意义，该方法在多种基准测试上实现了稳健的性能提升。

Abstract: Medical AI agents increasingly use external tools for diagnosis, treatment recommendation, and evidence retrieval, yet most existing approaches assume that task-appropriate tools are reliable within their intended scope. This assumption is fragile in real clinical settings, where even relevant tools may fail on challenging instances and lead to unsafe downstream decisions. To address this issue, we study medical tool use under imperfect-tool settings to correct failure instances missed by individual tools. Instance-dependent failure patterns create a gap between the best fixed single tool and an ideal instance-wise selector, which we refer to as the Single-Oracle risk gap. The core challenge is that conventional task-level tool selection cannot realize this gap, as it is inherently bounded by the performance of the best single tool. Motivated by this observation, we therefore account for instance-level heterogeneity and formulate tool use as an instance-level selection problem. Particularly, we propose a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework with rewards for probabilistic risk minimization and disagreement-aware synergy learning, which promotes instance-level correction of erroneous tool consensus. Furthermore, an entropy-guided sampling strategy is adopted to upweight high-disagreement instances, which provide stronger signals for learning instance-specific tool synergy. These two components complement each other in mitigating instance-level heterogeneity and improving tool synergy. Experiments on two tasks and seven medical benchmarks show that our method consistently achieves robust and stable improvements over a broad range of baselines, highlighting the importance of synergy-aware tool use for reliable medical agentic systems.

</details>


### [173] [Towards Feedback-to-Plan Decisions for Self-Evolving LLM Agents in CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26720)
*Yee Hin Chong,Jiaming Wu,Youhui Zhang,Peng Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CUDAnalyst解决大语言模型在CUDA内核生成中反馈归因不透明的问题，通过轨迹冻结和选择性反馈注入实现可控的代际归因，揭示显式规划仅在反馈对齐时有效，有效规划源于结构化多反馈交互，且强推理模型的规划可部分迁移至弱模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs as self-evolving agents for CUDA kernel generation exhibit opaque feedback attribution; standard end-to-end ablations fail due to iterative planning amplifying early perturbations and conflating feedback effects with trajectory-dependent drift.

Method: Introduce CUDAnalyst, a unified analysis layer using trajectory freezing and selective feedback injection for controlled generation-level attribution of planning decisions to feedback components, enabling stable evaluation and coalitional-style attribution of feedback effects and interactions.

Result: Explicit planning benefits only when feedback is aligned; effective planning emerges from structured multi-feedback interactions; high-level plans from stronger reasoning models can partially transfer to weaker ones.

Conclusion: The identified feedback-to-plan structure is robust across reference backbones, representative workloads, and reference induction regimes.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong empirical gains as self-evolving agents for CUDA kernel generation, driven by feedback-conditioned planning across generations. However, how planning decisions attribute and combine heterogeneous feedback signals remains opaque. Standard end-to-end ablations fail to resolve this question, as iterative planning amplifies early perturbations and conflates feedback effects with trajectory-dependent drift.
  We introduce \texttt{CUDAnalyst}, a unified analysis layer for controlled, generation-level attribution of planning decisions to feedback components via trajectory freezing and selective feedback injection. \texttt{CUDAnalyst} enables stable generation-level evaluation and principled coalitional-style attribution of feedback effects and interactions. Our results show that explicit planning is beneficial only when feedback is aligned, that effective planning emerges from structured multi-feedback interactions, and that high-level plans from stronger reasoning models can partially transfer to weaker ones. These trends hold across reference backbones, representative workloads, and reference induction regimes, indicating that the identified feedback-to-plan structure is robust within the controlled axes studied.

</details>


### [174] [It's Not the Capability: Harness Sensitivity Is Non-Monotone Across LLM Agent Tiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26731)
*Yong-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过432次实验，系统检验了LLM agent部署中"更高能力模型需要更少结构化引导"的假设。结果表明，这一假设不成立：前沿聊天模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash）在更严格harness下性能反而下降29-38个百分点；前沿推理模型（Qwen3.5-122B）却以严格harness取得最高91.7% VTSR且延迟最低；2B参数模型（Gemma4:e2B）可匹配强开源模型稳定性。失败分类显示能力强的模型主要因格式违规失败，而低能力模型主要因错误文件失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍假设：LLM agent部署中更结构化的harness会普遍提升可靠性，且更高能力模型需要更少的结构化引导——这意味着模型能力层级与最优harness复杂度呈单调反比关系。然而，这一假设尚未得到系统性验证。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，432次运行，交叉6个模型（分4个能力层级）与3种harness条件（轻量级、平衡型、严格型），在HEAT-24（24任务合成基准，git工作区验证）上进行测试。研究引入六标签失败分类法。

Result: 实验结果从两方面反驳了单调反比关系假设：1）Gemini 2.5 Flash（前沿聊天模型）harness复杂度增加导致VTSR下降29-38个百分点——"harness复杂度悖论"；2）Qwen3.5-122B（前沿推理模型，启用扩展思考）以严格harness达到最高91.7% VTSR且延迟最低；3）Gemma4:e2B（2B模型）在所有harness下均达到91.7% VTSR，匹配强开源层级稳定性。格式违规（format_violation）是高能力模型主要失败原因，错误文件（wrong_file）是低能力模型主要失败原因。

Conclusion: 模型类型（chat vs reasoning）决定了harness敏感性，且呈非单调特征。研究提供了实用的层级感知harness选择指南，并指出由于每层级仅代表单一模型，结果应作为模型特异性观察解读。

Abstract: A prevalent assumption in LLM agent deployment holds that more structured harnesses universally improve
  reliability, and that higher-capability models need proportionally less structural guidance -- together
  implying a monotone inverse relationship between model capability tier and optimal harness complexity. We
  test this hypothesis through a controlled 432-run experiment crossing six models across four capability
  tiers with three harness conditions (light, balanced, strict) on HEAT-24, a 24-task synthetic benchmark
  with git-based workspace verification. Our results refute the monotone inverse relationship on two
  fronts. First, for the frontier chat model evaluated (Gemini 2.5 Flash), increased harness verbosity
  lowers VTSR by 29-38 percentage points -- a harness-complexity paradox. Second, for the frontier
  reasoning model evaluated (Qwen3.5-122B, extended thinking enabled), strict harness achieves the highest
  VTSR (91.7%) and the lowest latency, the opposite of the prediction. Within the constrained tier, a 2B
  model (Gemma4:e2B) matches strong-open-tier stability at 91.7% across all harnesses. Because each tier is
  represented by a single model in this study, these results should be interpreted as model-specific
  observations; harness sensitivity appears non-monotone across the models evaluated, and depends
  critically on model type (chat vs. reasoning). We introduce a six-label failure taxonomy showing that
  format_violation dominates capable-model failures while wrong_file dominates low-capability failures, and
  we derive practical tier-aware harness selection guidelines.

</details>


### [175] [A Dataset of Robot-Patient and Doctor-Patient Medical Dialogues for Spoken Language Processing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26747)
*Heriberto Cuayahuitl,Grace Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MeDial-Speech医疗语音对话数据集（111+小时），用于评估GPT-5 mini、DeepSeek-V3和Claude Sonnet 4在医疗咨询中的表现。Claude Sonnet 4达到最高准确率（71.1%/74.7%），但所有模型均存在过度自信问题，数据集已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs虽已广泛应用于通用任务，但在医疗咨询场景（文本或语音）的应用仍面临挑战，缺乏专门的医疗语音数据集阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 收集了来自机器人-患者和医生-患者对话的真实语音数据（111+小时），覆盖四种疾病场景；设计20选项句子选择基准测试三种主流LLM在医疗对话中的表现。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4性能最优，手动转录准确率71.1%，自动转录准确率74.7%；GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek-V3表现次之；所有LLMs在概率预测上均表现出高度过度自信。

Conclusion: MeDial-Speech是目前规模最大的医疗语音对话数据集，为医疗AI系统开发提供了重要基准。实验表明Claude Sonnet 4表现最优，但LLMs普遍存在过度自信问题，提示需改进模型的概率校准能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have brought huge improvements to Artificial Intelligence (AI), which can be applied to general-purpose tasks. However, their application to textual or spoken medical consultations is still an open research problem. This paper proposes MeDial-Speech, a novel speech dataset for training and evaluating Med-AIs that can carry out consultations with patients. It was collected in realistic environments from robot-patient and doctor-patient dialogues, contains 111+ hours of speech data (without data augmentation), and covers four health conditions: Lewy body dementia, heart failure, shoulder pain, and angina. In addition, we propose a dialogue benchmark via sentence selection (with 20 options) to evaluate three state-of-the-art LLMs: GPT-5 mini, DeepSeek-V3, and Claude Sonnet 4. Experimental results reveal that Claude Sonnet 4 is the best in sentence selection, with 71.1% accuracy using manual transcriptions and 74.7% using automatic transcriptions, and that all LLMs are highly overconfident in their probabilistic predictions, regardless of selecting correct or incorrect sentences in medical dialogues. This dataset is free of charge for non-commercial purposes at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/hcuayahu/MeDial-Speech

</details>


### [176] [Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26772)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Dominik Meier,Jiachen Zhao,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型推理模型（LRMs）中拒绝行为的机制，发现拒绝是由残差流激活和思维链（CoT）共同编码的，这使得LRMs对单一激活干预具有更强的鲁棒性，但也暴露了CoT作为潜在攻击面的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过生成思维链（CoT）后再输出最终结果，这一过程引入了动态内部状态，使得拒绝机制的控制变得复杂。与传统指令微调LLM不同，LRM的拒绝行为不仅取决于激活空间，还依赖于CoT内容，理解这一机制对于控制模型行为至关重要。

Method: 以DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B为实验对象，采用激活 steering 技术进行三种干预实验：（1）固定CoT条件下的激活转向；（2）完全移除CoT；（3）两阶段干预——在激活转向条件下让模型重新生成CoT。

Result: 激活转向在固定CoT时仅39%概率逆转拒绝；移除CoT后升至70%；两阶段干预（重生成CoT+激活转向）达到94%逆转率，且重生成的CoT在移除激活转向后仍保留48%的效果。

Conclusion: LRM的拒绝行为由残差流激活和CoT共同编码，形成双重保障机制。这使得仅针对激活层面的干预效果有限，但同时意味着CoT本身可成为替代攻击面，存在安全隐患。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>


### [177] [The Attribution Blind Spot: Detecting When Language Models Rely on Memory Rather Than Retrieved Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26778)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Bo Yang,Chen Ye,Gaolei Li,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了"归因盲点"问题：当检索文档与模型预训练数据重叠时，无法区分输出是来自检索上下文还是参数记忆；为此提出计算现实监控（CRM）方法，通过比较有无上下文条件下的内部表征差异来检测预训练暴露留下的可测量轨迹特征，为证据溯源的系统奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Retrieval-augmented generation lacks reliable methods to verify whether retrieved context actually governs generation; the standard assumption that context-consistent output implies context-governed output breaks when retrieved documents overlap with pretraining data, as models can produce faithful-looking text entirely from parametric memory.

Method: CRM operationalizes cognitive science's reality monitoring framework by comparing internal representations with and without context to detect membership-conditioned representational divergence; across nine model variants spanning three families, the divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns with converging support from block-level noise intervention.

Result: Attribution blind spot is measurable through internal representational divergence that output-level monitors systematically miss; divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns with support from noise intervention experiments; findings generalize across tasks and datasets while collapsing on domain-confounded benchmarks.

Conclusion: CRM establishes that internal representations carry a diagnostic signal invisible at the output level, providing a foundation for systems whose internal awareness of evidence provenance governs their external behavior; the attribution blind spot is measurable and partially addressable.

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation promises to ground language model outputs in external evidence, yet the field has no reliable way to verify whether retrieved context actually governs generation -- a prerequisite for any high-stakes deployment. The standard assumption, that context-consistent output implies context-governed output, breaks when the retrieved document overlaps with the model's pretraining data: the model can produce faithful-looking text entirely from parametric memory, and both pathways yield indistinguishable output. We name this failure the attribution blind spot and introduce Computational Reality Monitoring (CRM) to address it. CRM operationalizes a principle adapted from cognitive science's reality monitoring framework: comparing internal representations with and without context reveals membership-conditioned representational divergence that output-level monitors systematically miss. CRM does not certify which source an individual generation used; it detects whether pretraining exposure leaves a measurable internal trajectory signature, establishing a necessary substrate for source attribution. Across nine model variants spanning three families, this divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns, receives converging support from block-level noise intervention, and generalizes across tasks and datasets while collapsing on domain-confounded benchmarks. The attribution blind spot is measurable and partially addressable: internal representations carry a diagnostic signal invisible at the output level, establishing a foundation for systems whose internal awareness of evidence provenance governs their external behavior.

</details>


### [178] [LiveK12Bench: Have Large Multimodal Models Truly Conquered High School-level Examinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26781)
*Xiaohan Wang,Mingze Yin,Yilin Zhao,Gang Liu,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveK12Bench是一个动态、综合性K-12多学科基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在真实考试场景中的推理能力，包含2000+道题目，覆盖数学、物理、化学、生物科目，通过自动化管道持续更新数据，并提出"模拟考试"评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM教育基准测试存在三大局限：静态数据集容易造成数据污染、模态和学科覆盖受限、评估标准单一。GPT-5等先进模型在理想评测条件下表现优异，但缺乏在真实考试环境下的系统评估，无法准确衡量其作为智能导师的教育准备度。

Method: 1) 构建LiveK12Bench基准：2000+道验证题目，涵盖数学、物理、化学、生物，来源于最新真实试卷，支持持续更新；2) 开发自动化管道：持续摄入和解析最新试卷以缓解数据泄漏问题；3) 提出"模拟考试"评估方案：评估模型端到端自主完成考试的能力，同时考察推理准确性和效率。

Result: 12个LMMs的实验表明，先进模型在考试现实约束下性能显著下降：GPT-5综合评分从79降至53（满分100）。研究发现模型对复杂视觉布局敏感，揭示了理想化推理能力与真实教育准备度之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: LiveK12Bench有效暴露了当前LMMs的关键弱点，证明即使是最先进的模型在真实考试场景中仍面临严峻挑战。该基准为评估模型教育实用性提供了更严格的测试标准，代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Advanced Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in K-12 reasoning tasks, exhibiting great promise as intelligent tutors. Realizing this potential requires models to navigate real-world examinations effectively, yet most existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of authentic testing environments. Specifically, most datasets are static, prone to data contamination, and are often confined to restricted modalities, disciplines, and evaluation criteria. To address these issues, we introduce LiveK12Bench, a dynamic, holistic, multi-disciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate the reasoning abilities of LMMs in realistic examination scenarios. LiveK12Bench comprises 2K+ verified questions spanning Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, and Biology, sourced from the latest real-world exam papers and designed to grow over time. Our framework features several core innovations: 1) featuring an automated pipeline that continuously ingests and parses the latest examination papers to mitigate data leakage; and 2) proposing a novel `Mock Exam' evaluation scheme, which assesses the ability to complete end-to-end exams autonomously with accurate and efficient reasoning paths. Extensive experiments on 12 LMMs reveal that advanced models suffer substantial performance degradation under exam-realistic constraints: GPT-5's score drops from 79 to 53 (out of 100) when process rigor and efficiency are jointly evaluated. Our findings expose critical vulnerabilities, such as sensitivity to complex visual layouts, highlighting the gap between idealized reasoning capabilities and true educational readiness. Both code and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [179] [Composition Collapse: Stable Factual Knowledge Does Not Imply Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26789)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Jie Chen,Hongzhi Wang,Xuyang Teng,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了后训练评估中聚合指标的系统性盲点：相同原子知识储备的模型可能表现出超过40个百分点的组合能力差异（组合崩溃），现有基准测试无法检测这一现象。研究提出双门控协议，将评估分解为原子稳定性、残余组合能力和临界深度三个维度，为多跳推理能力评估提供了更精确的测量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练评估依赖聚合基准分数，将多跳推理视为单一能力，掩盖了真实能力差异。统计上不可区分的原子知识配方可能导致超过40个百分点的组合行为差异，这种"组合崩溃"现象对聚合指标不可见。

Method: 引入双门控协议（double-gate protocol），将后训练收益分解为三个独立通道：原子稳定性、残余组合能力、临界深度，在包含时间事实链（深度2-11）的基准测试上评估四种后训练配方。

Result: 分解揭示后训练目标以聚合指标掩盖的方式改变组合能力；诊断探针表明相当比例的组合失败源于生成时的计算约束而非永久性组合不能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了后训练评估中聚合指标的局限性，提出了双门控协议来分解后训练增益，建议多跳推理能力评估应配合原子门控控制的组合指标。

Abstract: Post-training is routinely evaluated through aggregate benchmark scores that treat multi-hop reasoning as a single capability -- as if a model that answers more questions correctly must be better at assembling facts. We show that this assumption can be misleading: recipes with statistically indistinguishable atomic knowledge produce composition behaviour separated by over 40 percentage points, a phenomenon we call composition collapse: the systematic failure to assemble stably-known facts into chains, invisible to aggregate metrics. We introduce a double-gate protocol that changes the estimand from an aggregate compositionality gap to residual composition failure conditioned on stable atomic access, decomposing post-training gains into three independent channels: atomic stability, residual composition, and critical depth. On a benchmark of temporal factual chains spanning depths 2--11 across four post-training recipes, this decomposition reveals that post-training objectives shift composition capability in directions that aggregate metrics mask, and suggests that claims about multi-hop reasoning improvement should be accompanied by atomic-gate-controlled composition metrics. Diagnostic probes further show that a substantial share of measured composition failure reflects generation-time computation constraints rather than permanent inability to compose.

</details>


### [180] [What Makes Chain-of-Thought Work at Probe Time? Local Co-occurrence Rather Than Global Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795)
*Xiang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了思维链(CoT)提示之所以有效，主要并非依靠逻辑推导或答案复制，而是依靠词汇激活和局部token共现模式。通过词序打乱和不同粒度的token窗口实验，证明只需保留2-3个token的连续窗口就能恢复大部分CoT增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting能可靠提升语言模型准确率，但其背后的机制尚不明确。前人研究主要关注生成时行为，该论文转向探测时问题：给定固定的上下文rationale，其中什么成分改变了答案？

Method: 通过系统性消融实验：全局词序打乱测试词汇激活效应；不同窗口大小(n*=2-3)的连续token保留测试；跨模型家族、参数规模和数据集的泛化实验；排除显式答案声明复制和完整语法实现作为主要驱动因素。

Result: 1) 即使全局词序打乱仍显著优于无rationale基线，说明存在强词汇激活效应；2) 结构化文本的额外增益主要来自短程token邻接(2-3个token窗口)，而非句子级逻辑排序；3) 显式答案声明复制和完整语法实现并非主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 提出局部共现激活(LCA)理论解释CoT效果：主要增益来源于词汇激活和短程token共现，而非句子级逻辑推导。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting reliably improves language-model accuracy, but which properties of a rationale text drive the improvement is poorly understood. Prior work has largely studied generation-time behavior. We instead ask a probe-time question: given a fixed rationale in context, what in that text changes the answer? We identify two complementary sources of the gain. First, even a globally word-shuffled rationale substantially outperforms the no-rationale baseline, indicating a strong lexical activation effect. More importantly, the additional gain from structured text appears to arise less from sentence-level logical ordering and more from short-range token adjacency. Preserving contiguous windows of just $n^\star{=}2$--$3$ tokens recovers most of the remaining gain toward full CoT performance. Supporting experiments rule out copying of explicit answer declarations or answer values, as well as full grammatical realization, as primary drivers. Further generalization experiments show that the qualitative pattern remains stable across multiple model families, parameter scales, and datasets. These results support a local co-occurrence activation (LCA) account of probe-time CoT, in which the observed gains appear to arise primarily from lexical activation and short-range token co-occurrence rather than sentence-level logical derivation.

</details>


### [181] [Multi-Stakeholder LLM Alignment: Decomposing Estimation from Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26878)
*Lulu Zheng,Wenjin Yang,Xiangwen Zhang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Zheng Pan,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多利益方任务中LLM评判器的权重噪声问题提出DecompR方法，通过因果解耦权重校准与效用估算，实现更稳定公平的多方偏好聚合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有整体式LLM评判器将效用估算与效用聚合混为一谈，导致权重噪声。当利益方满意度分散时，这种噪声会产生大幅分数偏移，且随利益方数量增加而加剧。

Method: 将LLM判断分解为两个独立阶段：1）从查询结构中提取反事实校准的固定权重；2）对每个角色独立估算效用值，从而消除候选依赖性权重漂移并降低估算噪声。

Result: 实验验证了该权重诱导偏移在大分散度场景下确实存在，且随利益方数量增长而增加。

Conclusion: DecompR通过将权重校准与候选评分解耦，解决了多利益方场景下的权重噪声问题，实现了更稳定的多方偏好聚合评估。

Abstract: Multi-stakeholder tasks require one output to satisfy users with conflicting preferences. Holistic LLM judges conflate utility estimation and utility aggregation, yielding unstable implicit weights. We show empirically and theoretically that this aggregation-specific \emph{weighting noise} can create large score shifts when stakeholder satisfaction is dispersed; in our experiments, these weight-induced shifts also increase with stakeholder count. We propose \textsc{DecompR}: counterfactual-calibrated weights are fixed from query structure before candidate scoring, while per-role utilities are estimated independently, removing candidate-dependent weight drift and reducing estimation noise.

</details>


### [182] [TADDLE: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Detecting Deficient LLM-Generated Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26911)
*Hanqi Duan,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper introduces TADDLE, the first system designed to detect specific deficiency types in LLM-generated peer reviews. It includes a benchmark of 1,800 expert-annotated reviews on 50 ICLR 2025 papers, using a six-category defect taxonomy. The system uses a multi-tool agent architecture with semi-supervised learning to achieve strong detection performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-generated peer reviews are becoming increasingly prevalent at major venues, but their deficiencies are difficult to detect because they appear uniformly fluent and well-structured. Existing approaches either classify authorship without assessing quality, or evaluate quality using features designed for human-written reviews, creating a critical gap in the ability to identify specific defect types in LLM-generated reviews.

Method: TADDLE employs a tool-augmented agent architecture with four specialized analysis tools (Verify, Correct, Complete, and Transform) orchestrated by an agent. An integrator synthesizes tool outputs into classifications via two-stage semi-supervised learning. The system was evaluated on a benchmark of 1,800 reviews across 50 ICLR 2025 papers, annotated by 18 domain experts using a taxonomy of six defect categories.

Result: TADDLE demonstrates strong performance on both binary detection (identifying whether a review is deficient) and multi-label classification (identifying specific deficiency types) tasks.

Conclusion: TADDLE represents the first system capable of detecting specific deficiency types in LLM-generated peer reviews at the individual defect level. The system demonstrates strong performance on both binary and multi-label classification tasks, with the released benchmark enabling further research in this important emerging area.

Abstract: LLM-generated peer reviews are increasingly common at major venues, yet their deficiencies are hard to detect because they are uniformly fluent and well-structured. Existing work either classifies authorship without judging quality, or scores quality with features designed for human-written reviews; no prior system detects deficiencies in LLM-generated reviews at the level of individual defect types. To bridge the gap, we introduce TADDLE, a Tool-Augmented Agent for Detecting Deficient LLM-Generated Peer Reviews, together with the first expert-annotated benchmark for this task. Our benchmark comprises 1,800 reviews on 50 ICLR 2025 papers, multi-label-annotated by 18 domain experts against a taxonomy of six defect categories (plus a non-deficient label). TADDLE decomposes detection into four specialized analysis tools -- Verify, Correct, Complete, and Transform -- orchestrated by an agent; an integrator synthesizes their outputs into binary and multi-label classifications via two-stage semi-supervised learning. Extensive experiments show that TADDLE performs strongly on both binary detection and the multi-label classification task. We release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/AquariusAQ/TADDLE.

</details>


### [183] [Developing a Totally Unimodular Linear Program for Optimal Conformance Checking: When and Why It Complements A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26938)
*Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper reformulates alignment-based conformance checking as a totally unimodular linear program, exploiting network-flow properties to guarantee polynomial-time solutions without integer programming. A hybrid approach combining both methods achieves 38.6% runtime savings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The standard A*-based heuristic search for alignment-based conformance checking suffers from exponential runtime when dealing with long traces or substantial deviations, limiting its practicality for large-scale process mining applications.

Method: The paper reformulates alignment-based conformance checking as a totally unimodular linear program (LP) on the reachability graph of the synchronous product. This exploits the network-flow structure to guarantee integral optimal extreme-point solutions through LP relaxation, eliminating the need for integer variables and branch-and-bound search.

Result: Extensive empirical evaluation on over 2.1 million instances shows A* and LP have complementary performance: A* excels on short, well-conforming traces, while LP provides substantial speedups for longer traces with deviations.

Conclusion: The proposed LP-based approach demonstrates substantial speedups for longer traces with deviations, and when combined with A* through simple algorithm-selection guidelines, achieves 38.6% average runtime savings with 96% selection accuracy.

Abstract: Alignment-based conformance checking is the state-of-the-art approach for comparing observed process executions with normative process models. The standard exact solution relies on an A*-based heuristic search, which can exhibit exponential runtime in the presence of long traces or substantial deviations.
  This paper introduces a reformulation of alignment-based conformance checking as a totally unimodular linear program (LP) defined on the reachability graph of the synchronous product. By exploiting the underlying network-flow structure, the proposed formulation guarantees the existence of an integral optimal extreme-point solution through LP relaxation, thereby avoiding the combinatorial overhead associated with integer variables and branch-and-bound search.
  We conduct an extensive empirical evaluation on more than 2.1 million conformance checking instances derived from real-world and synthetic benchmark datasets. The results show that A* and the LP approach exhibit complementary performance characteristics: the former performs best on short, well-conforming traces, while the LP formulation provides substantial speedups for longer traces with deviations, precisely where conformance checking is most informative. Based on these findings, we derive simple algorithm-selection guidelines that combine both approaches, achieving average runtime savings of 38.6% with 96% selection accuracy compared to always using A*.

</details>


### [184] [LELA: An End-to-end LLM-based Entity Linking Framework with Zero-shot Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26956)
*Samy Haffoudhi,Nikola Dobričić,Fabian Suchanek,Nils Holzenberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将LELA实体消歧方法扩展为Python库，集成零样本NER，提供端到端实体链接管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体链接方法通常绑定于特定知识库和领域，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将模块化领域无关的LLM实体消歧方法LELA扩展为Python库，集成零样本命名实体识别，实现端到端实体链接流程。

Result: 实验验证了LELA在不同实体链接场景下的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LELA扩展为Python库，提供端到端实体链接管道，可在用户自定义输入文本上运行演示。

Abstract: Entity linking is a key component of many downstream NLP systems, yet existing approaches are often tied to the specific target knowledge bases and domains, limiting their real world application. In this paper, we extend LELA, a modular and domain-agnostic LLM-based entity disambiguation method, into a practical Python library that integrates zero-shot Named Entity Recognition (NER) -thereby providing a complete end-toend pipeline for entity-linking in real-world usage. We provide experimental results validating LELA's performance and robustness across diverse entity linking settings. In our demo, users can play with the system on their own input texts.

</details>


### [185] [Generating Robust Portfolios of Optimization Models using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27013)
*Eleni Straitouri,Cheol Woo Kim,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mathematical optimization is a powerful tool for structured decision-making across domains such as resource allocation and planning. Formulating optimization models faithful to reality, though, remains a significant bottleneck as it typically demands both domain expertise and optimization knowledge that are often scarce. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) promise to bridge this gap, enabling the generation of candidate optimization models from natural language descriptions. However, there is no guarantee that any single LLM-generated model is reliable, and existing approaches that output only one model are therefore risky. In this work, we propose a novel algorithm that generates a portfolio of optimization models, designed to be robust to the limitations of LLMs. Our method exploits the observation that a single LLM can play two distinct roles $\unicode{x2014}$ as a stochastic generator and as a reasoning evaluator $\unicode{x2014}$ and proposes a unified framework that leverages both capabilities in a complementary manner. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that, as long as either the generator or the evaluator is well-aligned with human preferences, the portfolio is guaranteed to contain high-quality candidates, enabling a principled human-in-the-loop process in which a decision-maker can review multiple candidates before committing to one. We further validate our approach empirically, demonstrating strong performance across a range of optimization modeling tasks.

</details>


### [186] [Boosting Knowledge Graph Foundation Models via Enhanced Negative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27023)
*Yinan Liu,Wenjin Xu,Zhiyuan Zha,Xiaochun Yang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KMAS，一种自适应负采样方法来增强知识图谱基础模型（KGFMs）。KMAS通过关系编码器生成硬负三元组，并动态调整其在训练过程中的比例（预热→线性增加→线性减少），以适应模型能力的演进。在44个数据集上的实验验证了该方法能有效提升多种SOTA KGFMs的性能，且开销较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱基础模型采用随机负采样构建训练数据，但这些负三元组质量有限，无法为模型训练提供充分的监督信号。

Method: KMAS通过现有KGFM的关系编码器生成更新的关系嵌入，以此构建硬负三元组；训练过程中采用动态调整策略：先进行预热，随后线性增加硬负样本比例，再线性减少，形成自适应的负采样方法。

Result: 在44个数据集上的实验表明，该负采样方法能有效增强多种SOTA KGFMs的性能，且未带来过多的时间和内存消耗。

Conclusion: KMAS能有效提升多种SOTA知识图谱基础模型的性能，且无需额外的显著时间和内存开销。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) have become the core backbone of numerous downstream tasks such as question answering and recommender systems. However, despite all this, KGs are often very incomplete. To perform zero-shot knowledge graph completion in unseen KGs, which have different relational vocabularies from those used for pre-training, KG foundation models (KGFMs) receive a wide range of attention. Existing KGFMs often perform training using random negative triples, which are constructed by replacing the head or tail entity of a positive triple with a random entity. However, these negative triples are often constructed with limited quality, providing weak supervision for KGFM training. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective adaptive negative sampling approach, KMAS, to enhance existing KGFMs. KMAS constructs hard negative triples through the updated relation embeddings generated from the existing KGFM's relation encoder. To further adaptively align with the evolving capability of the KGFM during the training process, KMAS adjusts the ratio of hard negative triples dynamically throughout the whole training process: after a warmup phrase, it increases the ratio linearly and then decreases linearly. Extensive experiments are conducted over 44 data sets. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed negative sampling method can enhance many SOTA KGFMs without requiring excessive additional time or memory consumption.

</details>


### [187] [BatteryMFormer: Multi-level Learning for Battery Degradation Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27044)
*Ruifeng Tan,Jintao Dong,Weixiang Hong,Jia Li,Jiaqiang Huang,Tong-Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BatteryMFormer，一种用于早期电池降解轨迹预测的多层次Transformer模型。该模型通过整合老化条件感知解码器、元降解模式记忆和双视图编码器三个组件，捕获老化条件先验知识、轨迹原型模式以及SOC局部变化特征，在四个电池数据集上均实现了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池降解数据存在两个关键特征未被现有方法充分建模：(1)多层次结构特性——包括老化条件内的规律性和跨电池的轨迹模式；(2)降解相关变化通常局限于特定SOC区间。当前方法未能显式建模这些特征，限制了早期预测的准确性。

Method: BatteryMFormer包含三个核心组件：(1)老化条件感知解码器，通过老化条件感知查询和老龄化条件感知注意力注入老化条件先验；(2)元降解模式记忆模块，学习并检索轨迹原型以指导长时域预测；(3)双视图编码器，从电压和电流时间序列中联合捕获时序动态和SOC局部变化特征。

Result: 在四个电池领域的大规模实验中，BatteryMFormer一致性地优于现有先进基线方法，验证了其对多层次时序建模和SOC局部变化捕获能力的有效性。

Conclusion: BatteryMFormer在四个电池领域的一致性最优性能表明其向可靠电池降解轨迹预测迈出了重要一步。代码已开源。

Abstract: Early battery degradation trajectory forecasting (BDTF), which predicts the full-life state-of-health trajectory from early operational data, is critical for battery optimization, manufacturing, and deployment. Battery degradation data exhibit two key characteristics. First, degradation data present a multi-level structure, including regularities shared within aging conditions and trajectory patterns shared across batteries. Second, degradation-related variations in voltage-current profiles are often localized to specific state-of-charge (SOC) intervals. Existing approaches often fail to explicitly model these characteristics. To bridge this gap, we propose BatteryMFormer, a multi-level Transformer for early BDTF. BatteryMFormer integrates (1) an aging-condition-aware decoder that injects aging-condition priors via aging-condition-informed queries and aging-condition-aware attention, (2) a meta degradation pattern memory that learns and retrieves trajectory prototypes to guide long-horizon forecasting, and (3) a dual-view encoder that jointly captures temporal dynamics and SOC-localized variations from voltage and current time series. Extensive experiments on four battery domains show that BatteryMFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, marking a significant step toward reliable BDTF. Our code is available at https://github.com/Ruifeng-Tan/BatteryMFormer.
